




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saddanītippakaraṇaṃ

dhātumālā

15. saravaggapañ‍cakantika suddhassaradhātu

itau  paraṃ tu saratau, kakārantādibhaidatau|

dhātuyau dhātunipphan‍na-rūpāni vividhāni ca||

sāṭṭhakathai piṭakamhi, jinapāṭhai yathābalaṃ|

nayaṃ upaparikkhitvā, samāsaina kathaissa’haṃ||

i gatiyaṃ| yaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ gatiatthau, buddhipi taisaṃ atthau| pavattipāpuṇānipi| tatra gamanaṃ duvidhaṃ kāyagamanaṃ ñāṇagamanañ‍ca| taisu kāyagamanaṃ nāma iriyāpathagamanaṃ, ñāṇagamanaṃ nāma ñāṇuppatti, tasmā payaugānurūpaina ‘‘gacchatī’’ti padassa ‘‘jānātī’’tipi atthau bhavati, ‘‘pavattatī’’tipi atthau bhavati, ‘‘pāpuṇātī’’tipi atthau bhavati, iriyāpathagamanaina gacchatītipi atthau bhavati, ñāṇagamanaina gacchatītipi atthau bhavati| tathā hi ‘‘sīghaṃ gacchatī’’tiādīsu iriyāpathagamanaṃ ‘‘gamana’’nti vuc‍cati| sundaraṃ nibbānaṃ gatau| ‘‘gatimā’’tiādīsu  pana ñāṇagamanaṃ| aivaṃ sabbaisampi gatyatthānaṃ dhātūnaṃ yathāpayaugaṃ atthau gahaitabbau|

tassimāni rūpāni bhavanti – iti, aiti, udaiti| kāritai ‘‘udāyatī’’ti rūpaṃ bhavati| uṭṭhāpaitīti hi atthau, dukārau āgamau| upaiti, samupaiti, vaiti, apaiti, avaiti, anvaiti, samaiti, abhisamaiti, samayau, abhisamayau, īdi, udi, aikaudi, paṇḍitau, itau, uditau, upaitau, samupaitau, anvitau, apaitau, samaitau, aitabbau, pac‍caitabbau, paṭiyamānau, paṭic‍cau, aintau, adhippaitau, adhippāyau, pac‍cayau, añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni| ‘‘itā, ita’’ntiādinā yathārahaṃ itthinapuṃsakavasainapi| pac‍caituṃ, upaituṃ, samupaituṃ, anvaituṃ, samaituṃ, abhisamaituṃ, ic‍ca, paṭic‍ca, samaic‍ca, abhisamaic‍ca, apaic‍ca, upaic‍ca, paṭimukhaṃ itvā, itvāna, upaitvā, upaitvāna, upaituna, añ‍ñānipi buddhavacanānurūpatau yaujaitabbāni|

itiiti kriyāsaddau, suttantaisu na dissati|

idamaittha na vattabbaṃ, dassanāyaiva mai rutau||

‘‘itāyaṃ kaudharūpaina’’, iti pāḷi hi dissati|

aṅguttaranikāyamhi, munināhac‍ca bhāsitā|

vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā aṅguttaranikāyai kaudhaṃ nindantaina –

‘‘itāyaṃ kaudharūpaina, mac‍cuvaisau guhāsayau|

taṃ damaina samucchindai, pañ‍ñāvīriyaina diṭṭhiyā’’ti||

tatra itāyanti iti ayanti chaidau| itiiti ca gacchati pavattatīti atthau| ayaṃ panaittha suttapadatthau – yau  dausau laukai ‘‘kaudhau’’ti laukiyamahājanaina vuc‍cati, nāyaṃ atthatau kaudhauti vattabbau| kinti pana vattabbau, aisau hi sarīrasaṅkhātaguhāsayau mac‍curājā aiva kaudhavasaina pamaddantau sattasantānai gacchatīti vattabbau| taṃ aivarūpaṃ ‘‘mac‍curājā’’ti vattabbaṃ bahunau janassa anatthakaraṃ kaudhaṃ hitakāmau damaina pañ‍ñāya vīriyaina diṭṭhiyā ca chindaiyyāti|

aitīti imassa pana āgacchatīti atthau| ‘‘aitī’’ti aittha hi āupasaggau sandhikic‍caina paṭicchan‍nattā na pākaṭau valāhakāvattharitau puṇṇacandau viya| tathā hi aittha ā iti aitīti sandhiviggahau bhavati, ākārassa ca ikāraina paraina saddhiṃyaiva aikārādaisau| tasmā ‘‘ayaṃ sau sārathī aiti| aitu vaissantarau rājā’’tiādīsu ‘‘āgacchati, āgacchatū’’tiādinā atthau kathaitabbau| byākaraṇasatthaipi hi ā iti aitīti sandhiviggahau dissati, tasmā ayampi nīti sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbā| atha vā itīti rassavasaina vuttaṃ padaṃ gamanaṃ baudhaiti, aitīti vuddhivasaina vuttaṃ pana yathāpayaugaṃ āgamanādīni| mattāvasainapi hi padāni savisaisatthāni bhavanti| taṃ yathā? sāsanai pabbajitau, raṭṭhā pabbājitauti| sañ‍ñaugāsañ‍ñaugavasainapi, taṃ yathā? gāmā niggacchati| yasaṃ pausau nigacchati, tasmā ayampi nīti sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbā| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

i gatiyanti kathitā, dhātu vuddhiṃ gatā yadā|

tadā āgamanatthassa, vācikā pāyatau vasā||

iriyāpathatthatau hai-sā nic‍cāgamavācikā|

‘‘ayaṃ sau sārathī aiti’’, ic‍cādaittha nidassanaṃ||

aniriyāpathatthaina, vattanai gamanaipi ca|

āgamanai ca hautīti, dhīmā lakkhaiyya taṃ yathā||

‘‘paṭic‍ca phalamaitī’’ti, aivamādīsu vattanai|

vuddhippattā ikāravhā, aisā dhātu pavattati||

‘‘atthamaintamhi sūriyai, vāḷā’’ ic‍cādīsu pana|

gatai, ‘‘aitīti itī’’ti-ādisvāgamanai siyā||

tathā hi ītīti anatthāya aiti āgacchatīti īti, upaddavau, iti āgamanatthau gahaitabbau| āha ca suttanipātaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘aitīti īti, āgantukānaṃ akusalabhāgīnaṃ byasanahaitūnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacana’’nti|

idāni yathārahaṃ nipātākhyātanāmikapariyāpan‍nānaṃ itiitau saddānamatthuddhārau vuc‍catai – tattha itisaddau haituparisamāpanādipadattha vipariyāya pakārāvadhāraṇa nidassanādianaikatthappabhaidau| tathā haisa ‘‘ruppatīti khau bhikkhavai tasmā rūpanti vuc‍catī’’tiādīsu haituatthai dissati| ‘‘tasmātiha mai bhikkhavai dhammadāyādā bhavatha, mā āmisadāyādā| atthi mai tumhaisu anukampā, kinti mai sāvakā dhammadāyādā bhavaiyyuṃ, nau āmisadāyādā’’tiādīsu parisamāpanai| ‘‘iti vā iti aivarūpā visūkadassanā paṭiviratau’’tiādīsu ādiatthau| ‘‘māgaṇḍiyauti tassa brāhmaṇassa saṅkhā samañ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti vauhārau nāmaṃ nāmakammaṃ nāmadhaiyyaṃ nirutti byañ‍janamabhilāpau’’tiādīsu padatthavipariyāyai| ‘‘iti khau bhikkhavai sappaṭibhayau bālau, appaṭibhayau paṇḍitau| saupaddavau bālau, anupaddavau  paṇḍitau| saupasaggau bālau, anupasaggau paṇḍitau’’tiādīsu pakārau| ‘‘atthi idappac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti iti puṭṭhaina satā ādanda atthītissa vacanīyaṃ| kiṃ pac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti iti cai vadaiyya, jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇanti ic‍cassa vacanīya’’ntiādīsu avadhāraṇai| ‘‘atthīti khau kac‍cāna ayamaikau antau, natthīti khau kac‍cāna ayaṃ dutiyau antau’’tiādīsu nidassanai| nipātavasainaiva tai payaugā gahaitabbā| ‘‘itāyaṃ kaudharūpainā’’ti aittha pana ākhyātavasaina gamanai itisaddau dissati| ayamaivatthau idhādhippaitau, nipātatthau pana na icchitabbau, viñ‍ñūnaṃ atthaggahaṇai kausal‍lupādanatthaṃ kaivalaṃ atthuddhāravasaina āgatauti daṭṭhabbaṃ| itarau pana –

gatyatthai cimasaddatthai, itausaddau pavattati|

anvitau’’ti hi gatyatthai, pac‍cattavacanaṃ bhavai||

imasaddassa atthamhi, nissak‍kavacanaṃ bhavai|

‘‘itau sā dakkhiṇā disā’’, itiādīsu pāḷisu||

gatyatthau icchitau aittha, itaratthau na icchitau|

atthuddhāravasā vuttau, kausal‍latthāya viñ‍ñunaṃ||

idha pana samayasaddassa atthuddhāraṃ sanibbacanaṃ vattabbampi avatvā upari ayadhātuvisayaiyaiva vakkhāma i ai ayadhātuvasaina tidhātumayattā samayasaddassa| tatra itīti ikārānantaratyantapadassa ca ‘‘aiti, udaitī’’tiādīnañ‍ca aikārānantaratyantapadānaṃ añ‍ñaisañ‍ca aivarūpānaṃ padamālā yathārahaṃ yaibhuyyaina attanaupadāni vaj‍jaitvā yaujaitabbā| īdisaisu hi ṭhānaisu duk‍karā kriyāpadamālā| yasmā pana imasmiṃ pakaraṇai sukarā ca duk‍karā ca tyantapadamālā jānitabbā, tasmā bhūvādigaṇādīsu aṭṭhasu gaṇaisu vihitaihi chan‍navutiyā vacanaihi sabbasādhāraṇaṃ asabbasādhāraṇañ‍ca padamālānayaṃ brūma –

akārānantaratyanta-padānaṃ pantiyau budhau|

bhavati rundhatādīnaṃ, yaujai sabbattha sabbathā||

iti aitī’’ti caitaisaṃ, padānaṃ pana pantiyau|

suddhassarapubbakānaṃ, yaujai viñ‍ñū yathārahaṃ||

ākārānantaratyanta-padānañ‍cāpi pantiyau|

‘‘yāti suṇāti asnā-ti’’ ic‍cādīnaṃ yathārahaṃ||

ivaṇṇānantaratyanta-padānamapi pāḷiyau|

yaujai ‘‘rundhiti rundhīti’’-ic‍cādīnaṃ yathārahaṃ||

ukārānantaratyanta-sutiiti padassa ca|

pairaṇatthai pavattassa, yaujai mālaṃ yathārahaṃ||

aikārānantaratyanta-padānampi yathārahaṃ|

‘‘jaiti rundhaiti kāraiti, kārāpaitī’’tiādīnaṃ||

aukārānantaratyanta-padānampi padak‍kamai|

‘‘karauti bhauti hautī’’ti-ādīnaṃ yuttitauvadai|

ic‍caivaṃ sattadhā vuttau, padamālānayau mayā|

itau muttau nayau nāma, natthi kauci kriyāpadai||

‘‘ādattai kurutai paitai’’, ic‍cādi nayadassanā|

yathārahaṃ yuttitauti, vacanaṃ aittha bhāsitaṃ||

idāni ikārānantaratyantapadassa kamau vuc‍catai – iti, inti| isi, itha| imi, ima| aparipuṇṇau vattamānānayau| itu, intu| ihi, itha| imi, ima| aparipuṇṇau pañ‍camīnayau| aittha ca imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ sāsanānurūpabhāvassa imāni sādhakapadāni  ‘‘vaiti, apaiti, anvaitī’’ti| tattha vi iti vaiti| vigacchatīti atthau, itisaddau haittha gamanaṃ baudhaiti| tathā apa iti apaiti| apagacchatīti atthau| anu iti anvaiti| anugacchatīti atthau| garū pana anu aiti anvaitīti vadanti| taṃ –

‘‘yathā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ, dantiṃ anvaiti hatthinī|

jaissantaṃ giriduggaisu, samaisu visamaisu ca|

aivaṃ taṃ anugacchāmi, puttai ādāya pacchatau’’ti||

imāya pāḷiyā na samaiti ‘‘jaissantaṃ anvaitī’’ti vacanatau ‘‘anugacchāmī’’ti vacanatau ca| tathā hi aitisaddau yattha cai iriyāpathavācakau, tattha āgamanaṃyaiva jautaiti, na gamanaṃ, tasmā āgamanatthassa ayuttitau gamanatthassa ca yuttitau vi itiādinā chaidau ñaiyyau| aitaisañ‍ca itisaddavasaina katachaidānaṃ atthibhāvaṃ yuttibhāvañ‍ca ‘‘itāyaṃ kaudharūpainā’’ti pāḷiyaiva sādhaiti, tasmāyaiva ‘‘anu iti, anu inti, anu isī’’tiādinā ‘‘anvaitī’’tiādīnaṃ chaidai labbhamānanayaina vuttappakārau vattamānāpañ‍camīnayau parassapadavasaina dassitau| sattamīrūpādīni sabbathā appasiddhāni|

imāni pana bhavissantiyā rūpāni, sittā tai lahumaissati| issati, issanti| issasi, issatha| issāmi, issāma| issatai, issantai| issasai, issavhai| issaṃ, issāmhai| asabbadhātukattaipi suddhassarattā dhātussa ikārāgamau na labbhati| paripuṇṇau bhavissantīnayau|

atha kālātipattiyā rūpāni bhavanti, issā, issaṃsu| issai, issatha| issaṃ, issamhā| issatha, issisu| issasai, issavhai| issaṃ, issāmhasai| kālātipattibhāvai  ca asabbadhātukattai ca santaipi suddhassarattā dhātussa akārikārāgamau na labbhati anaikantikattā vā anupapan‍nattā ca akārāgamau na hauti| dvin‍nañhaittha suddhassarānaṃ anantarikānaṃ aikatausan‍nipātau anupapatti| paripuṇṇau kālātipattinayau|

imasmiṃ pana ṭhānai sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai sautūnaṃ payaugatthaisu paramakausal‍lajananatthaṃ ‘‘nanu tai sutaṃ brāhmaṇa bhañ‍ñamānai, daivā na issanti purisaparak‍kamassā’’ti pāḷitau nayaṃ gahaitvā vuttappakāraihi bhavissantiyā rūpaihi sabbasau samānāni asamānatthāni vattamānikarūpāni ca īsakaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samānāni bhavissantīkālātipattīnaṃ rūpāni ca pakāsayissāma – vattamānāvasaina tāva ‘‘issati, issanti| issasi, issathā’’ti sabbaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ| atthau pana ‘‘issaṃ karautī’’tiādinā vattabbau| tasmiṃyaiva atthai bhavissantīvasaina ‘‘ississati, ississanti| ississasi, ississathā’’ti paripuṇṇaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ| atthau pana ‘‘issaṃ karissatī’’tiādinā vattabbau| kālātipattivasaina pana ‘‘ississā, ississaṃsu| ississai, ississathā’’ti paripuṇṇaṃ yaujaitabbaṃ, atthau pana ‘‘issaṃ akarissā’’tiādinā vattabbau| dhātvantaravasaina saṃsandanānayauyaṃ|

idāni aikārānantaratyantapadassa kamau vuc‍catai –

aiti, ainti| aisi, aitha| aimi, aima|

aitu, aintu| aihi, aitha| aimi, aima|

na ca appatvā dukkhantaṃ, vissāsaṃ aiyya paṇḍitau|

nivaisanāni māpaitvā, vaidaihassa yasassinau|

yadā tai pahiṇissāmi, tadā aiyyāsi khattiya||

aiyya, aiyyuṃ| aiyyāsi, aiyyātha| aiyyāmi, aiyyāma| aitha, airaṃ| aithau, aiyyāvhau| aiyyaṃ, aiyyāmhai|

sau purisau aiyya, tai aiyyuṃ| tvaṃ aiyyāsi, tumhai aiyyātha| ahaṃ aiyyāmi, mayaṃ aiyyāma| sau purisau aitha, tai airaṃ| tvaṃ aithau, tumhai aiyyāvhau| ahaṃ aiyyaṃ, mayaṃ aiyyāmhai|

paraukkhāhiyyattanaj‍jatanīrūpāni sabbasau appasiddhāni|

aissati, aissanti| aissasi, aissatha| aissāmi, aissāma| aissatai, aissantai| aissasai, aissavhai| aissaṃ, aissāmhai|

sammaudamānā gacchanti, jālamādāya pakkhinau|

yadā tai vivadissanti, tadā aihinti mai vasaṃ||

‘‘abhidausagatau idāni aihī’’ti vacanadassanatau aparānipi bhavissantīrūpāni gahaitabbāni|

aihiti, aihinti| aihisi, aihitha| aihimi, aihima| aihitai, aihintai| aihisai, aihivhai| aihissaṃ, aihissāmhai|

aissā, aissaṃsu| aissai, aissatha| aissaṃ aissāmhā| aissatha, aissisu| aissasai, aissavhai| aissiṃ, aissāmhasai|

athāparaupi aikārānantaratyantapadak‍kamau bhavati|

udaiti, udainti| udaisi, udaitha| udaimi, udaima||

udaitu, udaintu| udaihi, udaitha| udaimi, udaima, udāmasai|

udaiyya, udaiyyuṃ| saisaṃ naiyyaṃ| udissati, udissanti| saisaṃ naiyyaṃ| udissā, udissaṃsu| saisaṃ naiyyaṃ|

imāni suddhassaradhāturūpāni|

kakārantadhātu

ku  saddai kai ca| kauti, kavati, kāyati, aivaṃ kattupadāni bhavanti| kuyyati, kiyyati, aivaṃ kammapadāni| kānanaṃ, kabbaṃ, jātakaṃ, aivaṃ nāmikapadāni| kutvā, kutvāna, kavitvā, kavitvāna, kāvitvā, kāvitvāna, kāyituṃ, aivaṃ abyayapadāni|

tatra kānananti ṭhitamajjhanhikasamayai kavati saddaṃ karautīti kānanaṃ, vanaṃ| tathā hi –

‘‘ṭhitai majjhanhikai kālai, san‍nisīvaisu pakkhisu|

saṇataiva brahārañ‍ñaṃ, sā rati paṭibhāti ma’’nti

vuttaṃ| atha vā kaukilamayūrādayau kavanti saddāyanti kūjanti aitthāti kānanaṃ| manauharatāya avassaṃ kuyyati paṇḍitaihīti kabbaṃ| kāviyaṃ| kāvaiyyaṃ| añ‍ñatra pana kavīnaṃ idanti kabbanti taddhitavasaina atthau gahaitabbau| kaici tu kābyanti saddarūpaṃ icchanti, na taṃ pāvacanai pamāṇaṃ sak‍kaṭabhāsābhāvatau| sak‍kaṭabhāsātaupi hi ācariyā nayaṃ gaṇhanti| jātaṃ bhūtaṃ atītaṃ bhagavatau cariyaṃ, taṃ kīyati kathīyati aitaināti jātakaṃ| jātakapāḷi hi idha ‘‘jātaka’’nti vuttā| añ‍ñatra pana jātaṃ aiva jātakanti gahaitabbaṃ| tathā hi jātakasaddau daisanāyampi vattati ‘‘itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhamma’’ntiādīsu| jātiyampi vattati ‘‘jātakaṃ samaudhānaisī’’tiādīsu|

pak‍ka nīcagatiyaṃ| nīcagamanaṃ nāma hīnagamanaṃ hīnappavatti vā| nīcasaddau hi hīnavācakau ‘‘nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau’’ti aittha viya| pak‍kati kriyāpadamaittha dissati, na nāmikapadaṃ| yattha yattha nāmikapadaṃ na dissati, tattha tattha nāmikapadaṃ upaparikkhitvā  gahaitabbaṃ| kriyāpadamaiva hi duddasaṃ, kriyāpadai vij‍jamānai nāmikapadaṃ natthīti na vattabbaṃ, tasmā antamasau ‘‘pak‍kanaṃ, takanaṃ’’ ic‍caivamādīni bhāvavācakāni nāmikapadāni sabbāsu dhātūsu yathārahaṃ labbhantīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

taka hasanai| hasanaṃ hāsau| takati|

taki kicchajīvanai| kicchajīvanaṃ kasirajīvanaṃ| taṅkati| ātaṅkati| ātaṅkau| ātaṅkauti kicchajīvitakarau raugau, tathā hi aṭṭhakathācariyā ‘‘appābādhaṃ appātaṅka’’nti imasmiṃ pāḷippadaisai iti atthaṃ saṃvaṇṇaisuṃ ‘‘ābādhauti visabhāgavaidanā vuc‍cati, yā aikadaisai uppaj‍jitvā sakalasarīraṃ ayapaṭṭaina bandhitvā viya gaṇhāti| ātaṅkauti kicchajīvitakarau raugau| atha vā yāpaitabbaraugau ātaṅkau, itarau ābādhau| khuddakau vā raugau ātaṅkau, balavā ābādhau| kaici pana ‘ajjhattasamuṭṭhānau ābādhau, bahiddhāsamuṭṭhānau ātaṅkau’ti vadantī’’ti|

ātaṅkau āmayau raugau,

byādhā’bādhau gadau rujā|

akal‍lañ‍caiva gailañ‍ñaṃ,

nāmaṃ raugābhidhānakaṃ||

suka gatiyaṃ| saukati, sukau, sukī| tatra sukauti suvau| saukati manāpaina gamanaina gacchatīti sukau| tassa bhariyā sukī|

buk‍ka bhassanai| idha bhassanaṃ nāma sunakhabhassanaṃ adhippaitaṃ ‘‘sunakhau bhassitvā’’ti aittha viya, na ‘‘āvāsau gaucaraṃ  bhassa’’ntiādīsu viya| vacanasaṅkhātaṃ bhassanaṃ, buk‍kati sā|

dhaka paṭighātai gatiyañ‍ca| paṭighātau paṭihananaṃ| dhakati|

caka tittipaṭighātaisu| titti tappanaṃ, paṭighātaṃ paṭihananaṃva| cakati|

aka kuṭilagatiyaṃ| akati| aitā kuādikā akapariyantā dhātuyau parassa bhāsāti saddasatthavidū vadanti| taisaṃ matai aitā ‘‘ti anti, tu antu’’ ic‍cādīnaṃyaiva visayau| pāḷiyaṃ pana niyamau natthi, tasmā na taṃ idha pamāṇaṃ|

i ajjhayanai| ajjhayanaṃ uc‍cāraṇaṃ sikkhanaṃ vā, ayati, adhīyati, ajjhayati, adhītai, ajjhainaṃ, ajjhāyakau| dibbaṃ adhīyasai māyaṃ| adhīyanti mahārāja, dibbamāyidha paṇḍitā| ajjhainamariyā pathaviṃ janindā| tattha ajjhāyakauti ajjhayatīti ajjhāyakau, mantai parivattaitīti atthau|

u saddai| avati, avanti| avasi| aittha ‘‘yau ātumānaṃ sayamaiva pāvā’’ti pāḷi papubbassa udhātussa payaugauti daṭṭhabbau| papubbassa vadadhātussa dakāralaupappayaugautipi vattuṃ yuj‍jati|

vaṅka kauṭil‍lai| vaṅkati| vaṅkaṃ| vaṅkasaddau hi vak‍kasaddaina samānatthau, vak‍kasaddau ca vaṅkasaddaina| tathā hi –

‘‘yaṃ nissitā jagatiruhaṃ, svāyaṃ aggiṃ pamuñ‍cati|

disā bhajatha vak‍kaṅgā, jātaṃ saraṇatau bhaya’’nti||

pāḷi  dissati| ayaṃ pana vak‍kasaddau sak‍kaṭabhāsaṃ patvā kakārarakārasañ‍ñaugakkharikau bhavati, dhātubhāvau panassa paurāṇaihi na vuttau, tasmā kriyāpadaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ| imassa pana vaṅkasaddassa ‘‘vaṅka kauṭil‍lai’’ti dhātubhāvau vuttau, ‘‘vaṅkatī’’ti kriyāpadañ‍ca, pāḷiyaṃ tu ‘‘vaṅkatī’’ti kriyāpadaṃ na diṭṭhaṃ, tathā bhāvavācakau vaṅkasaddaupi| vāc‍caliṅgau pana anaikaisu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhau| tatta vaṅkatīti kriyāpadaṃ pāḷiyaṃ avij‍jamānampi gahaitabbamaiva nāthatīti kriyāpadamiva| bhāvavācakassa pana vaṅkasaddassa atthitā natthitā ca pāḷiādīsu punappunaṃ upaparikkhitabbā| kaicaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘yadi bhāvavācakau vaṅkasaddau natthi, kathaṃ ‘aṭṭhavaṅkaṃ maṇiratanaṃ uḷāra’nti aittha samāsau’’ti| aittha pana aṭṭhasu ṭhānaisu vaṅkaṃ aṭṭhavaṅkaṃ, na aṭṭhavaṅkāni yassāti| dabbavācakau hi vaṅkasaddau, na bhāvavācakauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

vaṅkaṃ vak‍kañ‍ca kuṭilaṃ, jimhañ‍ca rimhamanuju|

vaṅkasaddādayau aitai, vāc‍caliṅgā tiliṅgikā||

atha vā vaṅkasaddauyaṃ, ‘‘vaṅkaghastā’’tiādisu|

baḷisai giribhaidai ca, vattatai sa pumā tadā||

ayañhi ‘‘tai’mai janā vaṅkaghastā sayanti| yathāpi macchau baḷisaṃ, vaṅkaṃ maṃsaina chāditaṃ| vaṅkaghastauva ambujau’’tiādīsu baḷisai vattati|

aittha siyā ‘‘nanu ca bhau ‘yathāpi macchau baḷisaṃ, vaṅkaṃ maṃsaina chādita’nti aittha vaṅkasaddau guṇavācakau visaisanasadau, yaina baḷisau visaisitau, taina vaṅkaṃ kuṭilaṃ baḷisanti atthau viñ‍ñāyatī’’ti? tan‍na, vaṅkasaddai avuttaipi baḷisasabhāvassa vaṅkattā kuṭilatthau pākaṭauti natthi visaisanasaddaina  payaujanaṃ| idaṃ pana ‘‘baḷisaṃ vaṅka’’nti vacanaṃ ‘‘hatthi nāgau| sarauruhaṃ padumaṃ| hatthī ca kuñ‍jarau nāgau’’tiādivacanamiva pariyāyavacanaṃ, tasmā ‘‘vaṅka’’nti padassa ‘‘kuṭila’’nti atthau na gahaitabbau| atha vā yathā ‘‘yathā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ, dantiṃ anvaiti hatthinī’’ti aittha nāgasaddassa dantīsaddassa ca añ‍ñamañ‍ñapariyā yavacanattaipi dantinti manauramadantayuttanti atthau saṃvaṇṇitau, tathā ‘‘baḷisaṃ vaṅka’’nti imaisampi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pariyāyavacanattaipi vaṅkanti kuṭilanti atthau vattabbau| aivañhi sati atthau sālarājā viya suphul‍litau hauti, daisanā ca vilāsappattā, na pana ‘‘vaṅkaṃ baḷisa’’nti saddānaṃ guṇaguṇīvasaina samānādhikaraṇabhāvau icchitabbau ‘‘buddhau bhagavā vairañ‍jāyaṃ viharatī’’tiādīsu ‘‘buddhau bhagavā’’ti imaisaṃ viya samānādhikaraṇabhāvassa anicchitabbattā| na hi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu samānādhikaraṇabhāvau paurāṇaihi anumatau|

‘‘yattha aitādisau satthā, laukai appaṭipuggalau|

tathāgatau balappattau, sambuddhau parinibbutau’’ti,

‘‘buddhaṃ buddhaṃ nikhilavisayaṃ suddhiyā yāva suddhi’’nti ca ādīsu pana anumatau| aittha hi ‘‘aitādisau’’ti ca ‘‘appaṭipuggalau’’ti ca ‘‘tathāgatau’’ti ca ‘‘balappattau’’ti ca ‘‘sambuddhau’’ti ca ‘‘parinibbutau’’ti ca imāni ‘‘satthā’’ti anaina padaina samānādhikaraṇāni| tathā ‘‘buddhaṃ buddha’’nti dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ pacchimaṃ purimaina samānādhikaraṇaṃ bhavati|

iti ‘‘yathāpi macchau baḷisaṃ, vaṅkaṃ maṃsaina chādita’’nti aittha vaṅkasaddau baḷisassābhidhānantaraṃ, na guṇavācakau| aivaṃ vaṅkasaddau baḷisai vattati| ‘‘kaṅkaṃ gacchāma pabbataṃ| dūrai vaṅkatapabbatau’’tiādīsu  pana girivisaisai vattati| aittha ca ‘‘vaṅkapabbatau’’ti vattabbai sukhuc‍cāraṇatthaṃ niruttinayaina majjhai animittaṃ takārāgamaṃ katvā ‘‘vaṅkatapabbatau’’ti vuttaṃ| atha vā vaṅkauyaiva vaṅkatā, yathā daivau aiva daivatā| yathā ca disā aiva disatāti, aivaṃ tāpac‍cayavasaina vaṅkatā ca sā pabbatau cāti ‘‘vaṅkatapabbatau’’ti vuttaṃ, majjhai rassavasaina caitaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā vaṅkamassa saṇṭhānamatthīti vaṅkatauti mantuatthai tapac‍cayau, yathā pabbamassa atthīti pabbatauti| aivaṃ vaṅkatau ca sau pabbatau cāti vaṅkatapabbatau| ‘‘vaṅkapabbatau’’ ic‍caiva vā paṇṇatti, pādakkharapāripūriyā pana ‘‘dūrai vaṅkatapabbatau’’ti vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

lauka dassanai| laukati| laukau| ālaukauti añ‍ñānipi rūpāni gahaitabbāni| curādigaṇaṃ pana patvā imissā ‘‘laukaiti, laukayati, aulaukaiti, aulaukayatī’’tiādinā rūpāni bhavanti| laukauti tayau laukā saṅkhāralaukau sattalaukau aukāsalaukauti| tattha ‘‘aikau laukau, sabbai sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā’’ti āgatau saṅkhārau aiva laukau saṅkhāralaukau| sattā aiva laukau sattalaukau| cak‍kavāḷasaṅkhātau aukāsau aiva laukau aukāsalaukau, yau ‘‘bhājanalaukau’’tipi vuc‍cati| taisu saṅkhārau luj‍jatīti laukauti| vuttañhaitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘luj‍jati paluj‍jatīti khau bhikkhu tasmā laukauti vuc‍catī’’ti| laukiyati aittha puñ‍ñapāpaṃ tabbipākau cāti sattau laukau| laukiyati vicittākāratau dissatīti cak‍kavāḷasaṅkhātau aukāsau laukau| yasmā pana laukasaddau samūhaipi dissati, tasmā laukiyati samudāyavasaina  pañ‍ñāpiyatīti laukau, samūhauti ayampi atthau gahaitabbau| atha vā laukauti tayau laukā kilaisalaukau bhavalaukau indriyalaukauti| taisaṃ sarūpaṃ curādigaṇai kathaissāma bahuvidhatañ‍ca| bahiddhā pana kavīhi ‘‘laukau tu bhuvanai janai’’ti aittakamaiva vuttaṃ|

silauka saṅghātai| saṅghātau piṇḍanaṃ| silaukati, silaukau, silaukamanukassāmi| akkharapadaniyamitau vacanasaṅghātau silaukau| sau paj‍janti vuc‍cati, tathā hi ‘‘silaukau yasassi paj‍jai’’ti kavayau vadanti|

daika dhaika saddussāhaisu| saddau ravau, ussāhau vāyāmau| daikati| dhaikati|

raika saki saṅkāyaṃ| raikati| saṅkati, tasmiṃ mai saṅkatai manau| saṅkā|

aki lakkhaṇai| aṅkati, aṅkau, sasaṅkau|

maki maṇḍanai| maṇḍanaṃ bhūsanaṃ, maṅkati|

kata lauliyai| laulabhāvau lauliyaṃ yathā dakkhiyaṃ| kakati, kākau, kākī| aittha ‘‘kākau, dhaṅkau, vāyasau, bali, bhauji, ariṭṭhau’’ti imāni kākābhidhānāni|

kuka vaka ādānai| kukati, vakati, kaukau, vakau| aittha kaukauti arañ‍ñasunakhau| vakauti khuddakavanadīpikau, byagghautipi vadanti|

vaka dittiyaṃ paṭighātai ca| ditti saubhā, vakati|

kaki  vaki sak‍ka tika ṭika saika gatyatthā| kaṅkati, vaṅkati, sak‍kati, nisak‍kati, parisak‍kati, ausak‍kati, vadhāya parisak‍kanaṃ| biḷāranisak‍kamattampi| taikati, ṭaikati, ṭīkā saikati| aittha ṭīkāti ṭikiyati jāniyati saṃvaṇṇanāya atthau aitāyāti ṭīkā| aitā idhātuādikā saikapariyantā dhātuyau ‘‘attanaubhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti| taisaṃ matai aitā ‘‘tai antai, taṃ antaṃ’’ic‍cādīnaṃyaiva visayau, pāvacanai pana niyamau natthi|

hik‍ka abyattasaddai| abyattasaddau avibhāvitatthasaddau niratthakasaddau ca| hik‍kati, hik‍katai| imaṃ ‘‘ubhayataubhāsā’’ti vadanti| idaṃ tu pāvacanaina saṃsandati| parassattanaubhāsānañhi dhātūnaṃ ‘‘bhavati, bhavatai, bādhatai, bādhatī’’tiādinā yaibhuyyaina dvidhā dvidhā rūpāni sāsanai dissanti|

imāni kakārantadhāturūpāni|

khakārantadhātu

khā pakathanai khyā ca| pakathanaṃ ācikkhanaṃ daisanaṃ vā| khāti, saṅkhāti| āpubbattai visadisabhāvaina khātyakkharassa dvittaṃ, ākārassa ca sañ‍ñaugapubbattā rassattaṃ, akkhāti| akkhāsi purisuttamau| akkhaiyyaṃ tai ahaṃ ayyai| dhammau saṅkhāyati| akkhāyati| atra pana kakāralaupau| svākhātau bhagavatā dhammau| saṅkhātau| akkhātau| akkhātārau tathāgatā| saṅkhātā sabbadhammānaṃ vidhurau| saṅkhā, paṭisaṅkhā| kriyaṃ ākyāti kathaitīti ākhyātaṃ| kaici  pana ‘‘svākhātau’’ti ca ‘‘svākkhyātau’’ti ca ‘‘svākhyātau’’ti ca padamicchanti| tattha pacchimāni sak‍kaṭabhāsātau nayaṃ gahaitvā vuttāni, itaraṃ yathāṭhitarūpanipphattivasaina, atau yathādassitapadāniyaiva pasatthatarāni| tattha saṅkhāsaddassa atthuddhārau nīyatai – saṅkhāsaddau ñāṇakauṭṭhāsapañ‍ñattigaṇanāsu dissati| ‘‘saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivatī’’tiādīsu hi ñāṇai dissati| ‘‘papañ‍casañ‍ñāsaṅkhā samudācarantī’’tiādīsu kauṭṭhāsai| ‘‘taisaṃ taisaṃ dhammānaṃ saṅkhā samañ‍ñā’’tiādīsu pañ‍ñattiyaṃ| ‘‘na sukaraṃ saṅkhātu’’ntiādīsu gaṇanāyaṃ| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘ñāṇapañ‍ñattikauṭṭhāsa-gaṇanāsu padissati|

saṅkhāsaddauti dīpaiyya, dhammadīpassa sāsanai’’ti||

khi khayai| khiyanadhammaṃ khīyati| sāsanānurūpaina sarai ikārassa iyyādaisau, khiyyati| ‘‘khayau, khaṃ’’ ic‍capi rūpāni ñaiyyāni| tattha khayauti khiyanaṃ khayau| atha vā khiyanti kilaisā aitthāti khayau, magganibbānāni| khayasaṅkhātaina maggaina pāpuṇiyattā phalampi khayau| khanti tucchaṃ suñ‍ñaṃ vivittaṃ rittaṃ, khanti vā ākāsau|

khi nivāsai| khīyati, khiyyati vā| sāsanānurūpaina ikārassa īya iyyādaisau daṭṭhabbau| ayaṃ divādigaṇaipi pakkhipitabbau| khaṃ khayaṃ| abhiramaṇīyaṃ rājakkhayaṃ| tattha khīyatīti nivasati| khanti cakkhādiindriyaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇādīnaṃ nivāsaṭṭhaina| khayanti nivaisanaṃ| rājakkhayanti rañ‍ñau nivaisanaṃ| atrāyaṃ pāḷi –

‘‘sacai  ca aj‍ja dhāraisi, kumāraṃ cārudassanaṃ|

kusaina jātakhattiyaṃ, savaṇṇamaṇimaikhalaṃ|

pūjitā ñātisaṅghaihi, na gacchasi yamakkhaya’’nti||

tattha yamakkhayanti yamanivaisanaṃ|

khu saddai| khauti khavati|

khai khādanasattāsu| khāyati| undūrā khāyanti| vikkhāyitakaṃ| gaukhāyitakaṃ| assirī viya khāyati| disāpi mai na pakkhāyanti| aitthādimhi kāyatīti khādati| atha vā upaṭṭhāti pañ‍ñāyati|

sukha dukkha takriyāyaṃ| takriyāti sukhadukkhānaṃ vaidanānaṃ kriyā, sukhanaṃ dukkhananti vuttaṃ hauti| akammakā imai dhātavau| sukhati, dukkhati| sukhaṃ, dukkhaṃ| sukhitau, dukkhitau| sukhaṃ sātaṃ pīṇanaṃ, dukkhaṃ vighātaṃ aghaṃ kilaisau| tattha sukhanti sukhayatīti sukhaṃ| yassuppaj‍jati, taṃ sukhitaṃ karautīti atthau| dukkhanti dukkhayatīti dukkhaṃ| yassuppaj‍jati, taṃ dukkhitaṃ karautīti atthau| imāni nibbacanāni kāritavasaina vuttānīti daṭṭhabbaṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ sukhadukkhasaddatthaṃ vadantaihi garūhi sukhayati dukkhayatisaddānaṃ kammatthamādāya vivaraṇassa katattā| tathā hi ‘‘sukhaiti sukhayati, sukhāpaiti sukhāpayati, dukkhaiti dukkhayati, dukkhāpaiti dukkhāpayatī’’ti imāni taisaṃ kāritapadarūpāni, attānaṃ sukhaiti pīṇaitīti ca, sukhayati sukhaṃ, dukkhayatīti dukkhanti ca,

‘‘sacai  ca kimhici kālai,

maraṇaṃ mai purai siyā|

puttai ca mai paputtai ca,

sukhāpaiyya mahausadhau’’ti ca

pāḷiādidassanatau| saddasatthai pana dhātupāṭhasaṅkhaipai ca imai dhātavau curādigaṇaiyaiva vuttā| ‘‘sukhayati dukkhayatī’’ti ca akāritāni suddhakattupadāni icchitāni| mayaṃ tu taisaṃ tabbacanaṃ suddhakattari ca tāni padarūpāni na icchāma pāḷiādīhi viruddhattā, tasmāyaiva tai imasmiṃ bhūvādigaṇai vuttā| ayañhi suddhakattuvisayai asmākaṃ ruci ‘‘sukhatīti sukhitau, dukkhatīti dukkhitau’’ti|

nanu ca bhau ‘‘sukhati dukkhatī’’ti kriyāpadāni buddhavacanai na dissantīti? sac‍caṃ, aivaṃ santaipi aṭṭhakathānayavasaina gahaitabbattā dissantiyaiva nāma| na hi sabbathā sabbaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ rūpāni sāsanai laukai vā labbhanti, aikac‍cāni pana labbhanti, aikac‍cāni na labbhanti| aivaṃ santaipi nayavasaina labbhantiyaiva| ‘‘kappayavhau patissatā’’ti hi diṭṭhai ‘‘caravhau bhuñ‍javhau’’tiādīnipi nayavasaina diṭṭhāniyaiva nāma|

tatra panāyaṃ nayau| visuddhimaggādīsu hi ‘‘aikadviyaujanamattampi addhānaṃ gatassa vāyau kuppati, gattāni dukkhantī’’ti aivaṃ bhūvādigaṇikaṃ akammakaṃ suddhakattuvācakaṃ ‘‘dukkhantī’’ti kriyāpadaṃ dissati| tasmiṃ diṭṭhiyaiva ‘‘sukhati, sukhanti| sukhasi, sukhatha| sukhāmi, sukhāmā’’tiādīni ca ‘‘dukkhati, dukkhanti| dukkhasi, dukkhathā’’tiādīnica diṭṭhāni nāma haunti diṭṭhaina adiṭṭhassa tādisassa anavaj‍jassa nayassa gahaitabbattā, tasmā ‘‘sukhatīti sukhitau, dukkhatīti dukkhitau’’ti bhūvādinayau aiva gahaitabbau, na pana curādinayau| aparampaittha nibbacanaṃ, sukhaṃ sañ‍jātaṃ  aitassāti sukhitau, sañ‍jātasukhauti atthau| aisa nayau dukkhitauti aitthāpi| atha vā sukhaina itau pavattauti sukhitau| aisa nayau ‘‘dukkhitau’’ti aitthāpi| dul‍labhāyaṃ nīti sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbā|

maukkha muc‍canai| akammakauyaṃ dhātu| maukkhati| maukkhau| pātimaukkhau| kāritai ‘‘maukkhaiti, maukkhayati, maukkhāpaiti, maukkhāpayatī’’ti rūpāni| kaici panimaṃ ‘‘maukkha maucanai’’ti paṭhitvā curādigaṇai pakkhipanti| taisaṃ matai ‘‘maukkhaiti, maukkhayatī’’ti suddhakattupadāni bhavanti| aitāni pāḷiyā aṭṭhakathāya ca virujjhanti| tathā hi ‘‘maukkhanti mārabandhanā| na mai samaṇai maukkhasi| mahāyañ‍ñaṃ yajissāma, aivaṃ maukkhāma pāpakā’’ti pāḷiyā virujjhanti| ‘‘yau naṃ pāti rakkhati, taṃ maukkhaiti maucaiti āpāyikādīhi dukkhaihīti pātimaukkhau’’ti aṭṭhakathāya ca virujjhanti, tasmā pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘maukkhaisi maukkhaimā’’ti ca avatvā ‘‘maukkhasi, maukkhāmā’’ti suddhakattuvācakaṃ vuttaṃ, tañ‍ca khau apādānavisayaṃ katvā| aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘maukkhaiti, maucaitī’’ti haitukattuvācakaṃ vuttaṃ, tampi apādānavisayaṃyaiva katvā| aivaṃ imassa dhātunau suddhakattuvisayai akammakabhāvau viditau, haitukattuvisayai aikakammakabhāvau viditau muca paca chidādayau viya| maukkhadhātu dvigaṇikauti cai? na, anaikaisu sāṭṭhakathaisu pāḷippadaisaisu ‘‘maukkhaiti, maukkhayatī’’ti suddhakatturūpānaṃ adassanatauti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kakkha hasanai| kakkhati|

aukha rākha lākha dākha dhākha sausanālamatthaisu| aukhati| rākhati| lākhati| dākhati| dhākhati|

sākha  byāpanai| sākhati| sākhā|

ukha nakha makha rakha lakha rakhi lakhi ikhi rikhi gatyatthā| ukhati| nakhati| makhati| rakhati| lakhati| raṅkhati| laṅkhati| iṅkhati| riṅkhati|

rakkha pālanai| rakkhati| rakkhā, rakkhaṇaṃ, sīlaṃ rakkhitau daivadattau, sīlaṃ rakkhitaṃ daivadattaina, sīlaṃ rakkhakau daivadattau|

akkha byattisaṅkhātaisu| akkhati, akkhi, akkhaṃ|

nikkha cumbanai| nikkhati, nikkhaṃ|

nakkha gatiyaṃ| nakkhati| nakkhattaṃ| aittha nakkhattanti aittau itau cāti visamagatiyā agantvā attanau vīthiyāva gamanaina nakkhanaṃ gamanaṃ tāyati rakkhatīti nakkhattaṃ| paurāṇā pana ‘‘nakkharanti na nassantīti nakkhattānī’’ti kathayiṃsu| ‘‘nakkhattaṃ, jauti, nirikkhaṃ, bhaṃ’’ ic‍caitai pariyāyā|

vaikkha vaikkhanai| vaikkhati|

makkha saṅkhatai| makkhati|

takkha tapanai| tapanaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ| takkhati|

sukkha anādarai| sukkhati|

kakhi vakhi makhi kaṅkhāyaṃ| satthari kaṅkhati| vaṅkhati, maṅkhati| ‘‘kaṅkhā kaṅkhāyanā kaṅkhāyitattaṃ vimati vicikicchā dvaiḷhakaṃ dvaidhāpathau saṃsayau anaikaṃsagāhau āsappanā parisappanā apariyaugāhanā thambhitattaṃ cittassa manauvilaikhau’’ ic‍caitai kaṅkhāpariyāyā| aitaisu pana –

vattanti  laukavauhārai, ‘‘kaṅkhā vimati saṃsayau|

vicikicchā’’ti aitāni, nāmāniyaiva pāyatau||

kakhi ic‍cāyaṃ| dhanaṃ kaṅkhati, abhikaṅkhati, nābhikaṅkhāmi maraṇaṃ| abhikaṅkhitaṃ dhanaṃ|

dakhi dhakhi ghauravāsitai kaṅkhāyañ‍ca| daṅkhati| dhaṅkhati|

ukkha saicanai| ukkhati|

kakha hasanai| kakhati|

jakkha bhakkhaṇai ca| hasanānukaḍḍhanatthaṃ cakārau| jakkhati|

likha laikhanai| likhati, sal‍laikhati| atisal‍laikhataivāyaṃ samaṇau| laikhā, laikhanaṃ, laikhakau, likhitaṃ, sal‍laikhapaṭipatti, aitā dakhiādikā likhapariyantā ‘‘parassabhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

dhukkha dhikkha sandīpanakilaisanajīvanaisu| dhukkhati| dhikkhati| saddasatthavidū pana ‘‘dhukkhatai dhikkhatai’’ti attanaubhāsaṃ vadanti| tathā itau parāni rūpānipi|

rukkha vakkha varaṇai| varaṇaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ| rukkhati| vakkhati| rukkhau, vakkhau| aittha ca vakkhauti rukkhauyaiva| tathā hi ‘‘sādūni ramaṇīyāni, santi vakkhā arañ‍ñajā’’ti jātakapāṭhau dissati| imāni pana rukkhassa nāmāni –

‘‘rukkhau mahīruhau vakkhau, pādapau jagatīruhau|

agau nagau kujau sākhī, sālau ca viṭapī taru|

dumau phalī tu phalavā, gacchau tu khuddapādapau’’ti||

kaicaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu ca sālasaddaina sālarukkhauyaiva vuttau, nāñ‍ñau ‘sālā phandanamāluvā’ti payaugadassanatau, atha kimatthaṃ sālasaddaina yau kauci rukkhau  vuttau’’ti? na sālarukkhauyaiva sālasaddaina vuttau, atha khau sālarukkhaipi vanappatijaiṭṭharukkhaipi yasmiṃ kasmiñ‍ci rukkhaipi ‘‘sālau’’ti vauhārassa dassanatau añ‍ñaipi rukkhā vuttā| tathā hi sālarukkhaupi ‘‘sālau’’ti vuc‍cati| yathāha ‘‘saiyyathāpi bhikkhavai gāmassa vā nigamassa vā avidūrai mahantaṃ sālavanaṃ, tañ‍cassa ailaṇḍaihi sañchan‍naṃ| antaraina yamakasālāna’’nti| vanappatijaiṭṭharukkhaupi| yathāha –

‘‘tavaiva daiva vijitai, tavaivuyyānabhūmiyā|

ujuvaṃsā mahāsālā, nīlaubhāsā manauramā’’ti||

yau kauci rukkhaupi| yathāha ‘‘atha khau taṃ bhikkhavai māluvabījaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ sālamūlai nipataiyyā’’ti| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘sālarukkhai jaiṭṭharukkhai,

yasmiṃ kasmiñ‍ci pādapai|

sālau iti ravau sālā,

sandhāgārai thiyaṃ siyā’’ti||

sikkha vij‍jaupādānai| sikkhati| sikkhā, sikkhanaṃ, sikkhitaṃ sippaṃ, sikkhakau, sikkhitau, saikkhau, asaikkhau| kakāralaupai ‘‘saikhau asaikhau’’ti rūpāni bhavanti| tattha sikkhitauti sañ‍jātasikkhau, asikkhīti vā sikkhitau, tathā hi kattuppayaugau dissati ‘‘ahaṃ khau pana susikkhitau anavayau sakai ācariyakai kumbhakārakammai’’ti|

bhikkha yācanai| bhikkhati| bhikkhu, bhikkhā, bhikkhanaṃ, bhikkhakau, bhikkhitaṃ bhaujanaṃ| aittha pana ‘‘bhikkhu yati samaṇau muni pabbajitau  anagārau tapassī tapaudhanau’’ ic‍caitāni pariyāyavacanāni| aitaisu sāsanai ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti upasampan‍nau vuc‍cati| kadāci pana ‘‘bhikkhusataṃ bhaujaisi, bhikkhusahassaṃ bhaujaisī’’tiādīsu sāmaṇairaipiupādāya ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti vauhārau pavattati, tāpasāpi ca samaṇasaddādīhi vuc‍canti, ‘‘ahū atītamaddhānai, samaṇau khantidīpanau’’tiādi aittha nidassanaṃ|

dakkha vuddhiyaṃ sīghattai ca| dakkhati, dakkhiṇā, dakkhau| dakkhanti vaddhanti sattā aitāya yathādhippaitāhi sampattīhi iddhā vuddhā uk‍kaṃsagatā hauntīti dakkhiṇā, dātabbavatthu| dakkhati kusalakammai añ‍ñasmiñ‍ca kic‍cākic‍cai adandhatāya sīghaṃ gacchatīti dakkhau, chaikau, yau ‘‘kusalau’’tipi vuc‍cati|

dikkha muṇḍiaupanayananiyamabbatādaisaisu| dikkhadhātu muṇḍiyai, upanayanai, niyamai, vatai, ādaisai ca pavattati| dikkhati| dikkhitau muṇḍau| aittha siyā – nanu ca bhau sarabhaṅgajātakai ‘‘gandhau isīnaṃ ciradikkhitānaṃ, kāyā cutau gacchati mālutainā’’ti aitasmiṃ padaisai aṭṭhakathācariyaihi ‘‘ciradikkhitānanti cirapabbajitāna’’nti vuttaṃ| na hi tattha ‘‘ciramuṇḍāna’’nti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ santai kasmā idha ‘‘dikkhadhātu muṇḍiyai vuttā’’ti? sac‍caṃ, tattha pana dikkhitasaddassa pabbajitai vattanatau ‘‘cirapabbajitāna’’nti vuttaṃ, na dhātuatthassa vibhāvanatthaṃ| ida pana dhātuatthavibhāvanatthaṃ muṇḍiyai vuttā| tāpasā hi muṇḍiyatthavācakaina dikkhitasaddaina vattuṃ yuttā| tathā hi aṭṭhakathācariyaihi cak‍kavattisuttatthavaṇṇanāyaṃ ‘‘kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā’’ti imissā pāḷiyā atthavivaraṇai ‘‘tāpasapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajantāpi hi paṭhamaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhārainti, tatau paṭṭhāya parūḷhakaisai  bandhitvā vivaranti, taina vuttaṃ kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā’’ti aivaṃ atthau saṃvaṇṇitau|

ikkha dassanaṅkaisu| ikkhati, upaikkhati, apaikkhati| upaikkhā, apaikkhā, pac‍cavaikkhaṇā| kakāralaupai ‘‘upaikhā, apaikhā, upasampadāpaikhau’’ti rūpāni bhavanti|

dukkha hiṃsāgatīsu| dakkhati| dakkhakau|

cikkha cakkha viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ| cikkhati, ācikkhati, abbhācikkhati| ācikkhakau| cakkhati, cakkhu| aittha cakkhūti cakkhatīti cakkhu, samavisamaṃ abhibyattaṃ vadantaṃ viya hautīti atthau| atha vā ‘‘sūpaṃ cakkhati, madhuṃ cakkhatī’’tiādīsu viya yasmā assādatthaupi cakkhusaddau bhavati, tasmā ‘‘cakkhati viñ‍ñāṇādhiṭṭhitaṃ rūpaṃ assādaintaṃ viya hautī’’ti assādatthaupi gahaitabbau| ‘‘cakkhuṃ khau māgaṇḍiyaṃ rūpārāmaṃ rūparataṃ rūpasammudita’’nti hi vuttaṃ| satipi sautādīnaṃ saddārāmatādibhāvai nirūḷhattā nayanai aiva cakkhusaddau pavattati paṅkajādisaddā viya padumādīsu|

cakkha’kkhi nayanaṃ, laucanaṃ diṭṭhi dassanaṃ|

paikkhanaṃ acchi pamhaṃ tu, ‘‘pakhuma’’nti pavuc‍cati||

aitā rukkhādikā cakkhapariyantā ‘‘attanaubhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

khakārantadhāturūpāni|

gakārantadhātu

gu karīsussaggai| karīsussaggau vac‍cakaraṇaṃ| gavati| gai saddai| gāyati| gītaṃ|

vagga  gatiyaṃ| vaggati| vaggau, vaggitaṃ| aittha samudāyavasaina vagganaṃ pavattanaṃ vaggau| vaggitanti gamanaṃ| tathā hi nāgapaitavatthuaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘yau sau majjhai assatarīrathaina catubbhi yuttaina suvaggitaina| amhākaṃ puttau ahu majjhimau sau, amaccharī dānapatī viraucatī’’ti imissā pāḷiyā atthaṃ vadantaihi ‘‘suvaggitaināti sundaragamanainā’’ti| kiñ‍ci bhiyyau kriyāpadampi ca diṭṭhaṃ ‘‘dhunanti vagganti pavattanti cambarai’’ti|

ragi lagi agi vagi magi igi rigi ligi tagi sagi gamanai ca| cakārau gatipaikkhakau| raṅgati| raṅgau| laṅgati| laṅgau, laṅgī| aṅgati, aṅgaiti| aṅgau, samaṅgī, samaṅgitā, aṅgaṃ, aṅgaṇaṃ| vaṅgati| vaṅgau| maṅgati| maṅgau, upaṅgau, maṅgalaṃ| iṅgati| iṅgitaṃ| riṅgati| riṅganaṃ| liṅgati| liṅganaṃ| ul‍liṅgati, ul‍liṅganaṃ| taṅgati| taṅganaṃ| saṅgati| saṅganaṃ| tattha aṅganti yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci vatthūnaṃ avayavau, sarīrampi kāraṇampi ca vuc‍cati| aṅgaṇanti katthaci kilaisā vuc‍canti ‘‘rāgau aṅgaṇa’’ntiādīsu| rāgādayau hi aṅganti aitaihi taṃsamaṅgipuggalā nihīnabhāvaṃ gacchantīti ‘‘aṅgaṇānī’’ti vuc‍canti| katthaci malaṃ vā paṅkau vā ‘‘tassaiva rajassa vā aṅgaṇassa vā pahānāya vāyamatī’’tiādīsu| añ‍jati makkhaitīti hi aṅgaṇaṃ, malādi| katthaci tathārūpau vivaṭappadaisau ‘‘caitiyaṅgaṇaṃ, baudhiyaṅgaṇa’’ntiādīsu| añ‍jati tattha ṭhitaṃ atisundaratāya abhibyañ‍jaitīti hi aṅgaṇaṃ, vivaṭau bhūmippadaisau| ic‍caivaṃ –

rāgādīsu kilaisaisu, paṅkai kāyamalamhi ca|

vivaṭai bhūmibhāgai ca, ‘‘aṅgaṇa’’nti ravau gatau||

yugi  jugi vaj‍janai| yuṅgati| juṅgati|

ragi saṅkāyaṃ| raṅgati|

laga saṅgaica| ca kārau anantaravuttāpaikkhakau| lagati| ca jatau na hauti laganaṃ| baḷisai laggau|

thagaṃ saṃvaraṇai| thagati|

agga kuṭilagatiyaṃ| aggatīti aggi, kuṭilaṃ gacchatīti atthau|

aggi dhūmasikhau jauti, jātavaidau sikhī gini|

aggini bhāṇumā taijau, pāvakau tivakau’nalau||

hutāsanau dhūmakaitu, vaissānarau ca ac‍cimā|

ghatāsanau vāyusakhau, dahanau kaṇhavattani||

aitā guādikā aggapariyantā ‘‘parassabhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

gā gatiyaṃ| gāti|

gu saddai| gavati|

gu uggamai| uggamau uggamanaṃ pākaṭatā| gavati| saddasatthavidū panimāsaṃ ‘‘gātai gavatai’’ti attanaubhāsattaṃ vadanti|

gakārantadhāturūpāni|

ghakārantadhātu

ghā gandhaupādānai| ghāti| ghānaṃ| gandhaṃ ghatvā| atrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘gandhaṃ ghatvā sati muṭṭhā’’ti| aitissā pana divādigaṇaṃ pattāya ‘‘ghāyati ghāyitvā’’ti rūpāni bhavanti|

ghu  abhigamanai| abhigamanaṃ adhigamanaṃ| ghauti|

jaggha hasanai| jagghati, sañ‍jagghati| sañ‍jagghitthau mayā saha| jagghitumpi na saubhati| jagghitvā|

taggha pālanai| tagghati|

sighi āghānai| āghānaṃ ghānaina gandhānubhavanaṃ| siṅghati, upasiṅghati| upasiṅghitvā| ārā siṅghāmi vārijaṃ| aitā ‘‘parassabhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

ghu saddai| ghauti, ghavati|

raghi laghi gatyakkhaipai| gatyakkhaipau gatiyā akkhaipau| raṅghati, laṅghati, ul‍laṅghati| laṅghitā, ul‍laṅghikāpīti, laṅghitvā|

maghi kaitavai ca| cakārau pubbatthāpaikkhau| maṅghati|

rāgha lāgha sāmatthiyai| rāghati| lāghati|

dāgha āyāsai ca| āyāsau kilamanaṃ| cakārau sāmatthiyāpaikkhakau| dāghati| nidāghau|

silāgha katthanai| katthanaṃ pasaṃsanaṃ| silāghati| silāghā| buddhassa silāghatai| silāghitvā| ‘‘attanaubhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

gha kārantadhāturūpāni|

iti bhūvādāgaṇai kavaggantadhāturūpāni

samattāni|

cakārantadhātu

idāni  cavaggantadhāturūpāni vuc‍cantai –

suca saukai| saucati| saukau, saucanā, saucaṃ, saucantau, saucantī, saucantaṃ kulaṃ, saucitvā|

kuca saddai tārai| tārasaddau ac‍cuc‍casaddau| kaucati| uc‍casaddaṃ karautīti atthau|

kuñ‍ca kauṭil‍la’ppībhāvaisu| kuñ‍cati| kuñ‍cikā, kuñ‍citakaisau| kuñ‍citvā|

luñ‍ca apanayanai| luñ‍cati| luñ‍cakau, luñ‍cituṃ, luñ‍citvā|

añ‍cu gatipūjanāsu| maggaṃ añ‍cati| buddhaṃ añ‍cati| uddhaṃ anuggantvā tiriyaṃ añ‍citauti tiracchānau| kaṭukañ‍cukatā|

vañ‍cu cañ‍cu tañ‍cu gatiyaṃ| vañ‍cati| cañ‍cati| tañ‍cati| mañ‍cati| santi pādā avañ‍canā| avañ‍canāti vañ‍cituṃ gantuṃ asamatthā|

gucu gaṇaicu thaiyyakaraṇai| thainanaṃ thaiyyaṃ, caurikā| tassa kriyā thaiyyakaraṇaṃ| gaucati| gaṇaicati|

ac‍ca pūjāyaṃ ac‍cati| brahmāsurasurac‍citau|

tac‍ca hiṃsāyaṃ| tac‍cati|

cac‍ca jac‍ca paribhāsanavaj‍janaisu| cac‍cati| jac‍cati|

kuca saṃpac‍canakauṭil‍lapaṭik‍kamavilaikhanaisu| kucati, saṅkucati| saṅkaucau|

taca saṃvaraṇai| saṃvaraṇaṃ rakkhaṇaṃ| tacati| tacau|

dica  thutiyaṃ| dicati|

kuca saṅkaucanai| kaucati, saṅkaucati| saṅkaucau|

byāca byājikaraṇai| byājikaraṇaṃ byājikriyā| byācati|

vaca viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ| viyattassa aisā viyatti, tissaṃ viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ, viyattāyaṃ vācāyanti adhippāyau| viyattassa hi vadatau puggalassa vasaina vācā viyattā nāma vuc‍cati| yathā pana kucchisaddatiracchānagatādisaddau ‘‘abyattasaddau’’ti vuc‍cati, na aivaṃ vacanasaṅkhātau saddau ‘‘abyattasaddau’’ti vuc‍cati viñ‍ñātatthattā| ‘‘vatti, vacati, vacanti| vacasi’’ ic‍cādīni suddhakattupadāni| ‘‘vācaiti, vācainti’’ ic‍cādīni haitukattupadāni| ‘‘atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍cati| vuc‍canti| santau sappurisā laukai, daivadhammāti vuc‍carai’’ ic‍cādīni kammapadāni| garū pana vakārassa ukārādaisavasaina ‘‘uttaṃ uc‍catai uc‍cantai’’tiādīni icchanti, tāni sāsanai appasiddhāni, sak‍kaṭabhāsānulaumāni| sāsanasmiñhi rakārāgamavisayai nipubbassaiva vacassa vassa ukārādaisau siddhau ‘‘nirutti, niruttaṃ, nairutta’’nti| vacanaṃ, vācā, vacau, vacī, vuttaṃ, pavuttaṃ, vuc‍camānaṃ, adhivacanaṃ, vattabbaṃ, vacanīyaṃ, imāni nāmikapadāni| vattuṃ, vattavai, vatvā, vatvāna, imāni tumantādīni ‘‘parassabhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti|

tattha vattīti vadati| ākhyātapadañhaitaṃ| atthasaṃvaṇṇakaihipi ‘‘vatti aitāyāti vācā’’ti nibbacanamudāhaṭaṃ| saddasatthai ca tādisaṃ ākhyātapadaṃ diṭṭhaṃ| aittha panaikai vadanti ‘‘vacati, vacantītiādīni kriyāpadarūpāni buddhavacanai aṭṭhakathāṭīkāsu satthaisu ca anāgatattā chaḍḍaitabbānī’’ti| tan‍na, yasmā sāsanai ‘‘avaca, avaciṃsū’’ti suddhakattupadāni ca ‘‘vācaiti, vācaintī’’tiādīni haitukattupadāni ca dissanti, tasmā  buddhavacanādīsu anāgatānipi ‘‘vacati, vacantī’’tiādīni rūpāni gahaitabbāni| vacaiyya, vuc‍catu, vuc‍caiyya| saisaṃ sabbaṃ sabbattha vitthāratau gahaitabbaṃ|

paraukkhārūpāni vadāma – vaca, vacu| vacai, vacittha| vacaṃ, vacimha| vacittha, vacirai| vacitthau, vacivhau| vaciṃ, vacimhai|

hiyyattanīrūpāni vadāma – avacā, avacū| avacau, avacuttha| avaucaṃ, avacumha| avacuttha, avacutthuṃ| avacasai, avacuvhaṃ| avaciṃ, avacamhasai|

aj‍jatanīrūpāni vadāma – avaci, avaucuṃ, avaciṃsu| avaucau, avaucuttha| avauciṃ, avaucumha| avaucā, avaucu| avacasai, avaucivaṃ| avaucaṃ, avaucimhai|

bhavissantīrūpāni vadāma – vakkhati, vakkhanti| vakkhasi, vakkhatha| vakkhāmi, vakkhāma| vakkhatai, vakkhantai| vakkhasai, vakkhavhai| vakkhassaṃ vakkhamhai|

imaisaṃ pana padānaṃ ‘‘kathaissati, kathaissantī’’tiādinā atthau vattabbau| vakkha rausaiti dhātussa ca ‘‘vakkhati, vakkhanti| vakkhasī’’tiādīni vatvā avasānai uttamapurisaikavacanaṭṭhānai ‘‘vakkhaimī’’ti vattabbaṃ| atthau panimaisaṃ ‘‘rausati, rausantī’’tiādinā vattabbau| ayaṃ vacavakkhadhātūnaṃ bhavissantīvattamānāvasaina rūpasaṃsandanānayau| aparānipi vacadhātussa bhavissantī sahitāni rūpāni bhavanti – vakkhissati, vakkhissanti| vakkhissasi, vakkhissatha| vakkhissāmi, vakkhissāma| vakkhissatai, vakkhissantai| vakkhissasai, vakkhissavhai| vakkhissaṃ, vakkhissāmhai|

atrāyaṃ pāḷi –

‘‘abhītakappai caritaṃ, ṭhapayitvā bhavābhavai|

imamhi kappai caritaṃ, pavakkhissaṃ suṇauhi mai’’ti||

gadratapañhaipi  ‘‘rājā tumhaihi saddhiṃ paṭisanthāraṃ katvā gahapati patirūpaṃ āsanaṃ ñatvā nisīdathāti vakkhissatī’’ti aivamādiaṭṭhakathāpāṭhau dissati, tasmāyaiva aidisī padamālā racitā| vakkha rausaiti dhātussapi bhavissantīsahitāni rūpāni ‘‘vakkhissati, vakkhissantī’’tiādīni bhavanti| atthau panimaisaṃ ‘‘rausissati, rausissantī’’tiādinā vattabbau| ayaṃ vacavakkhadhātūnaṃ bhavissantīvasainaiva rūpasaṃsandanānayau|

avacissā, vacissā, avacissaṃsu, vacissaṃsu| saisaṃ sabbaṃ naiyyaṃ| idha pana vuttasaddassa atthuddhāraṃ vattabbampi avatvā upariyaiva kathaissāma itau ativiya vattabbaṭṭhānattā|

cu cavanai| cavati| kāritai ‘‘cāvaitī’’ti rūpaṃ| daivakāyā cutau| cutaṃ padumaṃ| cavituṃ, cavitvā|

lauca dassanai| laucati| laucanaṃ|

saica saicanai| saicati|

saca viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ| sacati|

kaca bandhanai| kacati|

maca muci kak‍kanai| kak‍kanaṃ sarīrai ubbaṭṭanaṃ| macati| muñ‍cati| maci dhāraṇucchāyapūjanaisu| dhāraṇaṃ ucchāyau pūjananti tayau atthā| tattha ucchāyau malaharaṇaṃ| mañ‍cati| mañ‍cau, mañ‍canaṃ| mañ‍cati puggalaṃ dhāraitīti mañ‍cau|

paca byattikaraṇai| pacati| pākau, paripākau, vipākau, pak‍kaṃ phalaṃ|

thuca pasādai| thaucati|

vaca vaci dittiyaṃ| vacati| vañ‍cati|

ruca  dittiyaṃ raucanai ca| ditti saubhā| raucanaṃ ruci| raucati| vairaucanau| samaṇassa raucatai sac‍caṃ| tassa tai saggakāmassa, aikattamuparaucitaṃ| ayañ‍ca divādigaṇai ruciatthaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘ruc‍catī’’ti rūpaṃ janaiti| taina ‘‘gamanaṃ mayha ruc‍catī’’ti pāḷi dissati| curādigaṇai pana ruciatthaṃ gahaitvā ‘‘raucaiti raucayatī’’ti rūpāni janaiti| taina ‘‘kiṃ nu jātiṃ na raucaisī’’tiādikā pāḷiyau dissanti| taigaṇikauyaṃ dhātu|

paca saṃpākai| pacati, pacanti| saddasatthavidū pana ‘‘attanaubhāsā’’ti vadanti|

añ‍ca byayagatiyaṃ| byayagati vināsagati| añ‍cati|

yāca yācanāyaṃ| brāhmaṇau nāgaṃ maṇiṃ yācati| nāgau maṇiṃ yācitau brāhmaṇaina| tai taṃ assai ayācisuṃ| sau taṃ rathamayācatha| daivadattaṃ āyācati| aivaṃ suddhakattari rūpāni bhavanti| brāhmaṇau brāhmaṇaina nāgaṃ maṇiṃ yācaiti, yācayati, yācāpaiti, yācāpayati| aivaṃ haitukattari| rājā brāhmaṇaina dhanaṃ yāciyati, yācayiyati, yācāpiyati, yācāpayiyati| aivaṃ kammani| yācaṃ, yācantau, yācantī, yācantaṃ kulaṃ| yācamānau, yācamānā, yācamānaṃ kulaṃ| yācakau, yācanā, yācitabbaṃ, yācituṃ, yācitvāna, yācituna, yāciya, yāciyāna| aivaṃ nāmikapadāni tumantādīni ca bhavanti|

paca pākai| audanaṃ pacati| ‘‘ubhayataubhāsā’’ti saddasatthavidū vadanti| yathā pana sāsanai ‘‘paṇḍitauti pavuc‍catī’’ti  vacadhātussa kammani rūpaṃ pasiddhaṃ, na tathā pacadhātussa| aivaṃ santaipi garū ‘‘tayā pac‍catai audanau’’ti tassa kammani rūpaṃ vadanti| sāsanai pana avisaisatau ‘‘pac‍catai’’ti vā ‘‘pac‍catī’’ti vā vuttassapi padassa akammakauyaiva divādigaṇikau payaugau icchitabbau ‘‘daivadattau nirayai pac‍cati| yāva pāpaṃ na pac‍catī’’tiādidassanatau| kaicaittha vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘sayamaiva pīyatai pānīyantiādi viya bhūvādigaṇapakkhikau kammakattuppayaugau aisa, tasmā ‘sayamaivā’ti padaṃ ajjhāharitvā ‘sayamaiva daivadattau pac‍catī’tiādinā atthau vattabbau’’ti tan‍na, ‘‘sayamaiva pīyatai pānīya’’nti aittha hi pānīyaṃ manussā pivanti, na pānīyaṃ pānīyaṃ pivati| manussaihaiva taṃ pīyatai, na sayaṃ| aivaṃ parassa pānakriyaṃ paṭic‍ca kammabhūtampi taṃ sukarapānakriyāvasaina sukarattā attanāva sijjhantaṃ viya hautīti ‘‘sayamaiva pīyatai pānīya’’nti rūḷhiyā payaugau katau|

‘‘sayamaiva kaṭau kariyatai’’ti aitthāpi kaṭaṃ manussā karaunti, na kaṭaṃ kaṭau karauti| manussaihaiva kaṭau kariyatai, na sayaṃ| aivaṃ parassa karaṇakriyaṃ paṭic‍ca kammabhūtaupi sau sukaraṇa kriyāvasaina sukarattā attanāva sijjhantau viya hautīti ‘‘sayamaiva kaṭau kariyatai’’ti rūḷhiyā payaugau katau|

aittha yathā sayaṃsaddau pānīyaṃ pānīyainaiva pīyatai, na amhaihi| kaṭau kaṭainaiva kariyatai, na amhaihīti sakammakavisayattā payaugānaṃ añ‍ñassa kriyāpaṭisaidhanasaṅkhātaṃ atthavisaisaṃ vadati, na tathā ‘‘daivadattau nirayai pac‍cati, kammaṃ pac‍catī’’tiādīsu tumhaihi ajjhāharitau sayaṃsaddau atthavisaisaṃ vadati akammakavisayattā aitaisaṃ payaugānaṃ| aivaṃ  ‘‘daivadattau’’tiādikassa pac‍cattavacanassa akammakakattuvācakattā kammarahitasuddhakattuvācakattā ca ‘‘pac‍catī’’ti idaṃ divādigaṇikarūpanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| pacadhātu saddasatthai divādigaṇa vuttau natthīti cai? natthi vā atthi vā, kimaittha saddasatthaṃ karissati, pāḷi aiva pamāṇaṃ, tasmā mayaṃ laukavauhārakusalassa bhagavatau pāḷinayañ‍ñaiva gahaitvā imaṃ pacadhātuṃ divādigaṇaipi pakkhipissāma| tathā hi dhammapālācariya anuruddhācariyādīhi abhisaṅkhatā divādigaṇikappayaugā dissanti –-

ñāṇayuttavaraṃ tattha, datvā sandhiṃ tihaitukaṃ|

pacchā pac‍cati pākānaṃ, pavattai aṭṭhakai duvai||

asaṅkhāraṃ sasaṅkhāra-vipākāni ca pac‍cati

ic‍caivamādayau| aittha pana taisaṃ idamaiva pāḷiyā na samaiti| yai curādigaṇamhi sakammakabhāvaina bhūvādigaṇai ca akammakabhāvaina pavattassa bhūdhātussaiva bhūvādigaṇai pavattassa sakamakassapi satau divādigaṇaṃ patvā akammakabhūtassa pacadhātussa sakammakattamicchanti| aitañhi sāṭṭhakathai taipiṭakai buddhavacanai kutau labbhā, tasmā bhagavatau pāvacanai sautūnaṃ saṃsayasamugghāṭatthaṃ aittha imaṃ nītiṃ paṭṭhapaima –

vināpi upasaggaina, gaṇanānattayaugatau|

sakammākammakā haunti, dhātū pacabhidādayau||

purisau audanaṃ pacati| sa bhūtapacaniṃ paci| audanau pac‍cati| kammaṃ pac‍cati| vīhisīsaṃ pac‍cati| rukkhaphalāni pac‍canti| nāgau pākāraṃ bhindati| taḷākapāḷi bhij‍jati| bhij‍janadhammaṃ bhij‍jati| aittha ca sayaṃsaddaṃ ajjhāharitvā ‘‘sayamaiva audanau pac‍catī’’tiādinā vuttaipi ‘‘purisau sayamaiva pāṇaṃ hanati| bhagavā sayamaiva  ñaiyyadhammaṃ abujjhī’’ti payaugaisu parassa āṇattisambhūtahananakriyāpaṭisaidhamiva paraupadaisasambhūtabujjhanakriyāpaṭisaidhanamiva ca añ‍ñassa kriyāpaṭisaidhanavasaina vuttattā yau sayaṃsaddavasaina kammakattubhāvaparikappau, taṃ na pamāṇaṃ| sayaṃsaddau hi suddhakattuatthaipi dissati, na kaivalaṃ ‘‘sayamaiva pīyatai pānīya’’ntiādīsu kammatthaiyaiva, tasmā sāsanānurūpaina atthau gahaitabbau nayañ‍ñūhi|

vināpi upasaggaina, vināpi ca gaṇantaraṃ|

sakammākammakā haunti, atthatau divuādayau||

kāmaguṇaihi dibbati| pac‍cāmittai dibbati| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

gaṇantarañ‍caupasaggaṃ, vināpi atthanānataṃ|

payaugatau sakammā ca, akammā ca gamādayau||

purisau maggaṃ gacchati| gambhīraisupi atthaisu ñāṇaṃ gacchati| dhammaṃ carati| tattha tattha carati|

gaṇantarañ‍caupasaggaṃ, payaugañ‍catthanānataṃ|

vināpi tividhā haunti, disādī rūpabhaidatau||

pāsādaṃ passati, pāsādaṃ dakkhati pāsādau dissati| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

sabhāvatau sakammā tu, rudadhātādayau matā|

sabhāvatau akammā ca, nandadhātādayau matā||

mataṃ vā amma raudanti, idha nandati paic‍ca nandati|

upasaggavasainaikai, sakammāpi akammakā|

sambhavanti tathaikac‍cai, akammāpi sakammakā||

aikac‍cai  tupasaggaihi, sakammā ca sakammakā|

akammakā akammā ca, aisatthaupaittha dīpitau|

purisau gāmā niggacchati, dhanaṃ adhigacchati, purisau pāṇaṃ abhibhavati, himavatā pabhavanti mahānadiyau| añ‍ñānipi payaugāni yaujaitabbāni|

tattha yadi sāsanai pacadhātussa kammani rūpaṃ siyā, ‘‘purisaina kammaṃ kariyatī’’ti payaugau viya ‘‘purisaina audanau paciyatī’’ti payaugau icchitabbau| yai pana garū ‘‘tayā pac‍catai audanau’’tiādīni icchanti, tai saddasatthanayaṃ nissāya vadanti mañ‍ñai| aivaṃ santaipi upaparikkhitvā yuttāni cai, gahaitabbāni| kāritai ‘‘purisau purisaina purisaṃ vā audanaṃ pācaiti, pācayati, pācāpaiti, pācāpayati| purisaina purisau audanaṃ pāciyati, pācayiyati, pācāpiyati, pācāpayiyatī’’ti rūpāni bhavanti| ‘‘yathā daṇḍaina gaupālau, gāvaṃ pācaiti gaucara’’ntiādīsu añ‍ñaupi atthau daṭṭhabbau|

pacaṃ, pacantau, pacantī, pacamānau, pacamānā| pātabbaṃ, pacitaṃ, pacitabbaṃ, pacanīyaṃ, pacituṃ, pacitvā| aittha ca ‘‘imassa maṃsañ‍ca pātabba’’nti payaugau udāharaṇaṃ| ‘‘pacati, pacanti| pacasī’’tiādi padak‍kamau subaudhau|

sica gharaṇai| saicati, saikau, ‘‘ubhataubhāsā’’ti vadanti|

imāni cakārantadhāturūpāni|

chakārantadhātu

parassabhāsādibhāvaṃ, sabbaisaṃ dhātunaṃ itau|

paraṃ na byākarissaṃ sau, sāsanai īritau na hi||

chu  chaidanai| chauti| chautvāna mauḷiṃ varagandhavāsitaṃ| acchaucchuṃvata bhau rukkhaṃ|

milaicha aviyattāyaṃ vācāyaṃ| milaicchati, milakkhu| pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu pac‍cājātau hauti milakkhūsu aviñ‍ñātāraisu|

vachi icchāyaṃ| vañchati| vañchitaṃ dhanaṃ|

achi āyāmai| añ‍cati| dīghaṃ vā añchantau dīghaṃ añchāmīti pajānāti|

huccha kauṭil‍lai| hucchati|

muccha mauhamucchāsu| mucchati| mucchitau visavaigaina, visañ‍ñī samapaj‍jatha| mucchā, mucchitvā|

phucha visaraṇai phauchati|

yucha pamādai| yucchati|

uchi uñchai| uñchau pariyaisanaṃ| uñchati| uñchācariyāya īhatha|

ucha pipāsāyaṃ| uchati|

puccha pañhai pucchati| pucchitā, pucchakau, puṭṭhau, pucchitau, pucchā| bhikkhu vinayadharaṃ pañhaṃ pucchati| pucchi, pucchituṃ| pucchitvā| aittha ca pañ‍cavidhā pucchā adiṭṭhajautanāpucchā diṭṭhasaṃsandanāpucchā vimaticchaidanāpucchā anumatipucchā kathaitukamyatāpucchāti| tāsaṃ nānattaṃ aṭṭhasāliniyāditau gahaitabbaṃ|

viccha gatiyaṃ| vicchati| vicchikā|

vacchu  chaidanai| vucchati| vuttā, vuttavā, vuttasirau| vakāragatassa akārassa uttaṃ|

vuttasaddau kaisauharaṇaipi dissati ‘‘kāpaṭikau māṇavau daharau vuttasirau’’tiādīsu| aittha ca sirasaddaina sirauruhā vuttā yathā mañ‍casaddaina mañ‍caṭṭhā, cakkhusaddaina ca cakkhunissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| raupitaipi ‘‘yathā sāradikaṃ bījaṃ, khaittai vuttaṃ virūhatī’’tiādīsu| kathitaipi ‘‘vuttamidaṃ bhagavatā, vuttamarahatā’’tiādisu| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

vacchuvapavacavasā, vuttasaddau pavattati|

kaisauhārai raupitai ca, kathitai ca yathāk‍kamanti||

aparau nayau – vuttasaddau ‘‘nau ca khau paṭivutta’’ntiādīsu vāpasamīkaraṇai dissati| ‘‘pan‍nalaumau paradattavuttau’’tiādīsu jīvitavuttiyaṃ| ‘‘paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pavuttau’’tiādīsu apagamai| ‘‘gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samīhita’’ntiādīsu pāvacanavasaina pavattitai| laukai pana ‘‘vuttau pārāyanau’’tiādīsu ajjhainai dissati, atridaṃ vuc‍cati

‘‘vāpasamīkaraṇai ca, athau jīvitavuttiyaṃ|

apagamai pāvacana-vasaina ca pavattitai|

ajjhainai caivamaitaisu, vuttasaddau padissatī’’ti||

aparaupi nayau – vuttasaddau saupasaggauca anupasaggau ca vapanai vāpasamīkaraṇai kaisauhārai jīvitavuttiyaṃ pamuttabhāvai  pāvacanavasaina pavattitai ajjhainai kathanaiti aivamādīsu dissati| tathā haisa –

‘‘gāvau tassa pajāyanti, khaittai vuttaṃ virūhati|

vuttānaṃ phalamasnāti, yau mittānaṃ na dubbhatī’’ti

ādīsu vapanai āgatau| ‘‘nau ca khau paṭivutta’’ntiādīsu aṭṭhadantakādīhi vāpasamīkaraṇai| ‘‘kāpaṭikau māṇavau daharau vuttasirau’’tiādīsu kaisauharaṇai| ‘‘pan‍nalaumau paradattavuttau migabhūtaina caitasā viharatī’’tiādīsu jīvitavuttiyaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi nāma paṇḍupalāsau bandhanā pavuttau abhabbau haritatthāyā’’tiādīsu bandhanatau pamuttabhāvai| ‘‘yaisamidaṃ aitarahi paurāṇaṃ mantapadaṃ gītaṃ pavuttaṃ samīhita’’ntiādīsu pāvacanabhāvaina pavattitai| laukai pana ‘‘vuttau gaṇau, vuttau pārāyanau’’tiādīsu ajjhainai| ‘‘vuttaṃ khau panaitaṃ bhagavatā, dhammadāyādā mai bhikkhavai bhavatha, mā āmisadāyādā’’tiādīsu kathanai| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

vapa vatu vacchuvaca-dhātūnaṃ vasatau matau|

saupasaggau naupasaggau, vuttasaddau yathārahaṃ||

vapanai ca vāpasamī-karaṇai muṇḍatāya ca|

jīvavutyaṃ pamuttatthai, vasā pāvacanassa tu|

pavattitai ca ajjhainai, kathanai cāti lakkhayai|

taccha tanukaraṇai| tacchati| tacchakau dāruṃ|

chakārantadhāturūpāni|

jakārantadhātu

jijayai| jaiti| jayati, parājayati| dhammaṃ carantau sāmikaṃ parājaiti| dhammaṃ carantau paraj‍jati| rājānaṃ jayāpaisuṃ| jayāpaitvā| aittha jayāpaisunti ‘‘jayatu bhava’’nti āsīsavacanaṃ vadiṃsūti atthau| jayanaṃ, jitaṃ, jayau, vijitaṃ, jinau, jaitā, jaitau, jitau mārau, māraṃ jitau, jitavā, jitāvī, vijitāvī, māraji, laukaji, audhijinau, anaudhijinau, jitau| vijitau, jaituṃ, vijaituṃ, jitvā, vijitvā| imassa pana dhātussa kiyādigaṇaṃ pattassa ‘‘jināti jinitvā’’tyādīni rūpāni bhavanti|

ji abhibhavanai| jaiti| jinau| pubbai viya rūpāni| aittha ca ‘‘tumhaihi ānanda sappurisaihi vijitaṃ, pacchimā janatāsālimaṃsaudanaṃ atimañ‍ñissatī’’ti pāḷi abhibhavanatthasādhakā| aittha hi vijitanti adhibhūtanti atthau|

ju gatiyaṃ| aittha sīghagati adhippaitā| javati| javanaṃ, javau, javaṃ, javantau, javanacittaṃ, javanapañ‍ñau, javanahaṃsau| manaujavaṃ gacchati yainakāmaṃ|

jaikhayai| jīyati| aikārassa īyādaisau| sāsanānurūpaina ‘‘kiṃ maṃ dhanaina jīyaithā’’ti hi pāḷi dissati| saddasatthavidū pana ‘‘jāyatī’’ti rūpaṃ vadanti|

saj‍ja gatiyaṃ| saj‍jati|

kuju khuju thaiyyakaraṇai| kaujati| khaujati|

vaja gatiyaṃ| dhaja dhaji ca| vajati| abbajati| manussattañ‍ca abbajai| vajau, vajanaṃ, pavajanaṃ, pabbaj‍jā, pabbajitau, pabbājitau|

sakā raṭṭhā pabbājitau, añ‍ñaṃ janapadaṃ gatau|

mahantaṃ kauṭṭhaṃ kayirātha, duruttānaṃ nidhaitavai||

dhajati| dhajau| dhañ‍jati| dhañ‍janaṃ| aittha dhajauti kaitu| dhañ‍jananti gamanaṃ|

aja khaipanai ca| gatiapaikkhakauyaiva cakārau| ajati| ajau| aittha ajauti aiḷakau| imāni panassa pariyāyavacanāni ‘‘ajau aiḷakau urabbhau avi maiṇḍau’’ti| tattha urabbhauti aiḷakau, yau ‘‘ajau’’tipi vuc‍cati| avīti rattalaumau aiḷakau| maiṇḍauti kuṭilasiṅgau aiḷakau| tathā hi janakajātakai ajarathatau maiṇḍarathā visuṃ vuttā| apica ajaiḷakanti ajatau aiḷakassa visuṃ vacanatau aiḷakasaddaina maiṇḍaupi gahaitabbau, mahausadhajātakaṭṭhakathāyañhi maiṇḍaiḷakānaṃ nibbisaisatā vuttāti|

aj‍ja saj‍ja aj‍janai| aj‍janaṃ aj‍janakriyā| aj‍jati| saj‍jati|

kaj‍ja byathanai| byathanaṃ hiṃsā| kaj‍jati|

khaj‍ja maj‍janai ca| maj‍janaṃ suddhi| byathanāpaikkhau cakārau| khaj‍jati| khaj‍jūrau|

khaja manthai| manthau vilauḷanaṃ| khajati|

khaji gativaikal‍lai| kissa bhantai ayyau khañ‍jatīti| ubhau khañ‍jā| khañ‍janaṃ, khañ‍jituṃ, khañ‍jitvā|

khaja kampanai| khajati| aijā| aittha ca aijāti lābhādiṃ paṭic‍ca aijati kampatīti aijā, balavataṇhāyaitaṃ nāmaṃ|

buja vajiranibbaisai| vajiranigghausaiti kaici vidū vadanti| baujati|

khija  kuji guji abyattasaddai| khijati| kuñ‍jati| guñ‍jati|

laja lāja taj‍ja bhassanai| lajati| lājati| taj‍jati|

laji dittiyañ‍ca| bhassanāpaikkhau cakārau| lañ‍jati| tatiyau nayalañ‍jakau| lañ‍jaiti pakāsaiti suttatthanti lañ‍jakau|

jaja jaji yuddhai| yujjhanaṃ yuddhaṃ| jajati| jañ‍jati|

tuja hiṃsāyaṃ| taujati|

tuji balanai ca| balanaṃ balanakriyā| hiṃsāpaikkhakau cakārau| tuñ‍jati|

gaja kuji muji gaj‍ja saddatthā| gajati| kuñ‍jati| muñ‍jati| gajau gaj‍jati, maighau gaj‍jati| yattha dāsau āmajātau, ṭhitau thul‍lāni gaj‍jati| maṇi gaj‍jati| ñāṇagaj‍janaṃ gaj‍jati| gaj‍jituṃ samatthau| gaj‍jitā| gaj‍jitvā| tattha gajauti hatthī| hatthissa hi anaikāni nāmāni –

hatthī nāgau gajau dantī, kuñ‍jarau vāraṇau karī|

mātaṅgau dviradau saṭṭhi-hāyanau’naikapau ibhau||

thambhau rammau dvipau caiva, hatthinī tu karaiṇukā|

hatthipautau hatthicchāpau, bhiṅkau ca kalabhau bhavai||

caja cāgai| cajati| paric‍cajati| cāgau| paric‍cāgau| cajanaṃ| cajaṃ, cajantau| cajamānau|

sanja saṅgai| saṅgau laganaṃ| sañ‍cati| sattau| sajanaṃ, satti| āsatti| sajituṃ| sajitvā|

īja gatiyaṃ| ījati|

bhaji bhaj‍janai| bhaj‍janaṃ tāpakaraṇaṃ| tilāni bhaj‍jati| purisaina bhaj‍jamānāni tilāni|

aija  bhaija bhāja dittiyaṃ| ditti saubhā| aijati| bhaijati| bhājati|

tija nisānai, khamāyañ‍ca| nisānaṃ tikkhatākaraṇaṃ| khamā khanti| taijati| titikkhati| taijanau| taijau| tattha taijanauti kaṇḍau sarau usu| taijauti sūriyau| atha vā taijauti taijanaṃ usmā uṇhattaṃ tāpau| taijauti vā ānubhāvau pabhāvau|

sañ‍ja parissaggai, āliṅganaṃ parissaggau| sañ‍jati|

khaji dānai, gatiyañ‍ca| khañ‍jati| khañ‍janaṃ|

rāja dittiyaṃ bhāja ca| rājati| bhājati| rājā| rājinī| vanarāji| rājitvā| virājitvā| atra viñ‍ñūnamatthavivaraṇai kausal‍lajananatthaṃ silaukaṃ racayāma –

ma’hā’rāja mahārāja, mahārāja mamaiva’hi|

nai’tassa iti vatvāna, dvai janā kalahaṃ karuṃ||

aittha ca paṭhamapādassa dutiyapadai ‘‘mai ahi mahī’’ti chaidau ‘‘puttā mai atthi puttā matthī’’ti viya| ‘‘mahi arāja mahārājā’’ti ca chaidau ‘‘yaupi ayaṃ yaupāya’’nti viya| aittha arājasaddau ‘‘atikaramakarācariyā’’ti aittha akarīti atthavācakau akarasaddau viya ākhyātaparaukkhāvibhattikau daṭṭhabbau| arāja viraucīti atthau| ayaṃ pana gāthāya piṇḍatthau ‘‘mahārāja mai ahi arāja, mama aiva ahi arāja, na aitassa iti vatvā dvai ahituṇḍikajanā kalahaṃ kariṃsū’’ti|

ranja rāgai| bhikkhu cīvaraṃ rajati| sattau rūpādīsu rañ‍jati| rajanaṃ| rajakau| rāgau| virāgau| haliddirāgau| rājā| rājinī| imassa ca divādigaṇaṃ pattassa ‘‘raj‍jati viraj‍jatī’’ti rūpāni  bhavanti| tattha rajananti rajanavatthu| rajakauti rajakārau vatthadhauvanakau| rāgauti raj‍janti sattā taina, sayaṃ vā rañ‍jati, rañ‍janamattamaiva vā aitanti rāgau, taṇhā| imāni pana tadabhidhānāni –

rāgau laubhau tasiṇā ca, taṇhā aijā visattikā|

satti āsatti mucchā ca, lubbhitattañ‍ca lubbhanā||

kāmau nikāmanā icchā, nikanti ca niyanti ca|

vanañ‍ca vanathau caiva, apaikkhā bhavanaitti ca||

anuraudhau ca sārāgau, saṅgau paṅkau ca sibbinī|

nandīrāgau anunayau, gaidhau sañ‍jananī tathā|

janikā paṇidhi caiva, ajjhausānantinaikadhā||

virāgauti maggau nibbānañ‍ca| rājāti pathavissarau| aittha dhātudvayavasaina nibbacanāni niyyantai| nānāsampattīhi rājati dibbati viraucatīti rājā| dānañ‍ca piyavacanañ‍ca atthacariyā ca samānattatā cāti imaihi catūhi saṅgahavatthūhi attani mahājanaṃ rañ‍jaitītipi rājā| rājinīti rājabhariyā| taisaṃ abhidhānāni vuc‍cantai sahābhidhānantaraihi –

rājā bhūpati daivau ca, manujindau disampati|

patthivau jagatipālau, bhūbhujau pathavissarau||

raṭṭhādhipau bhūmipālau, manussindau janādhipau|

narindau khattiyau caiva, khaittassāmi pabhāvakau||

muddhābhisittau rājāti, kathitau itarau pana|

rājañ‍ñau khattiyau cāti, vuttau khattiyajātikau||

muddhābhisittau anurājā, uparājāti bhāsitau|

catuddīpī rājarājā, cak‍kavattīti bhāsitau||

rājinī  uparī daivī, mahaisī bhūbhujaṅganā|

khattiyā rājapadumī, khattiyānī ca khattiyī|

itthāgārantu auraudhau, uparītipi vuc‍cati||

bhaja saivāyaṃ| bhajati| bhajanā| sambhajanā| bhatti| sambhatti| bhattā|

yaja daivapūjasaṅgatakaraṇadānadhammaisu| daivapūjaggahaṇaina buddhādipūjā gahitā| saṅgatakaraṇaṃ samaudhānakaraṇaṃ| tathā hi adhimuttatthairavatthumhi ‘‘yadatthi saṅgataṃ kiñ‍ci, bhavau vā yattha labbhatī’’ti gāthāyaṃ saṅgatasaddaina samaudhānaṃ vuttaṃ| dānaṃ paric‍cāgau| dhammau jhānasīlādi| aitaisvatthaisu yajadhātu vattati| pupphaihi buddhaṃ yajati| daivataṃ yajati| daivamanussaihi bhagavā yajiyati| ij‍jati| yiṭṭhaṃ| yañ‍ñau| yāgau| dhammayāgau| yajamānau sakai purai| yiṭṭhuṃ, yajituṃ| puthuyañ‍ñaṃ yajitvāna| sauḷasaparikkhāraṃ mahāyañ‍ñaṃ kattukāmau|

maj‍ja saṃsuddhiyaṃ| maj‍jati| bāhiraṃ parimaj‍jati| bhūmiṃ sammaj‍jati| maj‍janaṃ| sammaj‍janī|

niñ‍ji suddhiyaṃ| niñ‍jati| paniñ‍jati| niñ‍jituṃ| paniñ‍jituṃ| niñ‍jitvā| paniñ‍jitvā| ayaṃ pana pāḷi ‘‘tatau tvaṃ mauggal‍lāna uṭṭhāyāsanā udakaina akkhīni paniñ‍jitvā disā anulaukaiyyāsī’’ti|

niji abyattasaddai| niñ‍jati|

bhaja pākai| tilāni bhaj‍jati| bhaj‍jamānā tilāni ca|

uju aj‍javai| aj‍javaṃ ujubhāvau| aujati| uju|

saja  vissaggaparissaj‍janabbhuk‍kiraṇaisu| sajati| laukyaṃ sajantaṃ udakaṃ|

ruja bhaṅgai| rujati| rujā| raugau| aittha rujāti byādhi rujanaṭṭhaina| raugauti rujati bhañ‍jati aṅgapac‍caṅgānīti raugau, byādhiyaiva, yau ‘‘ātaṅkau’’tipi ‘‘ābādhau’’tipi vuc‍cati|

bhuja kauṭil‍lai| āvipubbau añ‍ñatthaisu ca| uragau bhujati| ābhujati| bhikkhu pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujati, ūrubaddhāsanaṃ bandhatīti atthau| mahāsamuddau ābhujati, āvaṭṭatīti atthau| kaici pana ‘‘ausak‍katī’’ti atthaṃ vadanti| ‘‘vaṇṇadāna’’nti ābhujati, manasi karautīti atthau| mūlāni vibhujatīti mūlavibhujau, rathau| aittha ca vibhujatīti chindati| bhaugau| bhaugī| ābhaugau| ābhujitvā| aittha ca bhaugauti bhujayati kuṭilaṃ kariyatīti bhaugau, ahisarīraṃ| bhaugīti sappau|

raji vijjhanai| nāgau dantaihi bhūmiṃ rañ‍jati| ārañ‍jati| aittha ca ‘‘tathāgatarañ‍jitaṃ itipī’’ti naittipāḷi nidassanaṃ| tassatthau ‘‘idaṃ sikkhattayasaṅgahitaṃ sāsanabrahmacariyaṃ tathāgatagandhahatthinau mahāvajirañāṇasabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇadantaihi rañ‍jitaṃ ārañ‍jitaṃ, taibhūmakadhammānaṃ ārañ‍janaṭṭhānantipi vuc‍catī’’ti| rañ‍jitanti hi rañ‍jati vijjhati aitthāti ruñ‍jitaṃ, rañ‍janaṭṭhānaṃ| ‘‘idaṃ naisaṃ padak‍kanta’’ntiādimhi viya aitassa saddassa siddhi vaiditabbā adhikaraṇatthasambhavatau|

vijī bhayacalanaisu| īkārantauyaṃ dhātu, tainassa saniggahītāgamāni rūpāni na santi| vaijati| vaigau| dhammasaṃvaigau| saṃvigau vaigaina palāyi| nadīvaigau| ūmivaigau, vātavaigau| aittha dhammasaṃvaigauti sahauttappaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ‘‘vaigau, javau, rayau’’ti imai aikatthā| divādigaṇaṃ pana pattassa ‘‘vij‍jati saṃvij‍jati ubbij‍jatī’’ti rūpāni bhavanti dvigaṇikattā|

laj‍ja laj‍janai| laj‍jati| laj‍jā| laj‍jāti hirī| yā ‘‘viriḷanā’’tipi vuc‍cati|

vaḷaji paribhaugai| vaḷañ‍jati|

kuj‍ja adhaumukhīkaraṇai| kuj‍jati| nikuj‍jati| uk‍kuj‍jati| paṭikuj‍jati| nikuj‍jitaṃ vā uk‍kuj‍jaiyya| añ‍ñissā pātiyā paṭikuj‍jati| avakuj‍jau nipaj‍jahaṃ| tattha kuj‍jati nikuj‍jatīti imāni ‘‘carati vicaratī’’ti padāni viya samānatthāni, adhaumukhaṃ karautīti hi atthau| uk‍kuj‍jatīti uparimukhaṃ karauti| paṭikuj‍jatīti mukhai mukhaṃ ṭhapaiti|

muj‍ja ausīdanai| muj‍jati| nimuj‍jati| nimuggau| ummuggau|

aupuji vilimpanai| gaumayaina pathaviṃ aupuñ‍jati|

jakārantadhāturūpāni|

jhakārantadhātu

jhai cintāyaṃ| jhāyati, nijjhāyati, upanijjhāyati, ujjhāyati, sajjhāyati| jhānaṃ, nijjhānaṃ, upanijjhānaṃ, ujjhāyanaṃ, sajjhāyanaṃ| nijjhatti| upajjhā, upajjhāyau| jhāyī, ajjhāyakau|

tattha jhāyananti duvidhaṃ jhāyanaṃ saubhanamasaubhanañ‍ca| taisu saubhanaṃ ‘‘jhāyī tapati brāhmaṇau| jhāyāmi  akutaubhayau’’tiādīsu daṭṭhabbaṃ| asaubhanaṃ pana ‘‘tattha tattha jhāyantau nisīdi| adhaumukhau pajjhāyantau nisīdī’’tiādīsu daṭṭhabbaṃ| jhāyīti ārammaṇūpanijjhānaina vā lakkhaṇūpanijjhānaina vā jhāyanasīlau cintanasīlau| jhāyī jhānavāti atthau| ajjhāyakauti idaṃ ‘‘na dānimai jhāyanti, na dānimai jhāyantīti khau vāsaiṭṭha ajjhāyakā ajjhāyakātvaiva tatiyaṃ akkharaṃ upanibbatta’’nti aivaṃ paṭhamakappikakālai jhānavirahitānaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ garahavacanaṃ uppan‍naṃ, idāni pana taṃ ajjhāyatīti ajjhāyakau, mantai parivattaitīti iminā atthaina pasaṃsāvacanaṃ katvā vauharantīti| ayaṃ panatthau ‘‘adhipubbassa i ajjhayanai’’ti dhātussa vasaina gahaitabbau| aivaṃ adhipubbassa idhātussa vasaina imassa dhātussa atthaparivattanaṃ bhavati| yaṃ sandhāya ‘‘ajjhāyakau mantadharau’’ti vuttaṃ|

jhai dittiyaṃ| dīpau jhāyati, dārūni jhāyanti| aittha jhāyatīti jalati| jhāyanajalanasaddā hi aikatthā|

jajjha paribhāsanataj‍janaisu| jajjhati|

ujjha ussaggai| ussaggau chaḍḍanaṃ| ujjhati| ujjhitaṃ|

jhakārantadhāturūpāni|

ñakārantadhātu

ñā avabaudhanai| ‘‘ñāti, ñanti, ñāsi| ñātu, ñantu| ñaiyya, ñaiyyu’’ntiādīni yathāpāvacanaṃ gahaitabbāni| ñāti, ñātakau, añ‍ñau, ñattaṃ, ñatti, pañ‍ñatti, viñ‍ñatti, sañ‍ñatti, sañ‍ñā, sañ‍ñāṇaṃ, pañ‍ñā, pañ‍ñāṇaṃ, ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ|

tattha  ñātīti jānāti| puna ñātīti bandhu| sau hi ‘‘ayaṃ amhāka’’nti ñātabbaṭṭhaina ñātīti| aivaṃ ñātakau| añ‍ñauti diṭṭhadhammikādayau atthai na ñāti na jānātīti añ‍ñau, avidvā bālauti atthau| ñattanti jānanabhāvau| ‘‘yāvadaiva anatthāya, ñattaṃ bālassa jāyatī’’ti pāḷi nidassanaṃ| sañ‍ñāṇanti cihanaṃ| kāritai ‘‘ñāpaiti sañ‍ñāpaiti viñ‍ñāpayatī’’tiādīni bhavanti| yasmā pana ‘‘añ‍ñāti paṭivijjhati| attatthaṃ vā paratthaṃ vā ñassati| anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ| aikac‍cai nabbhañ‍ñaṃsu, aikac‍cai abbhañ‍ñaṃsū’’ti pāḷiyau dissanti, tasmā ñātītiādīni ākhyātikapadāni diṭṭhāniyaiva haunti nayavasaina| tathā hi añ‍ñātīti aittha āiti upasaggau, sau parassakkharassa sañ‍ñauguc‍cāraṇicchāya rassaṃ katvā niddiṭṭhau| ñātīti sāsanai ākhyātikapadaṃ diṭṭhaṃ, tasmāyaiva ‘‘ñāti, ñanti| ñāsī’’tiādinā padamālākaraṇai natthaiva dausau|

ñā māraṇatausananisānaisu| māraṇaṃ jīvitindriyupacchaidakaraṇaṃ| tausanaṃ tuṭṭhi| nisānaṃ tikkhatā| ñatti| manuñ‍ñaṃ| pañ‍ñatti|

aittha ñattīti māraitīti vā tausaitīti vā nisaitīti vā atthau| ayañ‍ca ñattisaddau ‘‘vatti aitāyāti vācā’’ti aittha vattisaddau viya ākhyātikapadanti daṭṭhabbau| tathā ādattaiti aittha vibhattibhūtassa taisaddassa viya vibhattibhūtassa tisaddassa sañ‍ñaugabhāvau ca dhātuantassarassa rassattañ‍ca| manuñ‍ñanti manaṃ ābhusau ñaiti tausaitīti manuñ‍ñaṃ| ayamatthau manasaddūpapadassa āpubbassimassa ñādhātussa vasaina daṭṭhabbau| pañ‍ñattīti nānappakāratau pavattinivāraṇaina akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ñatti  māraṇaṃ pañ‍ñatti| atha vā dhammaṃ suṇantānaṃ dhammadaisanāya cittai anaikavidhaina saumanassuppādanaṃ| atikhiṇabuddhīnaṃ anaikavidhaina ñāṇatikhiṇakaraṇañ‍ca pañ‍ñatti nāma, tathā sautūnaṃ cittatausanaina cittanisānaina ca pañ‍ñāpanaṃ pañ‍ñattīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

iti bhūvādigaṇai cavaggantadhāturūpāni

samattāni|

ṭakārantadhātu

idāni ṭavaggantadhāturūpāni vuc‍cantai –

sauṭu gabbai| gabbaṃ dabbanaṃ| sauṭati|

yauṭu sambandhai| yauṭati|

maiṭu milaiṭu ummādai| maiṭati| milaiṭati|

kaṭa vassāvaraṇaisu| kaṭati|

saṭa paribhāsanai| saṭati|

laṭa bālyai ca| pubbāpaikkhāya cakārau| laṭati| lāṭau|

saṭa rujāvisaraṇagatyāvasānaisu| rujā pīḷā| visaraṇaṃ vippharaṇaṃ| gatyāvasānaṃ gatiyā avasānaṃ ausānaṃ abhāvakaraṇaṃ, nisīdananti vuttaṃ hauti| saṭati| sāṭau vuc‍cati sāṭakau|

vaṭa vaidhanai| vaṭati| vaṭau| vāṭau|

khiṭa uttāsanai| khaiṭati, ākhaiṭakau, khaiṭau, ukkhaiṭitau, samukkhaiṭitau|

siṭa anādarai| saiṭati|

jaṭa  ghaṭa saṅghātai| jaṭati| jaṭā, jaṭilau, jaṭī| antaujaṭā bahijaṭā, jaṭāya jaṭitā pajā| kāritai ‘‘sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭaṃ| arahattamaggakkhaṇai vijaṭaiti nāmā’’ti payaugau|

bhaṭa bhattiyaṃ| bhaṭati| bhaṭau| vaitanaṃ bhaṭakau yathā|

taṭa ussayai| ussayau ārauhau ubbaidhau| taṭati| taṭau, giritaṭau, nadītaṭau, taṭī, taṭaṃ|

khaṭa kaṃsai| khaṭati| khaṭau|

naṭa natiyaṃ| naṭati| naṭau, nāṭakaṃ|

piṭa saddasaṅghāṭaisu| paiṭati| paiṭakau, piṭakaṃ| piṭakasaddau ‘‘mā piṭakasampadānainā’’tiādīsu pariyattiyaṃ dissati| ‘‘atha purisau āgacchaiyya kudālapiṭakaṃ ādāyā’’tiādīsu yasmiṃ kismiñ‍ci bhājanai|

haṭa dittiyaṃ| haṭati| hāṭakaṃ, haṭakaṃ| yaṃ jātarūpaṃ haṭakanti vuc‍cati|

saṭa avayavai| saṭati|

luṭa vilauṭhanai| lauṭati|

ciṭa paisanai| caiṭati| caiṭakau|

viṭa saddai| vaiṭati| vaiṭakau|

aṭa paṭa iṭa kiṭa kaṭa gatiyaṃ| aṭati| paṭati| aiṭati| kaiṭati| kaṭati| paṭau ic‍caiva nāmikapadaṃ diṭṭhaṃ| paṭati jiṇṇabhāvaṃ  gacchatīti paṭau| paṭauti vatthaṃ| vatthassa hi anaikāni nāmāni –

paṭau cauḷau sāṭakau ca, vāsau vasanamaṃsukaṃ|

dussamacchādanaṃ vatthaṃ, cailaṃ vasani ambaraṃ||

muṭa pamaddanai| mauṭati|

cuṭa appībhāvai| cauṭati|

vaṭi vibhājanai| vaṭati| vaṇṭau|

ruṭi luṭi thaiyyai| ruṇṭati| luṇṭati| ruṇṭakau| luṇṭakau|

phuṭa visaraṇai phauṭati| phauṭau|

caiṭa caiṭāyaṃ| caiṭati| caiṭakau|

ghuṭa parivattanai| ghauṭati|

ruṭa luṭa paṭighātai| rauṭati| lauṭati|

ghaṭa caitāyaṃ| ghaṭati| ghaṭau| ghaṭau vuc‍cati kumbhau| imāni tadabhidhānāni –

ghaṭau kumbhau ghaṭī kumbhī, tuṇḍikirau tu ukkhalī|

mahantabhājanaṃ cāṭi, atikhuddaṃ kuṭṭaṃ bhavai|

caṭa bhaṭa paribhāsanai daiṭa ca| caṭati| bhaṭati| daiṭati|

kuṭa kauṭil‍lai| kuṭati| paṭikuṭati|

puṭa saṃkilaisanai| puṭati|

cuṭa chuṭa kuṭa chaidanai| cuṭati| chuṭati| kuṭati|

phuṭa vikasanai| phuṭati|

muṭa aggisaddapakkhaipamaddanaisu| muṭati|

tuṭa kalahakammani| tuṭati|

ghuṭa paṭighātai| ghuṭati| ghauṭakau|

ṭakārantadhāturūpāni|

ṭhakārantadhātu

ṭhā  gatinivattiyaṃ| gatinivatti uppaj‍jamānassa gamanassupacchaidau| ṭhāti, ṭhanti| tiṭṭhati| patiṭṭhāti| adhiṭṭhāti| adhiṭṭhaiti| saṇṭhāti| saṇṭhahati| adhiṭṭhahati| upaṭṭhahati| ṭhātu| tiṭṭhatu| tiṭṭhaiyya| aṭṭha, aṭṭhu| aṭṭhā, aṭṭhū, aṭṭhāsi, aṭṭhaṃsu| yāvassa kāyau ṭhassati| tiṭṭhissati| upassuti tiṭṭhissatha| aṭṭhissā, aṭṭhissaṃsu| atiṭṭhissā, atiṭṭhissaṃsu| ṭhātuṃ, upaṭṭhātuṃ| upaṭṭhahituṃ| adhiṭṭhātuṃ| adhiṭṭhahituṃ| ṭhatvā, adhiṭṭhitvā| upaṭṭhahitvā, adhiṭṭhahitvā| ṭhānaṃ, ṭhiti, saṇṭhiti, avaṭṭhiti| saṇṭhānaṃ, paṭṭhānaṃ| upaṭṭhākau, ṭhitau| pabbataṭṭhau bhūmaṭṭhau| upaṭṭhahaṃ ic‍cādīni|

tattha ṭhānasaddau issariyaṭhitikhaṇakāraṇaisu dissati| ‘‘kiṃ panāyasmā daivānamindau kammaṃ katvā imaṃ ṭhānaṃ pattau’’tiādīsu hi issariyai dissati| ‘‘ṭhānakusalau hauti akkhaṇavaidhī’’tiādīsu ṭhitiyaṃ| ‘‘ṭhānasaupaitaṃ tathāgataṃ paṭibhātī’’tiādīsu khaṇai| ‘‘ṭhānañ‍ca ṭhānasau ñatvā aṭṭhānañ‍ca aṭṭhānasau’’tiādīsu kāraṇai| kāraṇañhi yasmā tattha phalaṃ tiṭṭhati tadāyattavuttibhāvaina, tasmā ṭhānanti vuc‍cati|

issariyai ṭhitiyañ‍ca, khaṇasmimpi ca kāraṇai|

catūsvatthaisu aitaisu, ṭhānasaddau pavattatīti||

ṭhai saddasaṅkātaisu| ṭhīyati|

ṭhai vaiṭhanai| ṭhāyati|

paṭha  viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ| dhammaṃ paṭhati| pāṭhau, nakkhattapāṭhakau, sau haurapāṭhakaṃ pucchi| sabbapāṭhī bhavissati| paṭhituṃ, paṭhitavai, paṭhitvā, paṭhitvāna, paṭhituna, paṭhiya, paṭhiyāna|

aivaṃvidhaṃ tuṃpac‍cayantādivibhāgaṃ sabbattha yathārahaṃ vattukāmāpi gantha vitthārabhayaina na vadāma| avuttaupi īdisau vibhāgau nayānusāraina yathāsambhavaṃ sabbattha yaujaitabbau| yattha pana pāḷinidassanādivisaisau icchitabbau hauti, tatthaivaitaṃ dassaissāma|

vaṭha thūliyai| vaṭhati| vaṭharau| vaṭharauti thūlaghanasarīrasmiṃ vattabbavacanaṃ| tathā hi vinayaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘vaṭharauti thūlau, thūlau ca ghanasarīrau cāyaṃ bhikkhūti vuttaṃ hautī’’ti vuttaṃ|

maṭha nivāsai| maṭhati| maṭhau|

kaṭha kicchajīvanai| kaṭhati| kaṭhau|

raṭha paribhāsanai| raṭhati|

sāṭha balak‍kārai| balak‍kārau nāma attanau balaina yathājjhāsayaṃ dabbalassa abhibhavanaṃ| sāṭhati| sāṭhau|

uṭha ruṭha luṭha upaghātai| auṭhati| rauṭhati| lauṭhati|

piṭha hiṃsāsaṃkilaisaisu| paiṭhati| piṭharau|

saṭha kaitavai ca| pubbatthaisu cakārau| saṭhati| saṭhau saṭhauti kairāṭikau vuc‍cati|

suṭha gatipaṭighātai| gamanapaṭihananaṃ gatipaṭighātau| sauṭhati|

kuṭhi luṭhi ālassiyai ca| cakārau pubbatthai ca| kuṇṭhati| kuṇṭhau| luṇṭhati| luṇṭhau|

suṭhi  sausanai| suṇṭhati|

ruṭhi luṭhi aṭhi gatiyaṃ| ruṇṭhati| luṇṭhati| aṇṭhati|

vaiṭha vaiṭhanai| vaiṭhati, nibbaiṭhati| vaiṭhanaṃ, nibbaiṭhanaṃ|

vaṭhi aikacariyāyaṃ| vaṇṭhati|

maṭha kuṭhi saukai| maṭhati| kuṇṭhati|

aiṭha haiṭha vibādhāyaṃ| aiṭhati| haiṭhati| vihaiṭhati| vihaiṭhanaṃ|

luṭha paṭighātai| lauṭhati|

paṭha vikhyānai| paṭhati|

luṭha saṃkilaisai| lauṭhati|

ṭhakārantadhāturūpāni|

ḍakārantadhātu

ḍi vihāyasagatiyaṃ gamanamattai ca| ḍaiti, ḍayati| ḍaimānau| uc‍cai sakuṇa ḍaimāna| yai maṃ purai pac‍cuḍḍainti|

ḍi khipanuḍḍanaisu| ḍaiti| uḍḍaiti|

itau bahiddhā pāsaṇḍā, diṭṭhīsu pasīdanti tai|

na taisaṃ dhammaṃ raucaimi, na tai dhammassa kauvidā||

aittha ca pāsaṇḍāti pāsaṃ ḍaintīti pāsaṇḍā, sattānaṃ cittaisu diṭṭhipāsaṃ khipantīti atthau| atha vā taṇhāpāsaṃ diṭṭhipāsañ‍ca ḍainti uḍḍaintīti pāsaṇḍā|

muḍi  kaṇḍanai| muṇḍati| kumāraṃ muṇḍiṃsu| muṇḍau|

cuḍḍa hāvakaraṇai| cuḍḍati|

aḍḍa abhiyaugai| aḍḍati|

gaḍi vadanaikadaisai| gaṇḍati| gaṇḍau|

huḍi piḍi saṅghātai| huṇḍati| piṇḍati| piṇḍau|

hiḍi gatiyaṃ| hiṇḍati, āhiṇḍati|

kuḍi dāhai| kuṇḍati| kuṇḍau|

vaḍi maḍi vaiṭhanai| vaṇḍati| maṇḍati| maṇḍalaṃ|

bhaḍi paribhāsanai| bhaṇḍati| bhaṇḍanaṃ| bhaṇḍau|

maḍi maj‍janai| maṇḍati| maṇḍanaṃ|

tuḍi tauḷanai| tuṇḍati| tuṇḍau| tuṇḍainādāya gacchaiyya|

bhuḍi bharaṇai| bhuṇḍati|

caḍi kaupai| caṇḍati| caṇḍau| caṇḍālau, caṇḍik‍kaṃ|

saḍi rujāyaṃ| saṇḍati| saṇḍau|

taḍi tāḷanai| taṇḍati| vitaṇḍā|

paḍi gatiyaṃ| paṇḍati| paṇḍā, paṇḍitau| aittha paṇḍāti pañ‍ñā| sā hi sukhumaisupi atthaisu paṇḍati gacchati dukkhādīnaṃ pīḷanādikampi ākāraṃ jānātīti ‘‘paṇḍā’’ti vuc‍cati| paṇḍitauti paṇḍāya itau gatau pavattauti paṇḍitau| atha vā sañ‍jātā paṇḍā aitassāti paṇḍitau| paṇḍati ñāṇagatiyā gacchatītipi paṇḍitau| tathā hi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ  vuttaṃ ‘‘paṇḍantīti paṇḍitā| sandiṭṭhikasamparāyikatthaisu ñāṇagatiyā gacchantīti atthau’’ti|

gaḍi madai| gaṇḍati|

khaḍi manthai| khaṇḍati| khaṇḍitau, khaṇḍau|

laḍi jivhāmathanai| laṇḍati| laṇḍau|

ḍakārantadhāturūpāni|

ḍakārantadhātu

vaḍḍha vaḍḍhanai| vaḍḍhati| sirivaḍḍhakau, dhanavaḍḍhakau, vaḍḍhitau, buḍḍhau| aittha ca vakārassa bakārau, akārassa cukārau|

kaḍḍha ākaḍḍhanai| kaḍḍhati, ākaḍḍhati, nikaḍḍhati| akāmā parikaḍḍhanti, ulūkañ‍ñaiva vāyasā|

imāni ḍhakārantadhāturūpāni|

ṇakārantadhātu

aṇa raṇa vaṇa bhaṇa maṇa kaṇa saddai| aṇati| aṇakau brāhmaṇau| raṇati| raṇaṃ| vaṇati| vāṇakau| bhaṇati| bhāṇakau| maṇati| maṇikau| kaṇati| kāṇau| tattha brāhmaṇauti brahmaṃ aṇatīti brāhmaṇau, mantai sajjhāyatīti atthau| akkharacintakā pana ‘‘brahmunau apac‍caṃ brāhmaṇau’’ti vadanti| ariyā pana bāhitapāpattā brāhmaṇauti|

brāhmaṇau  sauttiyau vippau, bhauvādī brahmabandhu ca|

brahmasūnu dvijau brahmā, kamalāsanasūnu ca|

raṇasaddau ‘‘saraṇā dhammā araṇā dhammā’’tiādīsu kilaisaisu vattati| kilaisā hi raṇanti kandanti aitaihīti raṇāti vuc‍canti|

‘‘dhanuggahau asadisau, rājaputtau mahabbalau|

sabbāmittai raṇaṃ katvā, saṃyamaṃ ajjhupāgamī’’ti

aittha yuddhai vattati| raṇaṃ katvāti hi yuddhaṃ katvāti atthau| ‘‘tiṇañ‍ca kaṭṭhañ‍ca raṇaṃ karauntā, dhāviṃsu tai aṭṭhadisā samantatau’’ti aittha cuṇṇavicuṇṇakaraṇai vattati| raṇaṃ karauntāti hi cuṇṇavicuṇṇaṃ karauntāti atthau| aivaṃ atthavivaraṇampi saddasaṅkhātamatthaṃ antauyaiva katvā adhippāyatthavasaina kataṃ, na dhātunānatthavasaināti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā dhātūnamatthātisayayaugaupi bhavati, taina aivaṃ atthavivaraṇaṃ katantipi daṭṭhabbaṃ|

bhaṇa bhaṇanai| parittaṃ bhaṇati, vacanaṃ bhaṇati| dīghabhāṇakau, piyabhāṇī, bhāṇavārau| aittha bhāṇavārauti –

‘‘aṭṭhakkharā aikapadaṃ, aikā gāthā catuppadaṃ|

gāthā caikāmatau ganthau, ganthau bāttiṃsatakkharau|

bāttiṃsakkharaganthānaṃ, pañ‍ñāsaṃ dvisataṃ pana|

bhāṇavārau matau aikau, svaṭṭhakkharasahassakau’’ti||

aivaṃ aṭṭhakkharasahassaparimāṇau pāṭhau vuc‍cati|

auṇaṃ apanayanai| auṇati|

sauṇa vaṇṇagatīsu| sauṇati, sauṇau|

sauṇa  silauṇa saṅghātai| sauṇati| silauṇati|

ghiṇi ghuṇi ghaṇi gahaṇai| ghiṇṇati| ghuṇṇati| ghaṇṇati|

ghuṇa ghuṇṇa gamanai| ghauṇati| ghuṇṇati|

paṇa byavahārai, thutiyañ‍ca| paṇati vāṇijau, vauhāraṃ karauti ic‍catthau| saddhau buddhaṃ paṇati, thaumayati ic‍catthau, āpaṇaṃ, sāpaṇau gāmau|

gaṇa raṇa gatiyaṃ| gaṇati| raṇati|

caṇa saṇa dānai| caṇati| saṇati|

phaṇa gatiyaṃ| phaṇati| phaṇaṃ|

vaiṇu ñāṇacintānisāmanaisu| vaiṇati|

pīṇa pīṇanai| pīṇanaṃ paripuṇṇatā| pīṇati| pīṇau divā na bhuñ‍jati, pīṇaurakkhaṃsabāhu|

miṇa hiṃsāyaṃ| miṇati|

duṇa gatiyañ‍ca| hiṃsāpaikkhakau cakārau| duṇati|

saṇa abyattasaddai| saṇati| saṇataiva brahmārañ‍ñaṃ| saṇataivāti nadati viya|

tuṇa kauṭil‍lai| tauṇati|

puṇa nipuṇai| puṇati, nipuṇati| nipuṇadhammau| aittha ca nipuṇasaṇhasukhumasaddā vaivacanasaddā kusalachaikadakkhasaddā viyāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

muṇa paṭiñ‍ñāṇai| muṇati|

kuṇa saddaupakaraṇai| kauṇati|

cuṇa  chaidanai| cauṇati|

maṇa cāgai| vairaṃ maṇatīti vairamaṇi|

phuṇa vikiraṇai vidhunanai ca| phuṇati| aṅgārakāsuṃ aparai phuṇanti|

imāni ṇakārantadhāturūpāni|

iti bhūvādigaṇai ṭavaggantadhāturūpāni

samattāni|

takārantadhātu

atha tavaggantadhāturūpāni vuc‍cantai –

tai pālanai| pālanaṃ rakkhaṇaṃ| tāyati| tāṇaṃ, gauttaṃ, nakkhattaṃ| aghassa tātā| kicchainādhigatā bhaugā, tai tātau vidhami dhamaṃ| tattha gauttanti gaṃ tāyatīti gauttaṃ| ‘‘gautamau kassapau’’ti hi ādinā pavattamānaṃ gaṃ vacanaṃ buddhiñ‍ca tāyati aikaṃsikavisayatāya rakkhatīti gauttaṃ| yathā hi buddhi ārammaṇabhūtaina atthaina vinā na vattati, tathā abhidhānaṃ abhidhaiyyabhūtaina, tasmā sau gauttasaṅkhātau atthau tāni tāyati rakkhatīti vuc‍cati| kau pana sauti? añ‍ñakulaparamparāsādhāraṇaṃ tassa kulassa ādipurisasammuditaṃ taṃkulapariyāpan‍nasādhāraṇaṃ sāmañ‍ñarūpaṃ|

nakkhattanti visamagatiyā agantvā attanau vīthiyāva gamanaina nakkhanaṃ gamanaṃ tāyati rakkhatīti nakkhattaṃ, taṃ pana assayujādivasaina sattavīsatividhaṃ hauti| tathā hi assayujau  bharaṇī kattikā rauhaṇī migasirau addā punabbasu phussau assalisau māghau pubbaphagguṇī uttaraphagguṇī hatthau cittaṃ svāti visākhā anurādhā jaiṭṭhā mūlaṃ pubbāsaḷhaṃ uttarāsaḷhaṃ sāvaṇaṃ dhanasiṭṭhā satabhisattaṃ pubbabhaddapadaṃ uttarabhaddapadaṃ raivatī cāti sattavīsati nakkhattāni| tāni pana attanau gamanaṭṭhānaṃ īsakampi na vijahanti kiñ‍ci sīghaṃ kiñ‍ci dandhaṃ, kadāci sīghaṃ, kadāci dandhaṃ, aittau itau cāti aivaṃ visamagatiyā agantvā yantacak‍kai paṭipāṭiyā yaujitāni viya samappamāṇagatiyā attanau vīthiyāva gacchantāni maṇḍalākāraina sinairuṃ parivattanti| aivaṃ imāni nakkhanaṃ gamanaṃ tāyanti rakkhantīti nakkhattānīti vuc‍canti| paurāṇā pana kharadhātuvasaina ‘‘nakkharanti na nassantīti nakkhattānī’’ti āvaucuṃ, ‘‘nakkhattaṃ jauti rikkhaṃ taṃ’’ ic‍caitāni nakkhattatārakānaṃ nāmāni| ‘‘uḷu tārā tārakā’’ti imāni pana sabbāsampi tārakānaṃ sādhāraṇanāmāni| ausadhīti pana tārakāvisaisassa nāmaṃ|

citi sañ‍ñāṇai| sañ‍ñāṇaṃ cihanaṃ lakkhaṇakaraṇaṃ| caitati| cihanaṃ karautīti atthau| īkārantavasaina vuttattā asmā dhātutau saki saṅkāyanti dhātutau viya niggahītāgamau na hauti| aisa nayau añ‍ñaisupi īdisaisu ṭhānaisu|

pata gatiyaṃ| patati| papatati papātaṃ, papataiyyahaṃ| pāpattaṃ nirayaṃ bhusaṃ| ahaṃsaddaina yaujaitabbaṃ, pāpattaṃ papatitausmīti atthau| pāpattha nirayaṃ bhusaṃ, saukumārauti yaujaitabbaṃ, pāpattha papatitauti atthau| paraukkhāpadañhiaitaṃ dvayaṃ| ‘‘pāvadaṃ pāvadā’’tiādīsu viya upasaggapadassa dīghabhāvau, tatau aṃsaddassa ttaṃādaisau, asaddassa ca tthādaisau bhavati| acintaiyyau hi pāḷinayau|

ata  sātac‍cagamanai| sātac‍cagamanaṃ nirantaragamanaṃ| atati| yasmā pana atadhātu sātac‍cagamanatthavācikā, tasmā bhavābhavaṃ dhāvantau jātijarābyādhimaraṇādibhaidaṃ anaikavihitaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ atati satataṃ gacchati pāpuṇāti adhigacchatīti attātipi nibbacanamicchitabbaṃ| atthantaravasaina pana ‘‘āhitau ahaṃmānau aitthāti attā, attabhāvau’’ti ca ‘‘sukhadukkhaṃ adati anubhavatīti attā’’ti ca ‘‘attamanauti pītisaumanassaina gahitamanau’’ti ca atthau daṭṭhabbau, yattha yattha yathā yathā atthau labbhati, tattha tattha tathā tathā atthassa gahaitabbatauti|

cuta āsaicanai kharaṇai ca| cautati|

ati bandhanai| antati| antaṃ| antiyatibandhiyati antaguṇaināti antaṃ| idha anta saddassa atthuddhārau vuc‍catai ‘‘antaṃ antaguṇaṃ udariya’’nti aittha dvattiṃsākārantaugadhaṃ kuṇapantaṃ antaṃ nāma| ‘‘kāyabandhanassa antau jīrati| haritantaṃ vā’’ti aittha antimamariyādantau antau nāma| ‘‘antamidaṃ bhikkhavai jīvikāna’’nti aittha lāmakantau| ‘‘sak‍kāyau aikau antau’’ti aittha kauṭṭhāsantau| ‘‘aisaivantau dukkhassa sappac‍cayasaṅkhayā’’ti aittha kauṭantau| ic‍caivaṃ –

kuṇapantaṃ  antimañ‍ca, mariyādau ca lāmakaṃ|

kauṭṭhāsau kauṭi’mai atthau, antasaddaina bhāsitā||

kita nivāsai raugāpanayanai ca| kaitati| sākaitaṃ na garaṃ, nikaitau, nikaitaṃ pāvisi| āmaudamānau gacchati san‍nikaitaṃ| tikicchati, cikicchati, cikicchā, cikicchakau| tattha sākaitanti sāyaṃ gahitavasanaṭṭhānattā sākaitaṃ, yaṃsaddalaupau|

yata patiyatanai| patiyatanaṃ vāyāmakaraṇaṃ| yatati| yati, yatavā, payatanaṃ, āyatanaṃ, laukāyataṃ| aittha āyatananti āyatanatau āyatanaṃ, cakkhurūpādīni| aitāni hi taṃdvārārammaṇacittacaitasikā dhammā saina saina anubhavanādikic‍caina āyatanti uṭṭhahanti ghaṭanti vāyamanti aitaisūti ‘‘āyatanānī’’ti vuc‍canti| aittha pana nītanudhātūnaṃ vasainapi āyatanasaddatthau vattabbau siyā, sau uttari āvibhavissati|

āyatanasaddau nivāsaṭṭhānai ākarai samausaraṇaṭṭhānai sañ‍jātidaisai kāraṇai ca| tathā hi ‘‘laukai issarāyatanaṃ vāsudaivāyatana’’ntiādīsu nivāsaṭṭhānai āyatanasaddau vattati| ‘‘suvaṇṇāyatanaṃ rajatāyatana’’ntiādīsu ākarai| sāsanai pana ‘‘manauramai āyatanai, saivanti naṃ vihaṅgamā’’tiādīsu samausaraṇaṭṭhānai| ‘‘dakkhiṇāpathau gun‍naṃ āyatana’’ntiādīsu sañ‍jātidaisai| ‘‘tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati satiāyatanai’’tiādīsu kāraṇai vattatīti  vaiditabbau| sau ca nānāpavattinimittavasaina gahaitabbau|

nivāsai ākārai caiva, jātidaisai ca kāraṇai|

samausaraṇaṭṭhānai ca, āyatanaravau gatau||
