phakārantadhāturūpāni|

bakārantadhātu

bhabba  hiṃsāyaṃ| bhabbati| bhabbau|

pabba vabba mabba kabba khabba gabba sabba cabba gatiyaṃ| pabbati| vabbati| mabbati| kabbati| khabbati| gabbati| sabbati| cabbati|

abba sabba hiṃsāyañ‍ca| gatyāpaikkhāya cakārau| abbati| sabbati|

kubi acchādanai| kubbati|

lubi tubi addanai| lumbati| tumbati| lumbinīvanaṃ| udakatumbau| athaupi dvai ca tumbāni|

cubi vadanasaṃyaugai| puttaṃ muddhani cumbati| mukhai cumbati| aittha siyā ‘‘yadi vadanasaṃyaugai cubidhātu vattati, kathaṃ ambudharabinducumbitakūṭauti aittha avadanai aviñ‍ñāṇakai pabbatakūṭai ambudharabindūnaṃ cumbanaṃ vutta’’nti? sac‍caṃ, taṃ pana cumbanākārasadisainākāraina sambhavaṃ caitasi ṭhapaitvā vuttaṃ, yathā adassanasambhavaipi dassanasadisainākāraina sambhūtattā ‘‘raudantai dārakai disvā, ubbiddhā vipulā dumā’’ti acakkhukānampi rukkhānaṃ dassanaṃ vuttaṃ, aivamidhāpi cumbanākārasadisainākāraina sambhūtattā avadanānampi ambudharabindūnaṃ cumbanaṃ vuttaṃ| sabhāvatau pana aviñ‍ñāṇakānaṃ dassanacumbanādīni ca natthi, saviñ‍ñāṇakānaṃyaiva tāni hauntīti| ayaṃ nayau kamu padavikkhaipaitiādīsupi naitabbau|

ubbi tubbi thubbi dubbi dhubbi hiṃsatthā| ubbati| tubbati| thubbati| dubbati| dubbā| dhubbati| aittha dubbāti dabbatiṇaṃ, yaṃ ‘‘tiriyā nāma tiṇajātī’’ti pāḷiyaṃ āgataṃ| aittha ca dubbāti itthiliṅgaṃ, dabbanti napuṃsakaliṅganti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

mubbi  bandhanai| mubbati|

kubbi uggamai| kubbati|

pubba pabba sabba pūraṇai| pubbati| pabbati| sabbati| aitta siyā ‘‘nanu bhau pubbasabbasaddā sabbanāmāni, kasmā panaitai dhātucintāyaṃ gahitā’’ti? vuc‍catai – sabbanāmaisu ca tumantādivirahitaisu ca nipātaisu upasaggaisu ca dhātucintā nāma natthi, imāni pana sabbanāmāni na haunti| kaivalaṃ sutisāmañ‍ñaina sabbanāmāni viya upaṭṭhahanti, taina tai tabbhāvamuttattā dhātucintāyaṃ pubbācariyaihi gahitā ‘‘pubbati sabbatī’’ti payaugadassanatauti| yadi aivaṃ kasmā buddhavacanai aitāni rūpāni na santīti? anāgamanabhāvaina na santi, na avij‍jamānabhāvaina| kiñ‍cāpi buddhavacanaisu aitāni rūpāni na santi, tathāpi paurāṇaihi anumatā purāṇabhāsāti gahaitabbāni, yathā ‘‘nāthatīti nāthau’’ti aittha ‘‘nāthatī’’ti rūpaṃ buddhavacanai avij‍jamānampi gahaitabbaṃ hauti, aivaṃ imānipi| tasmā vauhāraisu viñ‍ñūnaṃ kausal‍latthāya sāsanai avij‍jamānāpi sāsanānurūpā laukikappayaugā gahaitabbāti ‘‘pubbati sabbatī’’ti rūpāni gahitāni| aisa nayau añ‍ñaisupi ṭhānaisu vaiditabbau|

camba adanai| cambati|

kabba khabba gabba dabbai| dabbau ahaṅkārau| kabbati| khabbati| gabbati|

abi dabi saddai| ambati| ambā, ambu| dambati|

labi avasaṃsanai| avasaṃsanaṃ avalambanaṃ| lambati, vilambati, byālambati| nīcai cau’lambatai sūriyau| ālambati| ālambanaṃ, tadālambanaṃ, tadālambaṇaṃ, tadālambaṃ vā| lābu| alābu vā, akārau hi tabbhāvai|

bakārantadhāturūpāni|

bhakārantadhātu

bhā dittiyaṃ| candau bhāti, pañhā maṃ paṭibhāti| ratti vibhāti| bhāṇu, paṭibhānaṃ| vibhātā ratti|

bhī bhayai| bhāyati| bhayaṃ, bhayānakau, bhīmau, bhīmasainau, bhīru, bhīrukau, bhīrukajātikau| kāritai ‘‘bhāyaiti, bhāyayati, bhāyāpaiti, bhāyāpayatī’’ti rūpāni|

sabhu sambhu hiṃsāyaṃ| sabhati| sambhati|

sumbha bhāsanai ca| cakārau hiṃsāpaikkhakau| sumbhati| sumbhau| kusumbhau|

aittha sumbhauti āvāṭau| ‘‘sumbhaṃ nikkhanāhī’’ti idamaittha nidassanaṃ| kusumbhauti khuddakaāvāṭau, ‘‘pabbatakandarapadarasākhāparipūrā kusumbhai paripūraintī’’ti idamaittha nidassanaṃ|

abbha vabbha mabbha gatiyaṃ| abbhati| abbhau| vabbhati| mabbhati|

aittha abbhauti maighau| sau hi abbhati anaikasatapaṭalau hutvā gacchatīti ‘‘abbhau’’ti vuc‍cati| ‘‘vij‍jumālī satak‍kakū’’ti vuttaṃ| satak‍kakūti ca anaikasatapaṭalau| aittha ca abbhasaddau tiliṅgikau daṭṭhabbau| tathā  hi ayaṃ ‘‘abbhuṭṭhitauva sa yāti, sa gacchaṃ na nivattatī’’ti aittha pul‍liṅgau| ‘‘abbhā, mahikā, dhūmau, rajau, rāhū’’ti aittha itthiliṅgau| ‘‘abbhāni candamaṇḍalaṃ chādaintī’’ti aittha napuṃsakaliṅgau|

imāni pana maighassa nāmāni –

maighau valāhakau laṅghi, jīmūtau ambudau ghanau|

dhārādharau ambudharau, paj‍jun‍nau himagabbhakau||

yabha maithunai| mithunassa janadvayassa idaṃ kammaṃ maithūnaṃ, tasmiṃ maithunai yabhadhātu vattati| yabhati| yābhassaṃ|

aittha ca ‘‘maithuna’’nti aisā sabbhivācā, laj‍jāsampan‍naihi puggalaihi vattabbabhāsābhāvatau| tathā hi ‘‘maithunau dhammau na paṭisaivitabbau’’ti ca ‘‘na mai rājā sakhā hauti, na rājā hauti maithunau’’ti ca saubhanai vācāvisayai ayaṃ bhāsā āgatā| ‘‘yabhatī’’tiādikā pana bhāsā ‘‘sikharaṇī’’tiādikā bhāsā viya asambhivācā| na hi hirauttappasampan‍nau laukiyajanaupi īdisiṃ vācaṃ bhāsati| aivaṃ santaipi adhimattuk‍kaṃsagatahirauttappaupi bhagavā mahākaruṇāya sañ‍cauditahadayau laukānukampāya parisamajjhai abhāsi| ahau tathāgatassa mahākaruṇāti|

imāni pana maithunadhammassa nāmāni –

saṃvaisanaṃ niddhuvanaṃ, maithunaṃ sūrataṃ rataṃ|

byathayau gāmadhammau ca, yābhassaṃ mauhanaṃ rati||

asaddhammau ca vasala-dhammau mīḷhasukhampi ca|

dvayaṃdvayasamāpatti, dvandau gammau’dakantikau||

sibha vibha katthanai| sibhati| vibhati|

daibha  abhi dabhi saddai| daibhati| ambhati| ambhau| dambhati|

aittha ca ambhau vuc‍cati udakaṃ| tañhi nij‍jīvampi samānaṃ aughakālādīsu vissandamānaṃ ambhati saddaṃ karautīti ambhauti vuc‍cati|

imānissa nāmāni –

pānīyaṃ udakaṃ tauyaṃ, jalaṃ pātau ca ambu ca|

dakaṃ kaṃ salilaṃ vāri, āpau ambhau papampi ca||

nīrañ‍ca kaipukaṃ pāni, amataṃ ailamaiva ca,

āpaunāmāni aitāni, āgatāni tatau tatau||

aittha ca ‘‘vālaggaisu ca kaipukai| pivitañ‍ca taisaṃ bhusaṃ hauti pānī’’tiādayau payaugā dassaitabbā|

thabhi khabhi paṭibaddhai thambhati, vitthambhati| khambhati, vikkhambhati| thambhau| thaddhau, upatthambhau| upatthambhinī| vikkhambhau| vikkhambhitakilaisau|

jabha jabhi gattavināmai| jabhati| jambhati, vijambhati| vijambhanaṃ, vijambhitā| vijambhantau, vijambhamānau, vijambhitau|

sabbha kathanai| sabbhati|

vabbha bhaujanai| vabbhati|

gabbha dhāraṇai| gabbhati| gabbhau|

aittha gabbhauti mātukucchipi vuc‍cati kucchigataputtaupi| tathā hi ‘‘yamaikarattiṃ paṭhamaṃ, gabbhai vasati māṇavau’’ti aittha mātukucchi ‘‘gabbhau’’ti vuc‍cati| ‘‘gabbhau mai daiva patiṭṭhitau| gabbhau ca patitau chamā’’ti ca aittha pana kucchigataputtau| apica gabbhauti āvāsavisaisau| ‘‘gabbhaṃ  paviṭṭhau’’tiādīsu hi auvarakau ‘‘gabbhau’’ti vuc‍cati|

rabha rābhassai| āpubbau rabha hiṃsākaraṇavāyamanaisu| rābhassaṃ rābhasabhāvau| taṃsamaṅginau pana pāḷiyaṃ ‘‘caṇḍā ruddhā rabhasā’’ti aivaṃ āgatā|

tattha rabhasāti karaṇuttariyā| rabhati, ārabhati, samārabhati, ārambhati| rabhasau| ārambhau| samārambhau, ārabhantau| samārabhantau| āraddhaṃ mai vīriyaṃ| sārambhaṃ| anārambhaṃ| sārambhau tai na vij‍jati| pakāraṇārambhau| vīriyārambhau| ārabhituṃ| ārabhitvā| ārabbha|

aittha vīriyārambhauti vīriyasaṅkhātau ārambhau| ārambhasaddau kammai āpattiyaṃ kriyāya vīriyai hiṃsāya vikaupanaiti anaikaisu atthaisu āgatau|

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhaṃ sambhaubhi, sabbaṃ ārambhapac‍cayā|

ārambhānaṃ niraudhaina, natthi dukkhassa sambhavau’’ti

aittha hi kammaṃ ārambhauti āgataṃ| ‘‘ārambhati ca vippaṭisārī ca hautī’’ti aittha āpatti| ‘‘mahāyañ‍ñā mahārambhā, na tai haunti mahapphal‍lā’’ti aittha yūpussāpanādikriyā| ‘‘ārambhatha nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai’’ti aittha vīriyaṃ| ‘‘samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ uddissa pāṇaṃ ārambhantī’’ti aittha hiṃsā| ‘‘bījagāmabhūtagāmasamārambhā paṭiviratau hautī’’ti aittha chaidanabhañ‍janādikaṃ vikaupanaṃ| ic‍caivaṃ –

kammai  āpattiyañ‍caiva, vīriyai hiṃsākriyāsu ca|

vikaupanai ca ārambha-saddau hautīti niddisai|

labha lābhai| labhati, labbhati| lābhau, laddhaṃ, alattha, alatthuṃ|

subha dittiyaṃ| saubhati| saubhā, saubhanaṃ, saubhitau|

khubha sañ‍calanai| khaubhati, saṅkhaubhati| hatthināgai padin‍namhi, khubbhittha nagaraṃ tadā| khaubhā, saṅkhaubhau|

nabha tubha hiṃsāyaṃ| nabhati| tubhati|

sambha vissāsai| sambhati| sambhatti, sambhattau|

lubha vimauhanai| laubhati, palaubhati| thul‍lakumārīpalaubhanaṃ| kāritai pana ‘‘laubhaiti, palaubhaiti, palaubhaitvā’’ti rūpāni bhavanti| divādigaṇaṃ pana patvā giddhiyatthai ‘‘lubbhatī’’ti rūpaṃ bhavati|

dabhi ganthanai| dambhati| dambhanaṃ|

rubhi nivāraṇai| rumbhati, san‍nirumbhati| san‍nirumbhau, san‍nirumbhitvā|

ubha ubbha umbha pūraṇai| ubhati| ubbhati| umbhati| ubhanā| ubbhanā| umbhanā| aubhau| kaiṭubhaṃ| ubbhaṃ| kumbhau| kumbhī| kāritai ‘‘aubhaiti| ubbhaiti| umbhaitī’’ti rūpāni bhavanti|

tattha kaiṭubhanti kriyākappavikappau kavīnaṃ upakāriyasatthaṃ| idaṃ panaittha nibbacanaṃ kiṭaiti gamaiti kriyādivibhāgaṃ, taṃ vā anavasaisapariyādānatau kaiṭaintau gamaintau aubhaiti pūraitīti kaiṭutaṃ kiṭaubhadhātuvasaina| ubbhati ubbhaiti  pūraitīti ubbhaṃ, pūraṇanti atthau| cariyāpiṭakaipi hi īdisī saddagati dissati, taṃ yathā? ‘‘mahādānaṃ pavattaisi, ac‍cubbhaṃ sāgarūpama’’nti| tattha ca ac‍cubbhanti ativiya yācakānaṃ ajjhāsayaṃ pūraṇaṃ| akkhumbhantipi pāṭhau| kumbhauti kaṃ vuc‍cati udakaṃ, taina ubbhaitabbauti kumbhau, sau aiva itthiliṅgavasaina kumbhī| aittha ca ‘‘kumbhī dhauvati aunatau’’ti payaugau|

kumbhasaddau ghaṭai hatthi-siraupiṇḍai dasambaṇai|

pavattatīti viñ‍ñaiyyau, viñ‍ñunā nayadassinā||

bhakārantadhāturūpāni|

makārantadhātu

mā mānai saddai ca| māti| mātā| aittha mātāti janikā vā cūḷamātā vā mahāmātā vā|

mū bandhanai| mavati| kiyādigaṇassa panassa ‘‘munātī’’ti rūpaṃ|

mai paṭidānaādānaisu| maiti, mayati| maidhā|

aittha maidhāti pañ‍ñā| sā hi sukhumampi atthaṃ dhammañ‍ca khippamaiva maiti ca dhāraiti cāti maidhāti vuc‍cati| aittha pana maitīti gaṇhāti| tathā hi aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘asani viya siluc‍cayai kilaisai maidhati hiṃsatīti maidhā, khippaṃ gahaṇadhāraṇaṭṭhaina vā maidhā’’ti| saṅgamatthavācakassa pana maidhadhātussa vasaina maidhati sīlasamādhiādīhi saddhammaihi siriyā ca saṅgacchatīti maidhāti atthau gahaitabbau| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

‘‘dvidhātuyaikadhātuyā, dviratthavatiyāpi ca|

maidhāsaddassa nipphattiṃ, jañ‍ñā sugatasāsanai’’ti||

aumā sāmatthiyai| sāmatthiyaṃ samatthabhāvau| aluttantauyaṃ dhātu, aumāti, aumanti|

atrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘aumāti bhantai bhagavā iddhiyā manaumayaina kāyaina brahmalaukaṃ upasaṅkamitu’’nti| tattha aumātīti pahauti sak‍kauti|

timu addabhāvai| addabhāvau tintabhāvau| taimati| tintau, taimiyau| taimitukāmā taimiṃsu|

aittha taimiyauti aivaṃnāmakau kāsirañ‍ñau puttau baudhisattau| sau hi rañ‍ñau caiva mahājanassa ca hadayaṃ taimaintau addabhāvaṃ pāpaintau sītalabhāvaṃ janaintau jātauti ‘‘taimiyau’’ti vuc‍cati|

nitami kilamanai| nitammati| hadayaṃ dayhatai nitammāmi|

camu chamu japu jhamu umu jimu adanai| camati| camū| camūti sainā| chamati| jamati| jhamati| umati| jaimati|

kamu padavikkhaipai| padavikkhaipau padasā gamanaṃ| idaṃ pana vauhārasīsamattaṃ vacanaṃ, tasmā ‘‘nāssa kāyai aggi vā visaṃ vā satthaṃ vā kamatī’’tiādīsu apadavikkhaipatthaupi gahaitabbau| kamati| caṅkamati, atik‍kamati| abhik‍kamati| paṭik‍kamati| pak‍kamati| parak‍kamati| vik‍kamati| nik‍kamati| saṅkamati| saṅkamanaṃ| saṅkanti| kamanaṃ| caṅkamanaṃ| atik‍kamau| abhik‍kamau| paṭik‍kamau| pak‍kamau| vik‍kamau| nik‍kamau| atik‍kantau purisau| abhik‍kantā ratti| nikkhamati| abhinikkhamati| kāritai  nikkhāmaiti| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni| yasmā panāyaṃ dhātu curādigaṇaṃ patvā icchākanti yatthaisu vattati, tasmā taipi atthai upasaggavisaisitai katvā idha abhik‍kantasaddassa atthuddhāraṃ vattabbampi avatvā upari curādigaṇaiyaiva kathaissāma|

yamu uparamai| uparamau viramanaṃ| yamabhi| yamau| ‘‘parai ca na vijānanti, mayamaittha yamāmasai’’ti idamaittha nidassanaṃ| tattha yamāmasaiti uparamāma, nassāma, marāmāti atthau|

nama bahuttai saddai| bahuttau saddau nāma uggatasaddau| namati|

ama dama hamma mima chama gatimhi| amati| damati| hammati| mimati| chamati| chamā|

chamāti pathavī| chamāsaddau itthiliṅgau daṭṭhabbau, ‘‘na chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa agilānassa dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti sikkhā karaṇīyā’’ti ca ‘‘chamāyaṃ parivattāmi vāricarauva ghammai’’ti ca payaugadassanatau| sau ca khau sattahi aṭṭhahi vā vibhattīhi dvīsu ca vacanaisu yaujaitabbau| chamanti gacchanti aitthāti chamā|

dhama saddaggisaṃ yaugaisu| dhamadhātu saddai ca mukhavātaina saddhiṃ aggisaṃyaugai ca vattati| tattha paṭhamatthai ‘‘saṅkhaṃ dhamati| saṅkhadhamakau| bhairiṃ dhamati| bhairidhamakau| dhamai dhamai nātidhamai’’ti payaugau| dutiyatthai ‘‘aggiṃ dhamati| samuṭṭhāpaiti attānaṃ, aṇuṃ aggiṃva sandhama’’nti payaugau|

bhāma kaudhai| bhāmati|

namu  namanai| namati| namau| nataṃ, namanaṃ| nati| namaṃ| namamānau| namantau| namitau| nāmaṃ| nāmitaṃ| namituṃ| natvā, natvāna| namitvā, namitvāna, namituna| kāritai ‘‘nāmaiti, nāmayati| nāmaitvā| nāmayitvā’’ti rūpāni bhavanti| tatra hi ‘‘namati namitvā’’ti aivaṃpakārāni padāni namanatthai vandanāyañ‍ca daṭṭhabbāni, ‘‘namau natvā’’ti aivaṃpakārāni pana vandanāyamaiva| atrāyamupalakkhaṇamattā payaugaracanā –

rukkhau phalī phalabhāragarutāya namitvāna bhij‍jati|

vuddhau jarājaj‍jaratāya namati namitvā gacchati|

saddhau buddhaṃ namati namitvā gacchati|

namau buddhassa satthāraṃ natvāna agamāsīti|

aittha namauti padaṃ nipātaisupi labbhati| taina hi pac‍cattaupayaugavacanāni abhin‍narūpāni dissanti ‘‘daivarāja namau tyatthu| namau katvā mahaisinau’’ti| upasaggaihipi ayaṃ yaujaitabbā ‘‘paṇamati, paṇāmau, uṇṇamati, uṇṇati’’ ic‍cādinā|

khamu sahanai| khamati| khanti| khamau, khamanaṃ, aivaṃ bhāvai| kattari pana ‘‘khantā| khamitā| khamau hauti sītassapi uṇhassapī’’ti payaugā|

sama adassanai| samati, vūpasamati aggi|

yama parivaisanai| yamati| yamau| yamarājā|

sama saddai| samati|

sama thama vailambai| samati| thamati|

vāyama īhāyaṃ| vāyamati| vāyāmau|

gamu  gatiyaṃ| gacchati| gamakau| gatau| gati| gamanaṃ| kāritai ‘‘gamaiti, gamayati, gacchāpaitī’’tiādīni bhavanti|

ramu kīḷāyaṃ| ramati| viramati| paṭiviramati| uparamati| ārati| virati| paṭivirati| uparati| vairamaṇi| viramaṇaṃ| rati| ramaṇaṃ| ratau| āratau viratau paṭiviratau| uparatau, uparamau| ārāmau|

vamu uggiraṇai| vamati| vamathu| vammikau|

dhīratthu taṃ visaṃ vantaṃ, yamahaṃ jīvitakāraṇā|

vantaṃ pac‍cāvamissāmi, mataṃ mai jīvitā varaṃ||

tattha vammikauti vamatīti, vantakauti, vantussayauti, vantasinaihasambandhauti vammikau| sau hi ahinakulaundūragharagauḷikādayau nānappakārai pāṇakai vamatīti vammikau| upacikāhi vantakauti vammikau| upacikāhi vamitvā mukhatuṇḍakaina ukkhittapaṃsucuṇṇaina kaṭippamāṇainapi paurisappamāṇainapi ussitauti vammikau| upacikāhi vantakhaiḷasinaihaina ābaddhatāya sattasattāhaṃ daivai vassantaipi na vippakirayati, nidāghaipi tatau paṃsumuṭṭhiṃ gahaitvā tasmiṃ muṭṭhinā pīḷiyamānai sinaihauva nikkhamati, aivaṃ vantasinaihasambandhauti vammikau|

aittha pana ‘‘bhagavā, himavā’’tiādīni padāni na kaivalaṃ vantupac‍cayavasainaiva nipphādaitabbāni, atha khau vamudhātuvasainapi nipphādaitabbāni, taināha visuddhimaggakārakau ‘‘yasmā pana tīsu bhavaisu taṇhāsaṅkhātaṃ gamanamanaina vantaṃ, tasmā ‘‘bhavaisu vantagamanau’ti vattabbai bhavasaddatau bhakāraṃ, gamanasaddatau gakāraṃ, vantasaddatau vakārañ‍ca dīghaṃ katvā ādāya bhagavāti vuc‍cati, yathā laukai ‘maihanassa  khassa mālā’ti vattabbai maikhalā’’ti vadatā niruttinayaina saddasiddhi dassitā|

aittha, siyā ‘‘visamamidaṃ nidassanaṃ, yaina ‘maihanassa khassa mālā’ti aittha maikārakhakāralākārānaṃ kamatau gahaṇaṃ dissati, ‘‘bhavaisu vantagamanau’ti aittha pana bhakāravakāragakārānaṃ kamatau gahaṇaṃ na dissatī’’ti? sac‍caṃ, idha pana ‘‘aggāhitau, vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau’’tiādīsu viya guṇasaddassa paranipātavasaina ‘‘bhavaisu gamanavantau’’ti vattabbaipi aivamavatvā saddasatthai yaibhuyyaina guṇasaddānaṃ pubbanipātabhvassa icchitattā saddasatthavidūnaṃ kaisañ‍ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ manaṃ tausaituṃ ‘‘bhagavā’’ti padai akkharak‍kamaṃ anapaikkhitvā atthamattanidassanavasaina ‘‘āhitaggi, sampan‍navij‍jācaraṇau’’tiādīni viya pubbanipātavasaina ‘‘bhavaisu vantagamanau’’ti vuttaṃ| īdisasmiñhi ṭhānai ‘‘āhitaggī’’ti vā ‘‘aggāhitau’’ti vā ‘‘chin‍nahatthau’’ti vā ‘‘hatthacchin‍nau’’ti vā padaisu yathā tathā ṭhitaisupi atthassa ayutti nāma natthi añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ samānatthattā taisaṃ saddānaṃ|

vaidajātautiādīsu pana ṭhānaisu atthaivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ visuddhimaggai ‘‘bhagavā’’ti padassa vamudhātuvasainapi nipphatti dassitā, taṭṭīkāyampi ca dassitā ‘‘bhagai vamīti bhagavā| bhāgai vamīti bhagavā’’ti| nibbacanaṃ pana aivaṃ vaiditabbaṃ – bhagasaṅkhātaṃ siriṃ issariyaṃ yasañ‍ca vami uggiri khaiḷapiṇḍaṃ viya anapaikkhau chaḍḍayīti bhagavā| atha vā bhāni nāma nakkhattāni, taihi samaṃ gacchanti pavattantīti bhagā, sinairuyugandharauttarakuruhimavantādibhājanalaukā, visaisasan‍nissayasaubhākappaṭṭhiyabhāvatau| taipi bhagavā vami tan‍nivāsisattāvāsasamatik‍kamanatau tappaṭibaddhachandarāgappahānaina pajahīti bhagavā|

cak‍kavattisiriṃ  yasmā, yasaṃ issariyaṃ sukhaṃ|

pahāsi laukacittañ‍ca, sugatau bhagavā tatau||

tathā khandhāyatanadhātādibhaidai dhammakauṭṭhāsai sabbaṃ papañ‍ca sabbaṃ yaugaṃ sabbaṃ ganthaṃ sabbaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ samucchinditvā amataṃ dhātuṃ samadhigacchantau vami uggiri anapaikkhau chaḍḍayi na pac‍cāvamīti bhagavā| atha vā sabbaipi kusalākusalai sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jai hīnappaṇītai kaṇhasuk‍kasappaṭibhāgai dhammai ariyamaggañāṇamukhaina vami uggiri anapaikkhau paric‍caji pajahīti bhagavā|

khandhāyatanadhātādī, dhammabhaidā mahaisinā|

kaṇhasuk‍kā yatau vantā, tataupi bagavā matau||

jātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana himavāti padassa vamudhātuvasainapi nipphatti dassitā| tathā hi sambhavajātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘himavāti himapātasamayai himayuttauti himavā| gimhakālai himaṃ vamatīti himavā’’ti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ jātakaṭṭhakathāyaṃ ‘‘himavā’’ti padassa vamudhātuvasainapi nipphatti dassitā, ayaṃ nayau īdisaisu ṭhānaisupi naitabbau| ‘‘guṇavāgaṇavā’’tiādīsu pana na naitabbau| yadi nayaiyya, ‘‘guṇavā gaṇavā’’ti padānaṃ ‘‘nigguṇau parihīnaguṇau’’ti aivamādiatthau bhavaiyya, tasmā ayaṃ nayau sabbatthapi na naitabbau| aittha siyā ‘‘yadi ‘‘bhagavā’tiādipadānaṃ vamudhātuvasaina nipphatti hauti, kathaṃ ‘‘bhagavantau, bhagavanta’’ntiādīni sijjhantī’’ti? yathā ‘‘bhagavā’’ti padaṃ niruttinayaina sijjhati, tathā tānipi tainaiva sijjhanti| acintaiyyau hi niruttinayau kaivalaṃ atthayuttipaṭibandhamattauva, atthayuttiyaṃ sati nipphādaitumasak‍kuṇaiyyānipi rūpāni anainaiva sijjhanti| aittha ca  yaṃ niruttilakkhaṇaṃ āharitvā dassaitabbaṃ siyā, taṃ upari rūpanipphādanādhikārai udāharaṇaihi saddhiṃ pakāsaissāma|

idha sāramatai munirājamatai,

paramaṃ paṭutaṃ sujanau pihayaṃ|

vipulatthadharaṃ dhaninītimimaṃ,

satataṃ bhajataṃ matisuddhakaraṃ||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñunaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

saravaggapañ‍cakantikau nāma dhātuvibhāgau

pan‍narasamau paricchaidau|

16. bhūvādigaṇikaparicchaida

itau paraṃ avaggantā, missakā caiva dhātuyau|

vakkhāmi dhātubhaidādi-kusalassa matānugā||

yakārantadhātu

yā gatipāpuṇaisu| yāti, yanti| yātu, yantu| yaiyya, yaiyyuṃ, anupariyaiyyuṃ| yathāsambhavaṃ padamālā yaujaitabbā| yantau purisau| yantī itthī| yantaṃ kulaṃ| yānaṃ, upayānaṃ, uyyānaṃ ic‍cādīni| divādigaṇikassa panassa ‘‘yāyati, yāyantī’’tiādīni rūpāni bhavanti|

tatra yānantiādīsu yanti aitaināti yānaṃ, rathasakaṭādi| upayanti aitaina issarassa vā piyamanāpassa vā santikaṃ gacchantīti upayānaṃ, paṇṇākāraṃ| ‘‘upayānāni mai daj‍juṃ, rājaputta tayi gatai’’ti aittha hi paṇṇākārāni ‘‘upayānānī’’ti  vuc‍canti| sampan‍nadassanīyapupphaphalāditāya uddhaṃ aulaukaintā yanti gacchanti aitthāti uyyānaṃ|

byā ummīsanai| byāti, byanti| byāsi, byātha| byāmi, byāma| yathāsambhavaṃ padamālā yaujaitabbā| tatra panāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘yāva byāti nimmīsati, tatrāpi rasatibbayau’’ti| tattha yāva byātīti yāva ummīsati, purāṇabhāsā aisā, ayañhi yasmiṃ kālai baudhisattau cūḷabaudhiparibbājakau ahausi, tasmiṃ kālai manussānaṃ vauhārau|

yu missanai gatiyañ‍ca| yauti, yavati| āyu, yauni|

tattha ‘‘āyū’’ti āsaddau upasaggau| āyavanti missībhavanti sattā aitaināti āyu| atha vā āyavanti āgacchanti pavattanti tasmiṃ sati arūpadhammāti āyu| tathā hi aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘āyavanaṭṭhaina āyu| tasmiñhi sati arūpadhammā āyavanti āgacchanti pavattanti, tasmā āyūti vuc‍catī’’ti| ‘‘āyu, jīvitaṃ, pāṇau’’ ic‍caitai pariyāyā laukavauhāravasaina| abhidhammavasaina pana ‘‘ṭhiti yapanā yāpanā jīvitindriyaṃ’’ ic‍caitaipi taihaiva saddhiṃ pariyāyā| yaunīti aṇḍajādīnaṃ aṇḍajādīhi saddhiṃ yāya missībhāvau hauti, sā yauni| idaṃ panaittha nibbacanaṃ ‘‘yavanti aittha sattā aikajātisamanvayaina añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ missakā hauntīti yauni’’ iti| aittha ca yaunisaddassa atthuddhārau nīyatai| yaunīti khandhakauṭṭhāsassapi kāraṇassapi passāvamaggassapi nāmaṃ| ‘‘catassau nāgayauniyau| catassau supaṇṇayauniyau’’ti aittha hi khandhakauṭṭhāsau yauni nāma| ‘‘yauni  haisā bhūmija phalassa adhigamāyā’’ti aittha kāraṇaṃ| ‘‘na cāhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ brūmi, yaunijaṃ mattisambhava’’nti aittha passāvamaggau| aitthaitaṃ vuc‍cati –

khandhānañ‍cāpi kauṭṭhāsai, muttamaggai ca kāraṇai|

imaisu tīsu atthaisu, yaunisaddau pavattati||

byai saṃvaraṇai| byāyati|

byai pavattiyaṃ| byaiti sahabyau|

aittha sahabyauti saha byaiti saha pavattatīti sahabyau, sahāyau, aikabhavūpagau vā| tathā hi ‘‘tāvatiṃsānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapan‍nau’’tiādīsu aikabhavūpagau ‘‘sahabyau’’ti vuc‍cati|

haya gatiyaṃ| hayati| hayau| hayauti assau| sau hi hayati sīghaṃ gacchatīti hayauti vuc‍cati| imāni panassa nāmāni –

assau turaṅgau turagau, vājī vāhau hayaupi ca|

tabbhaidā sindhavau caiva, gaujau assataraupi ca||

kāraṇākāraṇañ‍ñū tu, ājānīyau hayuttamau|

ghauṭakau tu khaḷuṅkassau, vaḷavauti ca vuc‍cati|

assapautau kisaurauti, khaḷuṅkautipi vuc‍cati|

hariya gatigailañ‍ñaisu| hariyati|

aya vaya paya maya taya caya raya gatiyaṃ| ayati| vayati| payati| mayati| tayati| cayati| rayati| ayau, samayau, vayau, payau, rayau| mayatayacayadhātūnaṃ nāmikapadāni upaparikkhitabbāni|

tattha  ayauti kāḷalauhaṃ, ayati nānākammārakic‍caisu upayaugaṃ gacchatīti ayau| vayauti paṭhamavayādiāyukauṭṭhāsau, vayati parihāniṃ gacchatīti vayau| payauti khīrassapi udakassapi nāmaṃ, payati janaina pātabbabhāvaṃ gacchatīti payau| rayauti vaigau, yau ‘‘javau’’tipi vuc‍cati, tasmā rayanaṃ javanaṃ rayau| aittha samayasaddassa atthuddhārau vuc‍catai aha nibbacanaina| samayasaddau –

samavāyai khaṇai kālai, samayai haitudiṭṭhisu|

paṭilābhai pahānai ca, paṭivaidhai ca dissati||

tathā hi ‘‘appaiva nāma svaipi upasaṅkamaiyyāma kālañ‍ca samayañ‍ca upādāyā’’ti aivamādīsu samavāyau atthau| ‘‘aikauva khau bhikkhavai khaṇau ca samayau ca brahmacariyavāsāyā’’tiādīsu khaṇau| ‘‘uṇhasamayau pariḷāhasamayau’’tiādīsu kālau| ‘‘mahāsamayau pavanasmi’’ntiādīsu samūhau| ‘‘samayaupi khau tai bhaddāli appaṭividdhau ahausī’’tiādīsu haitu| ‘‘taina samayaina uggāhamānau paribbājakau samaṇamuṇḍikāputtau samayappavādakai tindukācīrai aikasālakai mal‍likāya ārāmai paṭivasatī’’tiādīsu diṭṭhi|

‘‘diṭṭhai dhammai ca yau atthau, yau catthau samparāyikau|

atthābhisamayā dhīrau, paṇḍitauti pavuc‍catī’’ti

ādīsu  paṭilābhau| ‘‘sammā mānābhisamayā antamakāsi dukkhassā’’tiādīsu pahānaṃ| ‘‘dukkhassa pīḷanaṭṭhau saṅkhataṭṭhau santāpaṭṭhau abhisamayaṭṭhau’’tiādīsu paṭivaidhau| aittha ca upasaggānaṃ jautakamattattā tassa tassa atthassa vācakau samayasaddau aivāti samayasaddassa atthuddhāraipi saupasaggau abhisamayasaddau vuttau|

tattha sahakārīkāraṇatāya san‍nijjhaṃ samaiti samavaitīti samayau, samavāyau| samaiti samāgacchati maggabrahmacariyaṃ aittha tadādhārapuggalauti samayau, khaṇau| samainti aittha, aitaina vā saṅgacchanti dhammā sahajātadhammaihi uppādādīhi vāti samayau, kālau| dhammappavattimattatāya atthatau abhūtaupi hi kālau dhammappavattiyā adhikaraṇaṃ karaṇaṃ viya ca parikappanāmattasiddhaina rūpaina vauhariyatīti| samaṃ, saha vā avayavānaṃ ayanaṃ pavatti avaṭṭhānanti samayau, samūhau, yathā ‘‘samudāyau’’ti| avayavasahāvaṭṭhānamaiva hi samūhau| pac‍cayantarasamāgamai aiti phalaṃ aitasmā uppaj‍jati pavattati cāti samayau, haitu, yathā ‘‘samudayau’’ti| samaiti saṃyaujanabhāvatau sambandhā aiti attanau visayai pavattati, daḷhaggahaṇabhāvatau vā saṃyuttā ayanti pavattanti sattā yathābhinivaisaṃ aitaināti samayau, diṭṭhi| diṭṭhisaṃyaujanaina hi sattā ativiya bajjhanti| samiti saṅgati samaudhānanti samayau, paṭilābhau| samassa niraudhassa yānaṃ, sammā vā yānaṃ apagamau appavattīti samayau, pahānaṃ| ñāṇaina abhimukhaṃ sammā aitabbau adhigantabbauti samayau, dhammānaṃ aviparītau sabhāvau, abhimukhabhāvaina sammā aiti gacchati bujjhatīti samayau| yathābhūtasabhāvāvabaudhau| aivaṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ atthai samayasaddassa pavatti vaiditabbā|

nanu  ca atthamattaṃ pati saddā abhinivisantīti na aikaina saddaina anaikai atthā abhidhīyantīti? sac‍camaitaṃ saddavisaisai apaikkhitai| saddavisaisai hi apaikkhamānai aikaina saddaina anaikatthābhidhānaṃ na sambhavati| na hi yau kālatthau samayasaddau, sauyaiva samūhādiatthaṃ vadati| aittha pana taisaṃ taisaṃ atthānaṃ samaya saddavacanīyatā sāmañ‍ñamupādāya anaikatthatā samayasaddassa vuttā| aivaṃ sabbattha atthuddhārai adhippāyau vaiditabbau|

itau yātau ayatau ca, nipphattiṃ samudīrayai|

viñ‍ñū samayasaddassa, samavāyādivācinau||

itau yātau ayatau ca, samānatthaihi dhātuhi|

aivaṃ samānarūpāni, bhavantīti ca īrayai||

naya rakkhaṇai ca| cakārau gatipaikkhakau| nayati| nayau| nayauti nayanaṃ gamananti nayau, pāḷigati| nayanti vā rakkhanti atthaṃ aitaināti nayau, tathattanayādi|

daya dānagatihiṃsādānarakkhāsu| dayati| dayā|

dayāti maittāpi vuc‍cati karuṇāpi| ‘‘dayāpan‍nau’’ti aittha hi maittā ‘‘dayā’’ti, maittacittataṃ āpan‍nauti hi atthau| ‘‘adayāpan‍nau’’ti aittha pana karuṇā ‘‘dayā’’ti vuc‍cati| nik‍karuṇataṃ āpan‍nauti hi atthau| aivaṃ dayāsaddassa maittākaruṇāsu pavatti vaiditabbā| tathā hi abhidhammaṭīkāyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘dayāsaddau yattha yattha pavattati, tattha tattha adhippāyavasaina yaujaitabbau| dayāsaddau hi anurakkhaṇatthaṃ antaunītaṃ katvā pavattamānau maittāya ca karuṇāya ca pavattatī’’ti|

vacanatthau panaittha aivaṃ vaiditabbau – dayati dadāti sattānaṃ abhayaṃ aitāyāti dayā| dayati gacchati vibhāgaṃ akatvā  pāpakalyāṇajanaisu samaṃ vattati, sītaina samaṃ pharantaṃ rajaumalañ‍ca pavāhaintaṃ udakamivātipi dayā, maittā| dayati vā hiṃsati kāruṇikaṃ yāva yathādhippaitaṃ parassa hitanipphattiṃ na pāpuṇāti, tāvāti dayā| dayati anuggaṇhāti pāpajanampi saj‍janau aitāyātipi dayā| dayati attanau sukhampi pahāya khaidaṃ gaṇhāti saj‍janau aitāyāti dayā| dayanti gaṇhanti aitāya mahābaudhisattā buddhabhāvāya abhinīhārakaraṇakālai hatthagatampi arahattaphalaṃ chaḍḍaitvā saṃsārasāgaratau sattai samuddharitukāmā anassāsakaraṃ atibhayānakaṃ mahantaṃ saṃsāradukkhaṃ, pacchimabhavai ca saha amatadhātupaṭilābhaina anaikaguṇasamalaṅkataṃ sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇañ‍cātipi dayā, karuṇā| karuṇāmūlakā hi sabbai buddhaguṇā|

aparau nayau – dayanti anurakkhanti sattai aitāya, sayaṃ vā anudayati, anudayamattamaiva vā aitanti dayā, maittā caiva karuṇā ca| kiñ‍ci payaugamaittha kathayāma ‘‘saiyyathāpi gahapati gijjhau vā kaṅkau vā kulalau vā maṃsapaisiṃ ādāya dayaiyya| puttaisu maddī dayaisi, sassuyā sasuramhi ca| dayitabbau rathaisabha’’| tattha dayaiyyāti uppatitvā gacchaiyya, gatyatthavasainaitaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| dayaisīti maittacittaṃ karaiyyāsi| dayitabbauti piyāyitabbau| ubhayampaitaṃ vivaraṇaṃ rakkhaṇatthaṃ antaugadhaṃ katvā adhippāyatthavasaina katanti vaiditabbaṃ|

ūyī tantasantānai| ūyati| ūtau, ūtavā|

pūyī visaraṇai duggandhai ca| pūyati| pūtau, pūtavā| pūtimacchaṃ kusaggaina, yau narau upanayhati|

kanuyī  saddai| kanuyati| kanutau, kanutavā|

khamāya vidhūnanai| khamāyati| khamātau, khamātavā|

phāyi pāyi vuddhiyaṃ| phāyati| phītau, phatavā|

tattha tatavantupac‍cayā, yakāralaupau, dhātvantassa sarassa ikārādaisau ca daṭṭhabbau| aisa nayau ‘‘pūtau pūtavā’’tiādīsupi yathāsambhavaṃ daṭṭhabbau| pāyati| pāyau| apāyau| aittha ca natthi pāyau vuddhi aitthāti apāyau| atha vā pana ayatau sukhatau apaitauti apāyautipi nibbacanīyaṃ| apāyauti ca nirayau tiracchānayauni paittivisayau asurakāyauti cattārau apāyā|

tāyu santānapālanaisu| tāyati| tāyanaṃ| divādigaṇai pana tā pālanaiti dhātuṃ passatha, tassa ‘‘tāyati tāṇa’’nti rūpāni| ubhayaisaṃ kriyāpadaṃ samaṃ| akārayakārapac‍cayamattainaiva nānattaṃ, nāmikapadāni pana visadisāni ‘‘tāyanaṃ, tāṇa’’nti|

cāyu pūjānisāmanaisu| pūjā pūjanā| nisāmanaṃ aulaukanaṃ savanañ‍ca vuc‍cati| ‘‘iṅgha maddi nisāmaihi| nisāmayatha sādhavau’’ti ca ādīsu hi aulaukanasavanāni nisāmanasaddaina vuttāni| apica ñāṇaina upaparikkhaṇampi nisāmanamaivāti gahaitabbaṃ| cāyati, apacāyati| anagārai pabbajitai, apacai brahmacāriyai| yai vuddhamapacāyanti| apacitiṃ dassaiti| nic‍caṃ vuddhāpacāyinau|

yakārantadhāturūpāni|

rakārantadhātu

rā  ādānai| rāti|

ri santānai| raiti| raiṇu| raiṇūti rajau|

ru gatiyaṃ rausanai ca| ravati, viravati|

ru saddai| rauti, ravati| ravau, uparavau| rutamanuñ‍ñaṃ ruciyā ca piṭṭhi| rutanti ravanaṃ rutaṃ, saddau|

rai saddai| rāyati| rā| ratti| aittha ca rāti saddau| rattīti nisāsaṅkhātau sattānaṃ saddassa vūpasamakālau| rā tiyyati ucchij‍jati aitthāti ratti|

brū viyattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ| api hantvā hatau brūti|

bravīti, brunti| brūsi, brūtha| brūmi, brūma| brūtai, bruvantai| brūsai, bruvhai| bruvai, brumhai|

brūtu, bruvitu, bruvantu| brūhi, brūtha| brūmi, brūma| brūtaṃ, bruvantaṃ|

aittha ca ambaṭṭhasuttai ‘‘puna bhavaṃ gautamau bruvitū’’ti pāḷidassanatau ‘‘bruvitū’’ti vuttaṃ| aivaṃ sabbatthāpi upaparikkhitvā nayau gahaitabbau|

bruvaiyya, bruvai, bruvaiyyuṃ| bruvaiyyāsi, bruvaiyyātha| bruvaiyyāmi, bruvaiyyāma| bruvaitha, bruvairaṃ| bruvaithau, bruvaiyyāvhau| bruvaiyyaṃ| bruvaiyyāmhai|

pabrūti| anubrūti| pabrūtu, anubrūtu| pabruvaiyya, anubruvaiyya| aivaṃ sabbattha paanuupasaggaihipi yathāsambhavaṃ padamālā yaujaitabbā|

āha, āhu| bravai, bravittha, bravirai| bravitthau, bravivhau| braviṃ, bravimhai| paraukkhāvasaina vuttāni|

abravā, abravū| abravau, abravattha| abravaṃ, abravamhā| abravattha, abravatthuṃ| abravasai, abravhaṃ| abraviṃ, abravimhasai| hiyyattanīvasaina vuttāni|

abravi, abravuṃ| abravau, abravittha| abraviṃ, abravimhā| abravā, abravū| abravasai, abravivhaṃ| abravaṃ, abravimhai| aj‍jatanīvasaina vuttāni|

bruvissati, bruvissanti| abravissā, abravissaṃsu| saisaṃ sabbaṃ naitabbaṃ| kammapadaṃ appasiddhaṃ| sacai pana siyā, ‘‘brūyatī’’ti siyā ‘‘luyati, lūyatī’’ti padāni viya|

jīra brūhanai| brūhanaṃ vaḍḍhanaṃ| jīrati| jīraṃ| jīramānau| jīraṇaṃ| appassutāyaṃ purisau, balibaddauva jīrati|

pūra pūraṇai| pūrati| pūratauva mahaudadhi| sabbai pūraintu saṅkappā| pūrituṃ, pūritvā, pūraṃ, pūritaṃ| puṇṇaṃ, paripuṇṇaṃ| sampuṇṇaṃ, pūraṇaṃ| pūraṇau kassapau| kāritai ‘‘pāramiyau pūraiti, pūrayati, pūrāpaiti, pūrāpayati| pūraitvā, pūrayitvā, pūrāpaitvā, pūrāpayitvā, paripūraitvā’’ ic‍cādīni bhavanti|

ghaura gatipaṭighātai| gatipaṭighātaṃ gatipaṭihananaṃ| ghaurati|

dhaura gaticāturiyai| gaticāturiyaṃ gatichaikabhāvau| dhauraiti|

sara gatiyaṃ| sarati, visarati, ussarati| ussāraṇā| sarau| saṃsārau ic‍cādīni| tattha sarauti rahadau| saṃsārauti vaṭṭaṃ, yau ‘‘bhavau’’tipi vuc‍cati|

cara caraṇai| carati, vicarati, anucarati, sañ‍carati|

cara  gatibhakkhanaisu| carati, vicarati, anucarati, sañ‍carati, paṭicarati| cariyā| caritā| cārau| vicārau| anuvicārau| upavicārau| caraṇaṃ| cārakau| aucarakau| brahmacariyaṃ ic‍cādīni|

tattha caratīti gacchati, bhakkhati vā| tathā hi caranti padassa gacchantau khādantau cāti atthaṃ vadanti garū| paṭicaratīti paṭicchādaiti| cārakauti taṃpavaisitānaṃ sattānaṃ sukhaṃ carati bhakkhatīti cārakau, raudhau| aucarakauti adhaucārī| brahmacariyanti dānampi vaiyyāvac‍campi sikkhāpadampi brahmavihāraupi dhammadaisanāpi maithunaviratipi sadārasantausaupi upausathaupi ariyamaggaupi sakalaṃ sāsanampi ajjhāsayaupi vuc‍cati|

kintai vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ,

kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

iddhijutibalavīriyūpapatti,

idañ‍ca tai nāga mahāvimānaṃ||

ahañ‍ca bhariyā ca manussalaukai,

saddhā ubhau dānapatī ahumhā|

aupānabhūtaṃ mai gharaṃ tadāsi,

santappitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca||

taṃ mai vataṃ taṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ,

tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

iddhijutibalavīriyūpapatti,

idañ‍ca mai dhīra mahāvimāna’’nti

imasmiñhi puṇṇakajātakai dānaṃ ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttaṃ|

‘‘kaina  pāṇi kāmadadau, kaina pāṇi madhussavau|

kaina tai brahmacariyaina, puñ‍ñaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati||

taina pāṇi kāmadadau, taina pāṇi madhussavau|

taina mai brahmacariyaina, puñ‍ñaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhatī’’ti

imasmiṃ aṅkurapaitavatthumhi vaiyyāvac‍caṃ ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘idaṃ khau taṃ bhikkhavai tittiriyaṃ nāma brahmacariyaṃ ahausī’’ti imasmiṃ tittirajātakai sikkhāpadaṃ ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttaṃ| ‘‘taṃ khau pana pañ‍casikha brahmacariyaṃ naiva nibbidāya na virāgāya…pai॰… yāvadaiva brahmalaukūpapattiyā’’ti imasmiṃ mahāgauvindasuttai brahmavihārā ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttā| ‘‘aikasmiṃ brahmacariyasmiṃ, sahassaṃ mac‍cuhāyinau’’ti aittha dhammadaisanā ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttā| ‘‘parai abrahmacārī bhavissanti, mayamaittha brahmacārinau bhavissāmā’’ti sal‍laikhasuttai maithunavirati ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttā|

mayañ‍ca bhariyā nātik‍kamāma,

amhai ca bhariyā nātik‍kamanti|

añ‍ñatra tāha brahmacariyaṃ carāma|

tasmā hi amhaṃ daharā na mīyarai’’ti

mahādhammapālajātakai sadārasantausau ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttau|

hīnaina brahmacariyaina, khattiyai upapaj‍jati|

majjhimaina ca daivaisu, uttamaina visujjhatī’’ti

aivaṃ nimijātakai avītik‍kamavasaina katau upausathau ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttau| ‘‘idaṃ khau pana pañ‍casikha brahmacariyaṃ aikantanibbidāya virāgāya…pai॰… ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau’’ti  mahāgauvindasuttasmiṃyaiva ariyamaggau ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttau| ‘‘tayidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ iddhañ‍caiva phītañ‍ca vitthārikaṃ bāhujañ‍ñaṃ puthubhūtaṃ yāvadaiva manussaihi suppakāsita’’nti pāsādikasuttai sikkhattayasaṅgahaṃ sakalaṃ sāsanaṃ ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttaṃ|

‘‘api ataramānānaṃ, phalāsāva samijjhati|

vipak‍kabrahmacariyausmi, aivaṃ jānāhi gāmaṇī’’ti

aittha ajjhāsayau ‘‘brahmacariya’’nti vuttau| ic‍caivaṃ –

dānaṃ vaiyyāvaṭiyañ‍ca, sikkhā brahmavihārakā|

dhammakkhānaṃ maithunatā-virati ca upausathau||

sadāraisu ca santausau, ariyamaggau ca sāsanaṃ|

ajjhāsayau cimai brahma-cariyasaddaina vuc‍carai||

hura kauṭil‍lai| hurati|

sara saddaupatāpaisu| sarati| sarau, saraṇaṃ|

aittha ca sarauti saddaupi vuc‍cati usupi| saraṇanti sarati upatāpaiti hiṃsati saraṇagatānaṃ tainaiva saraṇagamanaina bhayaṃ santāpaṃ dukkhaṃ duggatiṃ parikilaisañ‍cāti saraṇaṃ, buddhādiratanattayaṃ| atha vā saddhā pasan‍nā manussā ‘‘amhākaṃ saraṇamida’’nti saranti cintainti, taṃ tattha ca vācaṃ niccharanti gacchanti cātipi saraṇaṃ|

sara cintāyaṃ| sarati, susarati ic‍capi payaugau| appakkharānañhi bahubhāvau añ‍ñathābhāvau ca hauti, yathā ‘‘dvai, duvai, taṇhā tasiṇā, pamhaṃ, pakhuma’’nti| anussarati, paṭissarati| saranti aitāya sattā, sayaṃ vā sarati, saraṇamattamaiva vā aitanti sati| anussati, paṭissati| saratīti satau| punappunaṃ saratīti paṭissatau|

dvara  saṃvaraṇai| saṃvaraṇaṃ rakkhaṇā| dvarati| dvāraṃ| dvisaddūpapadaaradhātuvasainapi idaṃ rūpaṃ sijjhati| tatrimāni nibbacanāni – dvaranti saṃvaranti rakkhanti aitaināti dvāraṃ, atha vā dvai kavāṭā aranti gacchanti pavattanti aitthātipi dvāranti| gaihadvārampi kāyadvārādīnipi upāyaupi dvāranti vuc‍cati| pāḷiyaṃ tu ‘‘dvāraṃ dvārā’’ti ca itthinapuṃsakavasaina dvārasaddau vuttau| tathā hi ‘‘dvārampi surakkhitaṃ hautī’’ti ca ‘‘dvārāpaisā’’ti ca tassa dviliṅgatā vuttā|

gara ghara saicanai| garati| gharati| gharaṃ|

dhūra hucchanai| hucchana kauṭil‍laṃ| dhūrati|

tara p‍lavanasaraṇaisu| tarati| taraṇaṃ| titthaṃ| tiṇṇau| uttiṇṇau| autiṇṇau ic‍cādīni| tattha taraṇaṃ vuc‍cati nāvā, tarati udakapiṭṭhai p‍lavati, taranti uttaranti vā nadiṃ aitaināti atthaina|

nāvā p‍lavau taraṃ pautau, taraṇaṃ uttaraṃ tathā|

jalayānanti aitāni, nāvānāmāni haunti tu||

tara sambhamai| sambhamau anavaṭṭhānaṃ| tarati| taritau| turaṅgau|

aittha ca ‘‘sau māsakhaittaṃ taritau avāsari’’nti pāḷi nidassanaṃ| tattha taritauti turitau sambhamantau| avāsarinti upagacchiṃ upavisiṃ vā|

jara raugai| aittha jararaugauyaiva ‘‘raugau’’ti adhippaitau payaugavasaina| jarasaddassa hi jararaugai pavattaniyamanatthaṃ ‘‘raugai’’ti vuttaṃ| taina añ‍ñau raugau idha raugasaddaina na vuc‍cati| jarati| jarau| saj‍jarau| paj‍jararaugau| jaraina pīḷitā manussā| yattha tu ayaṃ vayauhānivācakau, tattha payaugai ‘‘jīrati, jarā’’ti cassa rūpāni bhavanti|

dara bhayai| darati| darī| ‘‘bīlāsayā darīsayā’’ti nidassanaṃ| tattha darīti bhāyitabbaṭṭhaina darī|

dara ādarānādaraisu| darati, ādarati, anādarati| ādarau, anādarau|

aittha ca daratīti daraṃ karautīti ca anādaraṃ karautīti ca atthau| yathā hi ārakāsaddau dūrāsan‍navācakau, tathāyampi daradhātu ādarānādaravācakau daṭṭhabbau| darasaddau ca kāyadarathai cittadarathai kilaisadarathai ca vattati| ayañhi –

ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ, ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ|

vārinā viya ausiñ‍ci, sabbaṃ nibbāpayai dara’’nti

aittha kāyadarathai cittadarathai ca vattati| ‘‘vītaddarau vītasaukau vītasal‍lau, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya abhāsi buddhau’’ti aittha pana kilaisadarathai vattati| vītaddarauti hi aggamaggaina sabbakilaisānaṃ samucchin‍nattā vigatakilaisadarathauti atthau|

nara nayanai| narati| narau, nārī|

aittha narauti purisau| sau hi narati naitīti narau| yathā paṭhamapakatibhūtau sattau datarāya pakatiyā saiṭṭhaṭṭhaina puri uc‍cāṭṭhānai saiti pavattatīti purisauti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ nayanaṭṭhaina narauti vuc‍cati| puttabhātubhūtaupi hi puggalau mātujaiṭṭhabhaginīnaṃ naituṭṭhānai tiṭṭhati, pagaiva itarau itarāsaṃ| nārīti naraina yaugatau, narassāyanti vā nārī| aparampaittha narasaddassa nibbacanaṃ, nariyati sakaina  kammaina niyyatīti narau, sattau manussau vā| ‘‘kammaina niyyatai laukau’’ti hi vuttaṃ| tattha narasaddassa tāva purisavacanai ‘‘narā ca atha nāriyau’’ti nidassanaṃ| sattamanussavacanai pana ‘‘buddhau ayaṃ aidisakau naruttamau| āmauditā naramarū’’ti ca nidassanaṃ, tasmā ‘‘narauti purisau, narauti sattau, narauti manussau’’ti tattha tattha yathāsambhavaṃ atthau saṃvaṇṇaitabbau|

hara haraṇai| haraṇaṃ pavattanaṃ| harati| sāvatthiyaṃ viharati| vihāsi| vihaṃsu| viharissati| appamattau vihissati| vauharati| saṃvauharati॰ sabbau harati vā| rūpiyasaṃvauhārau, rūpiyasabbauhārau vā| pāṭihāriyaṃ| pītipāmauj‍jahārau| vihārau| vauhārau| abhihārau| cittaṃ abhinīharati| sāsanai viharaṃ, viharantau, viharamānau| vihātabbaṃ, viharituṃ| viharitvā| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

tattha pāṭihāriyanti samāhitai cittai vigatūpak‍kilaisai katakic‍caina pacchā haritabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti pāṭihāriyaṃ| paṭīti hi ayaṃ saddau ‘‘pacchā’’ti aitassa atthaṃ baudhaiti ‘‘tasmiṃ paṭipaviṭṭhamhi, añ‍ñau āgañchi brāhmaṇau’’tiādīsu viya| vihārauti ṭhānanisaj‍jādinā viharanti aitthāti vihārau, bhikkhūnaṃ āvāsau| viharaṇaṃ vā vihārau, viharaṇakriyā| vauhārauti byavahāraupi paṇṇattipi vacanampi caitanāpi| tattha

yau hi kauci manussaisu, vauhāraṃ upajīvati|

aivaṃ vāsaiṭṭha jānāti, vāṇijau sau nabrāhmaṇau’’ti||

ayaṃ  byavahāravauhārau nāma| ‘‘saṅkhā samañ‍ñā pañ‍ñatti vauhārau’’ti ayaṃ paṇṇattivauhārau nāma| tathā tathā vauharanti parāmasantīti ayaṃ vacanavauhārau nāma| ‘‘aṭṭha ariyavauhārā, aṭṭha anariyavauhārā’’ti ayaṃ caitanāvauhārau nāma| ic‍caivaṃ –

byavahārai vacanai ca, paṇṇatticaitanāsu ca|

vauhārasaddau catūsu, imaisvatthaisu dissati||

hara apanayanai| apanayanaṃ nīharaṇaṃ| dausaṃ harati| nīharati| nīhārau, pariharati| parihārau| rajauharaṇaṃ| sabbadausaharau dhammau| bhagavatau ca sāsanassa ca paṭipakkhai titthiyai haratīti pāṭihāriyaṃ| mattāvaṇṇabhaidainaittha ‘‘pāṭihairaṃ pāṭihīraṃ pāṭihāriya’’nti tīṇi padarūpāni, bhavanti|

hara ādānai| adin‍naṃ harati| harissati| hāhiti ic‍capa| ‘‘kharājinaṃ para suñ‍ca, khārikājañ‍ca hāhitī’’ti idamaittha nidassanaṃ| āharati, avaharati, saṃharati, apaharati, upaharati, paharati, sampaharati, samāharati| manauharau pāsādau| parassaharaṇaṃ| āhārau, avahārau, saṃhārau, upahārau, sampahārau, samāhārau| hariyyati, āhariyyati| āhariyyanti| āhaṭaṃ, harituṃ, āharituṃ, āharitvā, āharitvāna| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

dhara dharaṇai| dharaṇaṃ vij‍jamānatā| dharati| dharatai satthusāsanaṃ|

dhara aviddhaṃsanai| nibbānaṃ nic‍caṃ dharati|

khara khayai| kharati| kharaṇaṃ| nakkharanti na khiyyantīti akkharāni| nakkharanti na nassantīti nakkhattānīti paurāṇā|

jāgaraniddakkhayai  jāgarati| jāgarau, jāgaraṇaṃ, jāgaraṃ| dīghā jāgaratau ratti| jāgaramānau| ayañ‍ca dhātu tanādigaṇaṃ patvā ‘‘jāgarauti, paṭijāgarautī’’ti rūpāni janaiti|

īra vacanai gatikampanaisu ca| īrati| īritaṃ| airitaṃ| samīraṇau| jinairitau dhammau| kuppanti vātassapi airitassa|

tattha samīraṇauti vātau| sau hi samīrati vāyati, samīraiti ca rukkhasākhāpaṇṇādīni suṭṭhu kampaitīti ‘‘samīraṇau’’ti vuc‍cati|

harai laj‍jāyaṃ| aluttantauyamaikārantau dhātu, gilai pītikkhayaiti dhātu viya| harāyati| harāyanaṃ| aṭṭīyāmi harāyāmi|

aittha harāyatīti laj‍jati, hiriṃ karautīti atthau|

para pālanapūraṇaisu| ‘‘parati, paramau’’timassa rūpāni, nara nayanaiti dhātussa ‘‘narati narau’’ti rūpāni viya|

tattha paratīti pālaiti, pūrati vā| suddhakattuvasainidaṃ padaṃ vuttaṃ| haitukattuvasaina hi ‘‘pāraiti pārayatī’’tiādīni rūpāni bhavanti| paramauti pālakau pūrakau vā| aittha ca ‘‘pāramī’’ti padaṃ aitassatthassa sādhakaṃ| tathā hi pāramīti parati, pāraiti cāti paramau, dānādīnaṃ guṇānaṃ pālakau pūrakau ca mahābaudhisattau| paramassa idaṃ, paramassa vā bhāvau, kammaṃ vā pāramī, dānādikriyā| garūhi pana ‘‘pūraitīti paramau, dānādīnaṃ guṇānaṃ pūrakau pālakau cā’’ti vuttaṃ, taṃ vīmaṃsitabaṃ|

vara varaṇai| varati| vāraṇau, varuṇau|

gira  niggiraṇau| niggiraṇaṃ paggharaṇaṃ| girati, giri|

aittha girīti pabbatā, yau ‘sailau’’tiādīhi anaikaihi nāmaihi kathiyati| sau hi sandhisaṅkhātaihi pabbaihi citattā pabbamassa atthīti pabbatau| himavamanādivasaina jalassa sārabhūtānaṃ bhaisaj‍jādivatthūnañ‍ca giraṇatau girīti vuc‍cati|

imāni panassa nāmāni –

pabbatau acalau sailau, nagau giri mahīdharau|

addi siluc‍cayau cāti, giripaṇṇattiyau imā||

sura issariyadittīsu| surati| surau, asurau|

tatra sarauti surati īsati daivissariyaṃ pāpuṇāti viraucati cāti surau| sundarā rā vācā assāti vā surau, daivau| daivābhidhānāni divādigaṇai pakāsaissāma| asurauti daivau viya na surati na īsati na viraucati cāti asurau| surānaṃ vā paṭipakkhau mittapaṭipakkhā amittā viyāti asurau, dānavau, yau ‘‘pubbadaivau’’tipi vuc‍cati| tathā hi kumbhajātakai vuttaṃ –

‘‘yaṃ vai pivitvā pubbadaivā pamattā,

tidivā cutā sassatiyā samāyā|

taṃ tādisaṃ maj‍jamimaṃ niratthaṃ,

jānaṃ mahārāja kathaṃ pivaiyyā’’ti||

sagāthāvaggasaṃvaṇṇanāyaṃ pana ‘‘na suraṃ pivimha, na suraṃ pivimhā’ti āhaṃsu, tatau paṭṭhāya asurā nāma jātā’’ti vuttaṃ|

imāni  tadabhidhānāni –

asurau pubbadaivau ca, dānavau daivatāri tu|

nāmāni asurānanti, imāni niddisai vidū||

pākau iti tu yaṃ nāmaṃ, aikassa asurassa tu|

paṇṇattītipi aikac‍cai, garavau pana abravuṃ||

kura saddai ak‍kausai ca| kurati| kurarau, kurarī| kummau, kummī|

khura chaidanai vilaikhanai ca| khurati| khurau|

mura saṃvaiṭhanai| murati| murau, maurau|

ghura abhimatta saddaisu| ghurati| ghaurau|

pura aggagamanai| aggagamanaṃ nāma padhānagamanaṃ, paṭhamamaiva gamanaṃ vā| purati| puraṃ, purī| avāpurati| avāpuraitaṃ amatassa dvāraṃ| avāpuraṇaṃ ādāya gacchati|

tattha puranti rājadhānī| tathā hi ‘‘nagaraṃ puraṃ purī rājadhānī’’ti aitai pariyāyā| ‘‘aisau āḷārikau pausau, kumārī puramantarai’’tiādīsu pana gaihaṃ ‘‘pura’’nti vuc‍cati| padhānatāya puratau puratau gamanaina gantabbanti puraṃ, rājadhānī caiva gaihañ‍ca| avāpuraṇanti avāpuranti vivaranti dvāraṃ aitaināti avāpuraṇaṃ, yaṃ ‘‘kuñ‍cikā’’tipi ‘‘tāḷau’’tipi vuc‍cati| avāpuratītiādīsu ava āic‍cubhau upasaggāti daṭṭhabbā|

phara pharaṇai| pharaṇaṃ nāma byāpanaṃ gamanaṃ vā| samaṃ pharati sītaina| āhāratthaṃ pharati| pharaṇaṃ|

gara uggamai| garati| garu|

garūti  mātāpitādayau gāravayuttapuggalā| tai hi garanti uggacchanti uggatā pākaṭā hauntīti garūti vuc‍canti| apica pāsāṇacchattaṃ viya bhāriyaṭṭhaina garūti vuc‍canti| garusaddau ‘‘idamāsanaṃ atra bhavaṃ nisīdatu, bhavañhi mai añ‍ñatarau garūna’’nti aittha mātāpitūsu dissati| ‘‘sanarāmaralaukagaru’’nti aittha sabbalaukācariyai sabbañ‍ñumhi| apica garusaddau añ‍ñaisvatthaisupi dissati| sabbamaitaṃ aikatau katvā atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

mātāpitācariyaisu, duj‍jarai alahumhi ca|

mahantai cuggatai caiva, nichaikādikaraisu ca|

tathā vaṇṇavisaisaisu, garusaddau pavattati||

kaici panācariyā ‘‘garu garū’’ti ca dvidhā gahaitvā bhāriyavācakattai garusaddau ṭhitau| ācariyavācakattai pana gurusaddauti vadanti, taṃ na gahaitabbaṃ| pāḷivisayai hi sabbaisampi yathāvuttānaṃ atthānaṃ vācakattai garusaddauyaiva icchitabbau, akārassa ākārabhāvai ‘‘gārava’’nti savuddhikassa taddhitantapadassa dassanatau| sak‍kaṭabhāsāvisayai pana gurusaddauyaiva icchitabbau, ukārassa vuddhibhāvai añ‍ñathā taddhitantapadassa dassanatau|

mara pāṇacāgai| marati| mattuṃ| maritvā| haitukattari ‘‘purisau purisaṃ māraiti, mārayati| purisau purisaina purisaṃ mārāpaiti, mārāpayati| purisau purisaṃ māraituṃ māraitvā’’ ic‍cādīni rūpāni| mac‍cau| maru| maraṇaṃ, mac‍cu| maṭṭu| mārau|

tattha mattunti marituṃ| tathā hi alīnasattujātakai ‘‘yau mattumicchai pitunau pamaukkhā’’ti pāḷi dissati| mac‍cauti maritabbasabhāvatāya ‘‘mac‍cau’’ti laddhanāmau sattau| marūti dīghāyukaupi samānau maraṇasīlauti maru, daivau| maraṇanti cuti|

maraṇaṃ antakau mac‍cu, hindaṃ kālau ca maṭṭu ca|

nikkhaipau cuti caitāni, nāmāni maraṇassa vai||

mārauti sattānaṃ kusalaṃ māraitīti mārau, kāmadaivau|

imānissa nāmāni –

mārau namuci kaṇhau ca, vasavatti pajāpati|

pamattabandhu maddanau, pāpimā dabbakaupi ca|

kandappau ca ratipati, kāmau ca kusumāyudhau||

añ‍ñai añ‍ñānipi nāmāni vadanti, tāni sāsanānulaumāni na hauntīti idha na dassitāni| aṭṭhakathāsu pana ‘‘mārau, namuci, kaṇhau, pamattabandhū’’ti cattārauva nāmāni āgatāni|

aittha ca mārauti daivaputtamāraina saddhiṃ pañ‍ca mārā kilaisamārau khandhamārau abhisaṅkhāramārau mac‍cumārau daivaputtamārauti|

dhara avatthānai| dharati|

bhara pausanai| bharati| bharitau, bhattā|

thara santharaṇai| tharati, santharati| santharaṇaṃ|

dara vidāraṇai| bhūmiṃ darati| kudālau|

dara dāhai| kāyau darati| darau, darathau|

tira adhaugatiyaṃ| tirati| tiracchānau, tiracchā vā|

ara gatiyaṃ| arati| atthaṃ, atthau, utu|

aittha  atthaṃ vuc‍cati nibbānaṃ| taṃ taṃ sattakic‍caṃ arati vattaitīti utu|

rakārantadhāturūpāni|

lakārantadhātu

lā ādānai| lāti| lānaṃ, garuḷau, sīhaḷau, rāhulau, kusalaṃ, bālau, mahal‍lakau, mahal‍likā|

tatra garuḷauti garuṃ lāti ādadāti gaṇhātīti garuḷau, yau ‘‘supaṇṇau, dijādhipau, nāgāri, karauṭī’’ti ca vuc‍cati| sīhaḷauti sīhaṃ lāti ādadāti gaṇhātīti sīhaḷau, pubbapurisau| tabbaṃsai jātā aitarahi sabbaipi sīhaḷā nāma jātā|

rāhulautiādīsu pana rāhu viya lāti gaṇhātīti rāhulau, kau sau? sikkhākāmau āyasmā rāhulabhaddau buddhaputtau| tassa hi jātadivasai suddhaudanamahārājā ‘‘puttassa mai tuṭṭhiṃ nivaidaithā’’ti uyyānai kīḷantassa baudhisattassa sāsanaṃ pahiṇi| baudhisattau taṃ sutvā ‘‘rāhu jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti āha| puttassa hi jāyanaṃ rāhuggahau viya hauti| taṇhākilissanatāpādanatau bāḷhaina ca saṅkhalikādibandhanaina bandhaṃ viya hauti muc‍cituṃ appadānatauti ‘‘rāhu jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti āha| rājā ‘‘kiṃ mai puttau avacā’’ti pucchitvā taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā ‘‘itau paṭṭhāya mai nattā ‘rāhulau’ tvaiva hautū’’ti āha, tatau paṭṭhāya kumārau rāhulau nāma jātau|

mahāpadānasuttaṭīkāyañhi  ‘‘rāhu jātau’’ti aittha ‘‘rāhūti rāhuggahau’’ti vuttaṃ, taṃ pana ‘‘rāhulau’’ti vacanassatthaṃ pākaṭaṃ kātuṃ adhippāyatthavasaina vuttaṃ| na hi kaivalau ‘‘rāhū’’ti saddau ‘‘rāhuggahau’’ti atthaṃ vadati, atha khau jātasaddasambandhaṃ labhitvā vadati| tathā hi ‘‘rāhu jātau’’ti baudhisattaina vuttavacanassa ‘‘rāhuggahau jātau’’ti atthaौ bhavati, tasmā suddhaudanamahārājā ‘‘mama nattā rāhu viya lātīti rāhulauti vattabbau’’ti cintaitvā ‘‘rāhulautvaiva hautū’’ti āhāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

kaici pana ‘‘rāhulau jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti paṭhanti, katthaci pautthakai ca likhanti, taṃ na sundaraṃ, atthassa ayuttitau ṭīkāya ca saddhiṃviraucatau| na hi ‘‘rāhulau’’ti kumārassa nāmaṃ paṭhamaṃ uppan‍naṃ, pacchāyaiva pana uppan‍naṃ ayyakaina din‍nattā, tasmā tadā baudhisattaina ‘‘rāhulau jātau’’ti vattuṃ na yuj‍jati| yathā hi anabhisittai arājini puggalai ‘‘mahārājā’’ti vauhārau nappavattati| ṭīkāyañ‍ca ‘‘rāhūti rāhuggahau’’ti vuttaṃ| athāpi taisaṃ siyā ‘‘rāhulau jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti padassa vij‍jamānattā aiva ṭīkāyaṃ ‘‘rāhuggahau’’ti bhāvavasaina lāsaddaina samānatthau ādānatthau gahasaddau vuttauti aivampi nupapaj‍jati, ‘‘rāhulānaṃ jātaṃ bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti pāṭhassa vattabbattā| rāhulauti hi idaṃ padaṃ ‘‘sīhaḷau’’ti padaṃ viya dabbavācakaṃ, na kadācipi bhāvavācakaṃ, tasmā ‘‘rāhulau jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti aitaṃ aikac‍caihi duraupitaṃ pāṭhaṃ aggahaitvā ‘‘rāhu jātau bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti ayamaiva pāṭhau gahaitabbau, sāratau ca pac‍caitabbau suparisuddhaisu anaikaisu pautthakaisu diṭṭhattā, paurāṇaihi ca gambhīrasukhumañāṇaihi ācariyapacāriyaihi paṭhitattā|

ayaṃ  panaittha sādhippāyā atthappakāsanā – rāhu jātauti baudhisattau puttassa jātasāsanaṃ sutvā saṃvaigappattau ‘‘idāni mama rāhu jātau’’ti vadati, muc‍cituṃ appadānavasaina mama gahaṇatthaṃ rāhu uppan‍nauti hi atthau| bandhanaṃ jātanti iminā ‘‘mama bandhanaṃ jāta’’nti vadati| tathā hi ṭīkāyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘rāhūti rāhuggahau’’ti| tattha rāhuggahauti gaṇhātīti gahau, rāhu aiva gahau rāhuggahau, mama gāhakau rāhu jātauti atthau| atha vā gahaṇaṃ gahau, rāhunau gahau rāhuggahau, rāhuggahaṇaṃ mama jātanti atthau| puttau hi rāhusadisau| pitā candasadisau puttarāhunā gahitattā|

aikac‍cai pana ‘‘rāhulautvaiva hautū’’ti imaṃ padaisaṃ disvā ‘‘rāhu jātau’’ti vuttai iminā na samaiti, ‘‘rāhulau jātau’’ti vuttaiyaiva pana samaitīti mañ‍ñamānā aivaṃ pāṭhaṃ paṭhanti likhanti ca, tasmā sau anupaparikkhitvā paṭhitau duraupitau pāṭhau na gahaitabbau, yathāvuttau paurāṇakau paurāṇācariyaihi abhimatau pāṭhauyaiva āyasmantaihi gahaitabbau atthassa yuttitau, ṭīkāya ca saddhiṃ aviraudhatauti|

tattha kusalanti kucchitānaṃ pāpadhammānaṃ sānatau tanukaraṇatau ñāṇaṃ kusaṃ nāma, taina kusaina lātabbaṃ pavattaitabbanti kusalaṃ| bālauti diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikasaṅkhātai dvai anatthai daivadattakaukālikādayau viya lāti ādadātīti bālau| imāni pana taṃnāmāni –

bālau avidvā añ‍ñau ca, añ‍ñāṇī avicakkhaṇau|

apaṇḍitau akusalau, dummaidhau kumati jaḷau||

aiḷamūgau  ca nippañ‍ñau, dummaidhī avidū magau|

aviñ‍ñū andhabālau ca, duppañ‍ñau ca aviddasu||

mahal‍lakauti mahattaṃ lāti gaṇhātīti mahal‍lakau, jiṇṇapurisau| imānissa nāmāni –

jiṇṇau mahal‍lakau vuddhau, buddhau vuḍḍhau ca kattarau|

thairau cāti imai saddā, jiṇṇapañ‍ñattiyau siyuṃ||

tathā hi –

‘‘durai apassaṃ thairauva, cakkhuṃ yācitumāgatau’’|

aivamādīsu daṭṭhabbau, thairasaddau mahal‍lakai||

imāni pana nāmāni itthiyā itthiliṅgavasaina vattabbāni –

jiṇṇā mahal‍likā vuddhī, buddhī vuḍḍhī ca kattarā|

thairī cāti imai saddā, nāmaṃ jiṇṇāya itthiyā|

dala phala visaraṇai| dalati| phalati| dalitau rukkhau| phalitau bhūmibhāgau|

ala bhūsanai| alati| alaṅkārau, alaṅkatau, alaṅkataṃ| ‘‘sālaṅkānanayaugaipi, sālaṅkānanavaj‍jitā’’ti imissañhi kavīnaṃ kabbaracanāyaṃ alaṅkasaddau bhūsanavisaisaṃ vadati| kaici panaittha ala bhūsanapariyāpanavāranaisūti dhātuṃ paṭhanti, ‘‘alatī’’ti ca rūpaṃ icchanti| mayaṃ pana aladhātussa pariyattinivāraṇatthavācakattaṃ na icchāma payaugādassanatau| nipātabhūtau pana alaṃsaddau pariyattinivāraṇatthavācakau dissati ‘‘alamaitaṃ sabbaṃ| alaṃ mai taina raj‍jainā’’tiādīsu|

mīla nimailanai| mīlati, nimīlati, ummīlati| nimīlanaṃ|

bila patitthambhai| bilati|

nīla  vaṇṇai| nīlavatthaṃ|

sīla samādhimhi| sīlati| sīlaṃ, sīlanaṃ|

aittha sīlanti sīlanaṭṭhaina sīlaṃ| vuttañhaitaṃ visuddhimaggai ‘‘sīlanti kainaṭṭhaina sīlaṃ? sīlanaṭṭhaina sīlaṃ, kimidaṃ sīlanaṃ nāma? samādhānaṃ vā kāyakammādīnaṃ susīlyavasaina avippakiṇṇatāti atthau| upadhāraṇaṃ vā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ patiṭṭhānavasaina ādhārabhāvauti atthau| aitadaiva hi aittha atthadvayaṃ saddalakkhaṇavidū anujānanti| añ‍ñai pana ‘siraṭṭhau sīlaṭṭhau, sītalaṭṭhau sīlaṭṭhau’ti aivamādinā nayainaittha atthaṃ vaṇṇaintī’’ti| tattha ‘‘atthadvayaṃ saddalakkhaṇa vidū anujānantī’’ti idaṃ ‘‘sīla samādhimhi sīla upadhāraṇai’’ti dvigaṇikassa sīladhātussa atthai sandhāya vuttaṃ| imassa hi curādigaṇaṃ pattassa upadhāraṇai ‘‘sīlaiti, sīlayatī’’ti rūpāni bhavanti, upadhāraitītipi taisaṃ atthau| idha pana bhūvādigaṇikattā samādhānatthai ‘‘sīlatī’’ti rūpaṃ bhavati, samādhiyatīti tassa atthau| punapi aittha sautūnaṃ sukhaggahaṇatthaṃ nibbacanāni vuc‍cantai| sīlati samādhiyati kāyakammādīnaṃ susīlyavasaina na vippakiratīti sīlaṃ| atha vā sīlanti samādahanti cittaṃ aitaināti sīlaṃ| imāni bhūvādigaṇikavasaina nibbacanāni| curādigaṇikavasaina pana sīlaiti kusalai dhammai upadhāraiti patiṭṭhābhāvaina bhusau dhāraitīti sīlaṃ| sīlainti vā aitaina kusalai dhammai upadhārainti bhusau dhārainti sādhavauti sīlanti nibbacanāni|

kila bandhai| kilati| kilaṃ|

kūla āvaraṇai| kulati| kūlaṃ| vahai rukkhai pakūlajai| kūlaṃ bandhati| nadīkūlai vasāmahaṃ| kūlati āvarati udakaṃ bahi nikkhamituṃ na daitīti kūlaṃ|

sūla  rujāyaṃ| sūlati| sūlaṃ| kaṇṇasūlaṃ na janaiti|

tūla nik‍karīsai| nik‍karīsaṃ nāma karīsamattainapi aminaitabbatau lahubhāvauyaiva| tūlati| tūlaṃ bhaṭṭhaṃva mālutau|

pula saṅghātai| pulati| pañ‍capuli|

mūla patiṭṭhāyaṃ mūlati| mūlaṃ| mūlasaddau ‘‘mūlāni uddharaiyya antamasau ussīranāḷimattānipī’’tiādīsu mūlamūlai dissati| ‘‘laubhau akusalamūla’’ntiādīsu asādhāraṇahaitumhi| ‘‘yāvamajjhanhikai kālai chāyā pharati, nivātai paṇṇāni patanti, aittāvatā rukkhamūla’’ntiādīsu samīpai| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

mūlamūlai mūlasaddau, padissati tathaiva ca|

asādhāraṇahaitumhi, samīpamhi ca vattati||

phala nibbattiyaṃ| rukkhau phalati| rukkhaphalāni bhuñ‍jantā| mahapphalaṃ mahānisaṃsaṃ| sautāpattiphalaṃ| tattha phalanti mahānibbattikaṃ|

phala bhaidai| phalati| muddhā tai phalatu sattadhā| pādā phaliṃsu| tattha phalatūti bhaj‍jitu|

phala abyattasaddai| asanī phalati| dvaimai bhikkhavai asaniyā phalantiyā na santasanti| phalantiyāti saddaṃ karauntiyā|

cul‍la hāvakaraṇai| hāvakaraṇaṃ vilāsakaraṇaṃ| cul‍lati|

phul‍la  vikasanabhaidaisu| phul‍lati| phul‍laṃ| phul‍litau kiṃsukau| suphul‍litamaravindavanaṃ|

asītihatthamubbaidhau, dīpaṅkarau mahāmuni|

saubhati dīparukkhauva, sālarājāva phul‍litau||

khaṇḍaphul‍lapaṭisaṅkharaṇaṃ|

cil‍la saiṭhil‍lai| siṭhilabhāvau saiṭhil‍laṃ| cil‍lati|

vailu cailu kailu khailu pailu bailu sailu sala tila gatiyaṃ| vailati| cailati| kailati| khailati| pailati| bailati| sailati| salati| tilati| cailaṃ, bailakau| aittha cailanti vatthaṃ| pailakauti sasau|

khala calanai| khalati| khalau| khalauti duj‍janau asādhu asappurisau pāpajanau|

khala sañ‍cinanai| khalati| khalaṃ| khalanti vīhiṭṭhapanaukāsabhūtaṃ bhūmimaṇḍalaṃ| tañhi khalanti sañ‍cinanti rāsiṃ karaunti aittha dhañ‍ñānīti khalanti vuc‍cati| ‘‘khalaṃ sālaṃ pasuṃ khaittaṃ, gantā cassa abhikkhaṇa’’nti payaugau|

gila ajjhauharaṇai| gilati| gilamakkhaṃ purisau na bujjhati|

gala adanai| galati| galau| galanti adanti ajjhauharanti aitaināti galau| galauti gīvā vuc‍cati|

sala sal‍la āsugatiyaṃ| āsugati sīghagamanaṃ| salati| sal‍lati| sal‍laṃ| aittha ca ‘‘sal‍laṃ usu sarau sal‍lau kaṇḍau taijanau’’ti pariyāyā aitai|

khaula gatipaṭighātai| khaulati|

gilai  pītikkhayai| gilāyati| gilānau, gailañ‍ñaṃ| gilānauti akal‍lakau| vinayaipi hi vuttaṃ ‘‘nāhaṃ akal‍lakau’’ti| aṭṭhakathāyañ‍ca ‘‘nāhaṃ akal‍lakauti nāhaṃ gilānau’’ti vuttaṃ|

milai gattavināmai| milāyati| milāyanau, milāyantau, milāyamānau|

kailai mamāyanai| mamāyanaṃ taṇhādiṭṭhivasaina ‘‘mama ida’’nti gahaṇaṃ| kailāyati| tvaṃ kaṃ kailāyati|

sala calanai saṃvaraṇai ca, vala val‍la calanai ca| saṃvaraṇāpaikkhāyaṃ cakārau| salati| kusalaṃ| valati| val‍lati| val‍lūrau|

tattha kusalanti kucchitai pāpadhammai salayati calayati kampaiti viddhaṃsaitīti kusalaṃ| kucchitaṃ apāyadvāraṃ salanti saṃvaranti pidahanti sādhavau aitaināti kusalaṃ| val‍lanti saṃvaranti rakkhanti itau kākasainādayau sattai akhādanatthāyāti val‍lūrau|

mala mal‍la dhāraṇai| malati| malaṃ| mal‍lati| mal‍lau|

bhala bhal‍la paribhāsanahiṃsādānaisu| bhalati| bhal‍lati|

kala saṅkhyānai| kalati| kalā, kālau|

aittha kalāti sauḷasabhāgādibhāgau| kālauti ‘‘aittakau atk‍kantau’’tiādinā kalitabbau saṅkhātabbauti kālau, pubbaṇhādisamayau|

kal‍la asaddai| asaddau| nissaddau| kal‍lati|

jala dittiyaṃ| jalati| jalaṃ, jalantau, paj‍jalantau, jalamānau|

kau  aiti siriyā jalaṃ| jalaṃva yasasā aṭṭhā, daivadattauti mai sutaṃ| saddhammapaj‍jautau jalitau|

hula calanai| hulati| halau| halauti phālau, sau hi haulaiti bhūmiṃ bhindantau mattikakhaṇḍaṃ cālaitīti ‘‘halau’’ti vuc‍cati ukārassa akāraṃ katvā|

cala kampanai| calati| calitau, acalau| mahantau bhūmicālau| calanaṃ, cālau|

jala dhañ‍ñai| jalati| jalaṃ|

ṭala ṭula vailambai| ṭalati| ṭulati|

thala ṭhānai| thalati| thalau| thalauti nirudakappadaisau| pabbaj‍jānibbānaisupi taṃsadisattā tabbauhārau| yathā hi laukai udakaughaina anauttharaṇaṭṭhānaṃ ‘‘thalau’’ti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ kilaisaughaina anauttharaṇīyattā pabbaj‍jā nibbānañ‍ca ‘‘thalau’’ti vuc‍cati, ‘‘tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇau’’ti hi vuttaṃ|

phāla vilaikhanai| phālati bhūmiṃ vilaikhati bhindatīti phālau|

nala ganthai| nalati|

bala pāṇanai| iha pāṇanaṃ jīvanaṃ sasanañ‍ca| balati| balaṃ, bālau|

aittha balanti jīvitaṃ kappainti aitaināti balaṃ, kāyabalabhaugabalādikaṃ balaṃ| atha vā balanti sammājīvanaṃ jīvanti aitaināti balaṃ, saddhādikaṃ balaṃ| āgamaṭṭhakathāyaṃ pana ‘‘assaddhiyai na kampatīti saddhābala’’ntiādi vuttaṃ, taṃ daḷhaṭṭhaina balanti vattabbānaṃ saddhādīnaṃ akampanabhāvadassanatthaṃ vuttanti  daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā dhātūnaṃ atthātisayayaugatau assaddhiyādīnaṃ abhibhavanaina saddhādibalānaṃ abhibhavanatthaupi gahaitabbau ‘‘abalā naṃ baliyantī’’ti aittha viya| bālauti balati assasati caiva passasati cāti bālau, assasitapassasitamattaina jīvati, na saiṭṭhaina pañ‍ñājīvitaināti vuttaṃ hauti| tathā hi aṭṭhakathāyaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘balantīti bālā, assasitapassasitamattaina jīvanti, na pañ‍ñājīvitaināti atthau’’ti| pañ‍ñājīvinauyaiva hi jīvitaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ nāma| taināha bhagavā ‘‘pañ‍ñājīviṃ jīvitamāhu saiṭṭha’’nti|

pula mahattai| pulati| vipulaṃ|

kula saṅkhānai bandhumhi ca| kaulati| kulaṃ, kaulau|

sala gamanai| salati|

kila pītiya kīḷanaisu| pītassa bhāvau pītiyaṃ yathā dakkhiyaṃ| kīḷanaṃ kīḷāyaiva| kilati|

ila kampanai| ilati| ailaṃ, ailā| aittha ailaṃ vuc‍cati dausau| kainaṭṭhaina? kampanaṭṭhaina| dausauti caittha aguṇau vaiditabbau, na paṭighau| ‘‘nailaṅgau saitapacchādau’’ti idamaittha nidassanaṃ|

apica ailaṃ vuc‍cati udakaṃ| tathā hi ‘‘ailambujaṃ kaṇṭakiṃ vārijaṃ yathā’’ti imissā pāḷiyā atthaṃ niddisantau āyasmā sāriputtau ‘‘ailaṃ vuc‍cati udaka’’nti āha| ailāti lālā vuc‍cati ‘‘ailamūgau’’ti aittha viya| apica ailāti khaiḷau vuc‍cati ‘‘sutvā nailapatiṃ vācaṃ, vāḷā panthā apak‍kamu’’nti aittha viya| aittha nailapatiṃ vācanti khaiḷabindunipātavirahitaṃ  vacananti atthau| lālākhaiḷavācakassa tu ailāsaddassa añ‍ñaṃ pavattinimittaṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ| anaikappavattinimittā hi saddā| kiṃ vā añ‍ñaina pavattinimittaina, ila kampanaiti aivaṃ vuttaṃ kampanaṃ aiva lālākhaiḷavācakassa ailāsaddassa pavattinimittaṃ, tasmā ilanti jigucchitabbabhāvaina kampainti hadayacalanaṃ pāpuṇanti janā aitthāti ailāti atthau gahaitabbau| samānapavattinimittāyaiva hi saddā laukasaṅkaitavasaina nānāpadatthavācakāpi bhavanti| taṃ yathā? hinauti gacchatīti haitu, sappati gacchatīti sappau, gacchatīti gauti| tathā asamānappavattinimittāyaiva samānapadatthavācakāpi bhavanti| taṃ yathā? rañ‍catīti rājā, bhūmiṃ pālaitīti bhūmipālau, narai indatīti narindauti| aisa nayau sabbatthāpi vibhāvaitabbau|

sakārantadhātu

sā pākai| sāti|

si saivāyaṃ| saivati| saivanā, saivakau, saivitau, sivau, sivaṃ|

su gatiyaṃ| savati| pasavati| pasutau, sutau|

aittha sutauti dūtau, ‘‘vittiñhi maṃ vindati suta disvā’’ti ‘‘daivasutau ca mātalī’’ti ca imāni tattha payaugāni|

su savanai| savanaṃ sandanaṃ| savati| āsavau|

sū pasavai| pasavau jananaṃ| savati, pasavati| suttaṃ|

aittha pana suttanti atthai savati janaitīti suttaṃ, taipiṭakaṃ buddhavacanaṃ, tadañ‍ñampi vā hatthisuttādi suttaṃ|

hiṃsa hiṃsāyaṃ| hiṃsati| hiṃsakau, hiṃsanā, hiṃsā|

issa issāyaṃ| issati| purisaparak‍kamassa daivā na issanti| issā, issāyanā|

namassa vandanānatiyaṃ| vandanānati nāma vandanāsaṅkhātaṃ namanaṃ, sakammakauyaivāyaṃ dhātu, na namudhātu viya sakammakau caiva akammakau ca| namassati|

ghusa saddai| ghusati, ghausati| paṭighausau, nigghausau, vacīghausau|

pusa buddhiyaṃ| pusati| pausau| sampīḷai mama pausanaṃ| pausananti vaḍḍhanaṃ|

musa thaiyyai| thainanaṃ thaiyyaṃ caurikā| musati| duddikkhau cakkhumusanau| musalau|

pusa pasavai| pusati|

vāsi bhūsa alaṅkārai| vāsati| bhūsati, vibhūsati| bhūsanaṃ, vibhūsanaṃ|

usa rujāyaṃ| usati|

isa ucchai| aisati| isi|

aittha pana sīlādayau guṇai aisantīti isayau, buddhādayau ariyā tāpasapabbaj‍jāya ca pabbajitā narā| ‘‘isi tāpasau jaṭilau jaṭī jaṭādharau’’ti aitai tāpasapariyāyā|

kasa vilaikhanai| kasati, kassati| kassakau, ākāsau|

aittha kassakauti kasikārakau| ākāsauti nabhaṃ| tañhi na kassatīti ākāsau| kasituṃ vilaikhituṃ na sak‍kāti atthau| imāni tadabhidhānāni –

ākāsau ambaraṃ abbhaṃ, antalikkha’maghaṃ nabhaṃ|

vaihāsau gaganaṃ daivau, kha’mādic‍capathaupi ca||

tārāpathau ca nakkhatta-pathau ravipathaupi ca|

vaihāyasaṃ vāyupathau, apathau anilañ‍jasaṃ||

kasa  sisa jasa jhasa vasa masa disa jusa yusa hiṃsatthā| kasati| sisati| jasati| jhasati| vasati| masati| masakau| aumasati, aumasavādau| disati| jusati| yūsati|

tattha aumasatīti vijjhati| aumasavādauti paraisaṃ sūciyā viya vijjhanavādau| masakauti makasau|

bhassa bhassanai| bhassanti kathanaṃ vuc‍cati ‘‘āvāsau gaucarau bhassaṃ| bhassakāraka’’ntiādīsu viya| bhassati| bhaṭṭhaṃ| bhaṭṭhanti bhāsitaṃ, vacananti atthau| aittha pana –

‘‘subhāsitā atthavatī, gāthāyau tai mahāmuni|

nijjhattaumhi subhaṭṭhaina, tvañ‍ca mai saraṇaṃ bhavā’’ti

pāḷi nidassanaṃ| tattha nijjhattauti nijjhāpitau dhammaujapañ‍ñāya pañ‍ñattigatau amhi| subhaṭṭhaināti subhāsitaina|

jisu nisu visu misu vassa saicanai| jaisati| naisati| vaisati| maisati| daivau vassati|

marisu sahanai ca| cakārau saicanāpaikkhakau| marisati|

pusa pausanai| pausati| pausau| kammacittautuāhiāraihi pausiyatīti pausau| ‘‘añ‍ñaipi daivau pausaitī’’ti dassanatau pana curādigaṇaipi imaṃ dhātuṃ vakkhāma|

pisu silisu pusu palusu usu upadāhai| paisati| silaisati| silaisau| pausati| palausati| ausati| usu|

ghasu saṃharisai| saṃharisau saṅghaṭṭanaṃ| ghassati|

hasu āliṅgai| āliṅgau upagūhanaṃ| hassati|

hasa  hasanai| hasati| assā hasanti, ājānīyā hasanti, pahasati, uhasati| kāritai ‘‘hāsaiti’’ic‍cādi, uhasiyamānau, hāsau, pahāsau, hasanaṃ, pahasanaṃ, hasitaṃ| hakāralaupaina mandahasanaṃ ‘‘sita’’nti vuc‍cati ‘‘sitaṃ pātvākāsī’’tiādīsu|

tattha uhasatīti avahasati| udasiyamānauti avahasiyamānau| tatrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘idha bhikkhuṃ arañ‍ñagataṃ vā rukkhamūlagataṃ vā suñ‍ñāgāragataṃ vā mātugāmau upasaṅkamitvā uhasati’’ iti ca ‘‘sau mātugāmaina uhasiyamānau’’ iti ca| hāsauti hasanaṃ vā saumanassaṃ vā ‘‘hāsau mai upapaj‍jathā’’tiādīsu viya|

tusa hasa hisa rasa saddai| tusati, hasati, hisati, rasati, rasitaṃ| atrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘bhairiyau sabbā vaj‍jantu, vīṇā sabbā rasantu tā’’ iti|

rasa assādanai| rasati| rasau|

rasa assādasinaihaisu| rasati| rasau|

rasa hāniyaṃ| rasati| rasanaṃ, rasau|

atrāyaṃ pāḷi –

‘‘nahaiva ṭhita nā’sīnaṃ, na sayānaṃ na paddhaguṃ|

yāva byāti nimīsati, tatrāpi rasatibbayau’’ti||

tattha rasatibbayauti sau sau vayau rasati parihāyati, na vaḍḍhatīti atthau|

lasa silaisanakīḷanaisu| lasati| lāsau| lasī ca tai nippalitā| lasi vuc‍cati matthaluṅgaṃ| nippalitāti nikkhantā|

nisa  samādhimhi| samādhi samādhānaṃ cittaikaggatā| naisati|

misa masa saddai rausai ca| maisati| masati| maisau| masakau|

pisi paisu gatiyaṃ| pisati| paisati|

sasu hiṃsāyaṃ| sasati| satthaṃ| satthaṃ vuc‍cati asi|

saṃsa thutiyañ‍ca| cakārau hiṃsāpaikkhāya| saṃsati, pasaṃsati| pasaṃsā, pasaṃsanā| pasatthau bhagavā| pasaṃsamānau, pasaṃsitau, pasaṃsakau, pasaṃsitabbau, pasaṃsanīyau, pāsaṃsau, pasaṃsitvā ic‍cādīni|

disa paikkhanai| aitissā pana nānārūpāni bhavanti – ‘‘dissati padissati’’ ic‍cādi akammakaṃ| ‘‘passati dakkhati’’ic‍cādi sakammakaṃ|

dissatu, passatu, dakkhatu| dissaiyya, passaiyya, dakkhaiyya| dissai, passai, dakkhai| dissa, passa, dakkha| adissā, apassā| addā sīdantarai nagai| addakkhā, addakkhuṃ, adassuṃ| adassi, apassi, adakkhi|

dassissati, passissati, dakkhissati| adassissā, apassissā, dakkhissā| aivaṃ vattamānapañ‍camiyādivasaina vitthāraitabbāni| kāritai ‘‘dassaiti dassayatī’’ti rūpāni| kammai ‘‘passiyati’’ ic‍cādīni|

disā| passau| passaṃ| passitā| dassaitā| dassanaṃ| vipassanā, ñāṇadassananti nāmikapadāni| tadatthai pana tumatthai ca ‘‘dakkhitāyai’’ti rūpaṃ| ‘‘āgatāmha imaṃ dhammasamayaṃ, dakkhitāyai aparājitasaṅgha’’nti hi pāḷi| imasmiṃ pana pāḷippadaisai ‘‘dakkhitāyai’’ti idaṃ tadatthai tumatthai vā catutthiyā rūpaṃ| tathā  hi dakkhitāyaiti imassa dassanatthāyāti vā passitunti vā atthau yaujaitabbau| disātiādīsu pana puratthimādibhaidāpi disāti vuc‍cati| yathāha –

‘‘disā catassau vidisā catassau,

uddhaṃ adhau dasa disatā imāyau|

katamaṃ disaṃ tiṭṭhati nāgarājā,

yamaddasā supinai chabbisāṇa’’nti||

mātāpitādayaupi| yathāha –

‘‘mātāpitā disā pubbā, ācariyā dakkhiṇā disā|

puttadārā disā pacchā, mittāmac‍cā ca uttarā|

dāsakammakarā haiṭṭhā, uddhaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

aitā disā namassaiyya, alamattau kulai gihī’’ti|

pac‍cayadāyakāpi| yathāha – ‘‘agārinau an‍nadapānavatthadā, avhāyikā nampi disaṃ vadantī’’ti|

nibbānampi| yathāha –

‘‘aitādisā paramā saitakaitu,

yaṃ patvā niddukkhā sukhinau bhavantī’’ti|

aivaṃ disāsaddaina vuc‍camānaṃ attharūpaṃ ñatvā idānissa nibbacanamaivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| dissati candāvaṭṭanādivasaina ‘‘ayaṃ purimā ayaṃ pacchimā’’tiādinā nānappakāratau pañ‍ñāyatīti disā, puratthimadisādayau| tathā ‘‘imai amhākaṃ garuṭṭhāna’ntiādinā passitabbāti disā, mātāpitādayau| dissanti sakāya puñ‍ñakriyāya imai dāyakāti pañ‍ñāyantīti disā, pac‍cayadāyakā| dissati uppādavayābhāvaina nic‍cadhammattā sabbakālampi vij‍jatīti disā, nibbānaṃ| passauti kāraṇākāraṇaṃ  passatīti passau| aivaṃ passatīti passaṃ| atrāyaṃ pāḷi –

‘‘passati passau passantaṃ, apassantampi passati|

apassantau apassantaṃ, passantampi na passatī’’ti||

passatīti passitā| dassaitīti dassitā| dassananti dassanakriyā| apica dassananti cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| tañhi rūpārammaṇaṃ passatīti dassananti vuc‍cati| tathā ‘‘dassanaina pahātabbā dhammā’’ti vacanatau dassanaṃ nāma sautāpattimaggau| kasmā sautāpattimaggau dassanaṃ? paṭhamaṃ nibbānadassanatau| nanu gautrabhū paṭhamataraṃ passatīti? nau na passati, disvā kattabbakic‍caṃ pana na karauti saṃyaujanānaṃ appahānatau, tasmā ‘‘passatī’’ti na vattabbau| yattha katthaci rājānaṃ disvāpi paṇṇākāraṃ datvā kic‍canipphattiyā adiṭṭhattā aj‍jāpi rājānaṃ na passāmīti vadantau gāmavāsī nidassanaṃ|

vipassanāti anic‍cādivasaina khandhānaṃ vipassanakaṃ ñāṇaṃ| ñāṇadassananti dibbacakkhupi vipassanāpi maggaupi phalampi pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇampi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇampi vuc‍cati| ‘‘appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti aittha hi dibbacakkhu ñāṇadassanaṃ nāma| ‘‘ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaitī’’ti aittha vipassanāñāṇaṃ| ‘‘abhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāyā’’ti aittha maggau, ‘‘ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammau alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti aittha phalañāṇaṃ| ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi, akuppā mai caitauvimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’ti aittha pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaṃ| ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca  pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi sattāhakālaṅkatau āḷārau kālāmau’’ti aittha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| aitthaitaṃ bhavati –

‘‘dibbacakkhupi maggaupi, phalañ‍cāpi vipassanā|

pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇampi, ñāṇaṃ sabbañ‍ñutāpi ca|

ñāṇadassanasaddaina, imai atthā pavuc‍carai’’ti||

daṃsa daṃsanai| daṃsati, vidaṃsati| dantau| kāritai ālaukaṃ vidaṃsaiti|

aisa buddhiyaṃ| aisati|

saṃsa kathanai| saṃsati| yau mai saṃsai mahānāgaṃ|

kilisa bādhanai| kilisati| kilaisau|

aittha bādhanaṭṭhaina rāgādayaupi ‘‘kilaisā’’ti vuc‍canti dukkhampi| aitaisu dukkhavasaina –

‘‘idañ‍ca pac‍cayaṃ laddhā, pubbai kilaisamattanau|

ānandiyaṃ vicariṃsu, ramaṇīyai giribbajai’’ti

payaugau vaiditabbau| divādigaṇaṃ pana pattassa ‘‘kilissatī’’ti rūpaṃ|

vasa sinaihanai| vasati| vasā|

aittha ca vasā nāma vilīnasinaihau| sā vaṇṇatau nāḷikairatailavaṇṇā| ācāmai āsittatailavaṇṇātipi vattuṃ vaṭṭati|

īsa hiṃsāgatidassanaisu| īsati| īsau|

bhāsabyattāyaṃ vācāyaṃ| bhāsati| bhāsā, bhāsitaṃ, bhātā| paribhāsati| paribhāsā, paribhāsakau|

tatra  bhāsanti atthaṃ aitāyāti bhāsā, māgadhabhāsādi| bhāsitanti vacanaṃ| vacanatthau hi bhāsitasaddau nic‍caṃ napuṃsakaliṅgau daṭṭhabbau| yathā ‘‘sutvā luddassa bhāsita’’nti| vāc‍caliṅgau pana bhāsitasaddau tiliṅgau daṭṭhabbau| yathā ‘‘bhāsitau dhammau, bhāsitaṃ catusac‍caṃ, bhāsitā vācā’’ti| pubbai bhāsatīti bhātā, jaiṭṭhabhātāti vuttaṃ hauti| sau hi pubbai jātattā aivaṃ vattuṃlabhati| kiñ‍cāpi bhātusaddau ‘‘bhātikasataṃ, sattabhātarau| bhātaraṃ kaina dausaina, duj‍jāsi dakarakkhinau’’tiādīsu jaiṭṭhakaniṭṭhabhātūsu vattati, tathāpi yaibhuyyaina jaiṭṭhakai nirūṭṭhau, ‘‘bhātā’’ti hi vuttai jaiṭṭhabhātāti viñ‍ñāyati, tasmā katthaci ṭhānai ‘‘kaniṭṭhabhātā’’ti visaisaitvā vuttaṃ|

nanu ca bhau katthaci ‘‘jaiṭṭhabhātā’’ti visaisaitvā vuttanti? sac‍caṃ, taṃ pana bhātāsaddassa kaniṭṭhaipi vattanatau pākaṭīkaraṇatthaṃ ‘‘jaiṭṭhabhātā’’ti vuttaṃ| yathā hi hariṇaisu vattamānassa migasaddassa kadāci avasaisacatuppadaisupi vattanatau ‘‘hariṇamigau’’ti visaisaitvā vācaṃ bhāsanti, aivaṃ sampadamidaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| yathā ca gauhatthimahiṃsaacchasūkarasasabiḷārādīsu sāmañ‍ñavasaina migasaddai vattamānaipi ‘‘migacammaṃ migamaṃsa’’nti āgataṭṭhānai ‘‘hariṇassā’’ti visaisanasaddaṃ vināpi ‘‘hariṇamigacammaṃ hariṇamigamaṃsa’’nti visaisatthādhigamau hauti, aittha na gauhatthiādīnaṃ cammaṃ vā maṃsaṃ vā viñ‍ñāyati| tathā ‘‘migamaṃsaṃ khādantī’’ti vacanassa gauhatthiādīnaṃ maṃsaṃ khādantīti atthau na sambhavati, aivamaiva katthaci vināpi jaiṭṭhakaiti visaisanasaddaṃ ‘‘bhātā’’ti vuttaiyaiva ‘‘jaiṭṭhakabhātā’’ti atthau viñ‍ñāyatīti| nanu ca bhau ‘‘migacammaṃ, migamaṃsa’’nti aittha cammamaṃsasaddaihaiva  visaisatthādhigamau hautīti? na hauti, migasaddassa iva cammamaṃsasaddānaṃ sāmañ‍ñavasaina vattanatau, aivañ‍ca sati kaina visaisatthādhigamau hautīti cai? laukasaṅkaitavasaina, tathā hi migasaddai ca cammasaddādīsu ca sāmañ‍ñavasaina vattamānaisupi laukasaṅkaitaina paricchin‍nattā gauhatthiādīnaṃ cammādīni na ñāyanti laukaina, atha khau hariṇacammādīniyaiva ñāyanti| ‘‘saṅkaitavacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, laukasammuti kāraṇa’’nti hi vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

gilaisu anvicchāyaṃ| punappunaṃ icchā anvicchā| gilaisati|

yaisu payatanai| yaisati|

jaisu naisu aisu haisu gatiyaṃ| jaisati| naisati| aisati| haisati| dhātvantassa pana sañ‍ñaugavasaina ‘‘jaissati, naissatī’’tiādīnipi gahaitabbāni| jaissamānau| jaissaṃ, jaissantau| aittha ca –

‘‘yathā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ, dantiṃ anvaiti hatthinī|

jaissantaṃ giriduggaisu, samaisu visamaisu cā’’ti

pāḷi nidassanaṃ|

daisu haisu abyattasaddai| daisati| haisati|

kāsa saddakucchāyaṃ| kāsati, uk‍kāsati| kāsau| kāsaṃ sāsaṃ daraṃ balyaṃ, khīṇamaidhau nigacchati|

kāsu bhāsu dittiyaṃ| dittīti pākaṭatā, virājanatā vā| kāsati, pakāsati| pakāsati taijau| dūrai santau pakāsainti| bhāsati| pabhāsati midaṃ byamhaṃ| pakāsau| kāsu aubhāsau|

tatra  pakāsatīti pakāsau, pākaṭau hautīti atthau| tucchabhāvaina puñ‍jabhāvaina vā kāsati pakāsati pākaṭā hautīti kāsu| ‘‘kāsu’’ iti āvāṭaupi vuc‍cati rāsipi|

‘‘kiṃnu santaramānauva, kāsuṃ khanasi sārathi|

puṭṭhau mai samma akkhāhi, kiṃkāsuyā karissasī’’ti

aittha hi āvāṭau kāsu nāma| ‘‘aṅgārakāsuṃ aparai phuṇanti, narā rudantā paridaḍḍhagattā’’ti aittha rāsi| kāritai – pakāsaitīti pakāsakau| aubhāsaitīti aubhāsakau| kammai pakāsiyatīti pakāsitau| aivaṃ bhāsitau| bhāvai – kāsanā| saṅkāsanā| pakāsanā| tumantādittai ‘‘pakāsituṃ, pakāsaituṃ, aubhāsituṃ, aubhāsaituṃ| pakāsitvā, pakāsaitvā, aubhāsitvā, aubhāsaitvā’’ti rūpāni bhavanti| taddhitai bhāsu aitassa atthīti bhāsurau, pabhassarau yau kauci| bhāsurauti vā kaisarasīhau| imasmiṃ atthai bhāsusaddau ‘‘rāja dittiya’’nti aittha rājasaddau viya virājanavācakau siyā, tasmā rūpasiriyā virājanasampan‍natāya bhāsu virājanatā aitassa atthīti bhāsurauti nibbacanaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

nāsu rāsu saddai| nāsati| rāsati| nāsā, nāsikā|

tatra nāsāti hatthisauṇḍāpi nāsāti vuc‍cati ‘‘sacai maṃ nāganāsūrū, aulaukaiyya pabhāvatī’’tiādīsu viya| manussādīnaṃ nāsikāpi nāsāti vuc‍cati ‘‘yau tai hatthai ca pādai ca, kaṇṇanāsañ‍ca chaidayī’’tiādīsu viya| nāsanti abyattasaddaṃ karaunti aitāyāti nāsā| nāsā aiva nāsikā| yattha nibbacanaṃ na vadāma, tattha taṃ suviñ‍ñaiyyattā appasiddhattā  vā na vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, avuttampi payaugavicakkhaṇaihi upaparikkhitvā yaujaitabbaṃ| atridaṃ vuc‍cati –

nāsā sauṇḍā karau hatthau,

hatthidabbai samā matā|

nāsā ca nāsikā ca dvai,

narādīsu samā matā’’ti||

nasa kauṭil‍lai| nasati|

bhisi bhayai| bhiṃsati| bhiṃsanakau| tadāsi yaṃ bhiṃsanakaṃ| bhaismākāyau|

gasu adanai| gasati|

ghusī kantikaraṇai| īkārantauyaṃ, taina itau na niggahītāgamau| ghusati|

dhaṃsu gatiyaṃ| dhaṃsati| rajau nuddhaṃsati uddhaṃ|

pasa vitthārai| pasati| pasu|

kusa avhānai raudanai ca| kausati, pak‍kausati| pak‍kausakau, pak‍kausitau, pak‍kausanaṃ|

kassa gatiyaṃ| kassati, parikassati| paṭikassati| mūlāya paṭikassaiyya| paṭikassaiyyāti ākaḍḍhaiyya, mūlāpattiyaṃyaiva patiṭṭhāpaiyyāti atthau|

asa  dityādānaisu ca| cakārau gatipaikkhakau| asati|

disa ādānasaṃvaraṇaisu| dissati purisau|

dāsu dānai| dāsati|

rausa bhayai| rausati| rausakau|

bhaisu calanai| bhaisati|

pasa bādhanaphassanaisu| pasati| pāsau, nāgapāsau, hatthapāsau|

lasa kantiyaṃ| lasati, abhilasati, vilasati| lāsau, vilāsau, vilasanaṃ|

casa bhakkhaṇai| casati|

tisa tittiyaṃ| titti tappanaṃ paripuṇṇatā suhitatā| tisati| titti|

vasa nivāsai| vasati, vasiyati, vacchati| vatthu, vatthaṃ, parivāsau, nivāsau, āvāsau, upavāsau, upausathau, vippavāsau, cirappavāsī, cirappavutthau, vasitvā, vattuṃ, vasituṃ ic‍cādīni|

atra upavāsauti an‍naina vaj‍jitau vāsau upavāsau| upausathauti upavasanti aitthāti upausathau, upavasanti sīlaina vā anasanaina vā upaitā hutvā vasantīti atthau| ayaṃ panaittha atthuddhārau – ‘‘āyāmāvusau kappina upausathaṃ gamissāmā’’tiādīsu pātimaukkhuddaisau  upausathau| ‘‘aivaṃ aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau khau visākhai upausathau upavutthau’’tiādīsu sīlaṃ| ‘‘suddhassa vai sadā phaggu, suddhassupausathau sadā’’tiādīsu upavāsau| ‘‘upausathau nāma nāgarājā’’tiādīsu pañ‍ñatti| ‘‘na bhikkhavai tadahupausathai sabhikkhukā āvāsā’’tiādīsu upavasitabbadivasauti|

vasa kantiyaṃ| vacchati| jinavacchayau|

sasa susanai| sasati| sasau|

sasa pāṇanai| sasati| satauva assasati, satauva passasati| sasau, sasanaṃ| assāsau passāsau assasantau passasantau|

asa bhuvi| atthi| asa|

aittha atthīti ākhyātapadaṃ| na atthi khīrā brāhmaṇī| atthitā, atthibhāvau, ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci ratanaṃ atthī’’tiādīsu viya nipātapadaṃ| tasmā atthīti padaṃ ākhyātanipātavasaina duvidhanti vaiditabbaṃ| asaiti avibhattikaṃ nāmikapadaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘asasmīti hautī’’ti pāḷi nidassanaṃ| tattha atthīti asa, nic‍cassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| iminā sassatadiṭṭhi vuttā|

tatrāyaṃ padamālā – ‘‘atthi, santi| asi, attha| asmi, asma, amhi, amha’’ ic‍caitāni pasiddhāni| ‘‘atthu, santu| āhi, attha| asmi, asma amhi, amha’’ ic‍caitāni ca, ‘‘siyā, assa, siyuṃ, assu, siyaṃsu| assa, assatha| siyaṃ, assa, assāma’’ ic‍caitāni ca pasiddhāni|

aittha pana ‘‘taisañ‍ca khau bhikkhavai samaggānaṃ sammaudamānānaṃ…pai॰… siyaṃsu dvai bhikkhū abhidhammai nānāvādā’’ti pāḷi nidassanaṃ| tattha siyaṃsūti bhavaiyyuṃ| abhidhammaiti visiṭṭhai dhammai|

idāni siyāsaddassa atthuddhārau pabhaidau ca vuc‍catai| siyāti aikaṃsai ca vikappanai ca ‘‘pathavīdhātu siyā ajjhattikā, siyā bāhirā’’ti aikaṃsai| ‘‘siyā añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau āpattivītik‍kamau’’ti vikappanai|

siyāti aikamārakhyātapadaṃ, aikamabyayapadaṃ| ākhyātattai aikavacanantaṃ, abyayattai yathāpāvacanaṃ| ‘‘puttā matthi dhanā matthī’’ti aittha atthīti abyayapadamiva aikavacanantampi bahuvacanantampi bhavati| tassākhyātattai payaugauviditauva| abyayattai pana ‘‘sukhaṃ na sukhasahagataṃ, siyā pītisahagata’’nti ‘‘imai dhammā siyā parittārammaṇā’’ti ca aikavacanabahuvacanappayaugā vaiditabbā| aittha dhātuyā kic‍caṃ natthi| paraukkhāyaṃ ‘‘itiha asa itiha asā’’ti dassanatau asa iti padaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| hiyyattanīrūpāni appasiddhāni| aj‍jataniyā pana ‘‘āsi, āsiṃsu, āsuṃ| āsi, āsittha| āsiṃ, āsimhā’’ ic‍caitāni pasiddhāni| bhavissantiyā ‘‘bhavissati, bhavissanti’’ ic‍cādīni| kālātipattiyā ‘‘abhavissā, abhavissaṃsu’’ ic‍cādīni bhavanti|

sāsa anusiṭṭhiyaṃ| sāsati, anusāsati| kammantaṃ vau sāsati, sāsanaṃ, anusāsanaṃ, anusāsanī, anusiṭṭhi, satthā, satthaṃ, anusāsakau, anusāsikā|

tatra sāsananti adhisīlādisikkhattayasaṅgahitasāsanaṃ, pariyattipaṭipattipaṭivaidhasaṅkhātaṃ vā sāsanaṃ| tañhi sāsati aitaina, aittha vāti ‘‘sāsana’’nti pavuc‍cati| apica sāsananti ‘‘rañ‍ñau  sāsanaṃ paisaitī’’tiādīsu viya pāpaitabbavacanaṃ| tathā sāsananti auvādau, yau ‘‘anusāsanī’’ti ca, ‘‘anusiṭṭhī’’ti ca vuc‍cati| satthāti tividhayānamukhaina sadaivakaṃ laukaṃ sāsatīti satthā, diṭṭhadhammikasamparāyikaparamatthaihi yathārahaṃ sattai anusāsatīti atthau| satthanti saddai ca atthai ca sāsati ācikkhati aitaināti satthaṃ| kiṃtaṃ? byākaraṇaṃ|

īsa issariyai| issariyaṃ issarabhāvau| īsati| vaṅgīsau, janapadaisau, manujaisau|

tatra vaṅgīsauti vācāya īsau issarauti vaṅgīsau| kau sau? āyasmā vaṅgīsau arahā| āha ca sayamaiva –

‘‘vaṅgai jātauti vaṅgīsau, vacanai issarauti ca|

‘vaṅgīsau’ iti mai nāmaṃ, abhavī laukasammata’’nti|

āsa upavaisanai| upavaisanaṃ nisīdanaṃ ‘‘āsanai upaviṭṭhau saṅghau’’ti aittha viya| āsati| acchati| āsīnau| āsanaṃ| upāsati| upāsakau|

tattha āsananti āsati nisīdati aitthāti āsanaṃ, yaṃ kiñ‍ci nisīdanayauggaṃ mañ‍capīṭhādi|

kasī gatisausanaisu| īkārantauyaṃ dhātu, tainitau na niggahītāgamau| kasati|

nisī cumbanai| nisati|

disī appītiyaṃ| dhammaṃ daissati| disau| diṭṭhau| daissī| daissau| daissiyau|

tatra disauti ca diṭṭhauti ca pac‍cāmittassādhivacanamaitaṃ| sau hi parai daissati nappiyāyati, paraihi vā daissiyati piyau  na kariyatīti ‘‘disau’’ti ca ‘‘diṭṭhau’’ti ca vuc‍cati| atha vā disauti caurau vāpac‍cāmittau vā| diṭṭhauti pac‍cāmittauyaiva| atrimai payaugā –

‘‘disau disaṃ yaṃ taṃ kayirā, vairī vā pana vairinaṃ|

micchāpaṇihitaṃ cittaṃ, pāpiyau naṃ tatau karai’’ti ca||

‘‘disā hi mai dhammakathaṃ suṇantū’’ti ca, ‘‘disā hi mai tai manussai bhajantu yai dhammamaivādapayanti santau’’ti ca,

‘‘yassaitai caturau dhammā, vānarinda yathā tava|

sac‍caṃ dhammau dhiti cāgau, diṭṭhaṃ sau ativattatī’’ti ca||

daissīti daissanasīlau appiyāyanasīlauti daissī| ‘‘dhammakāmau bhavaṃ hauti, dhammadaissī parābhavau’’ti idamaittha payauganidassanaṃ| daissauti appiyau, tathā daissiyauti| aittha ca –

‘‘na mai daissā ubhau puttā, maddīdaivī na daissiyā|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ, tasmā piyai adāsaha’’nti ca,

‘‘na mai sā brāhmaṇī daissā, napi mai balaṃ na vij‍jatī’’ti ca,

‘‘mātā pitā na mai daissā, napi daissaṃ mahāyasaṃ|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ piyaṃ mayhaṃ, tasmā vatamadhiṭṭhahi’’nti ca

payaugā| sabbattha maiti ca mayhanti ca sāmivacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘pac‍camittau ripu diṭṭhau, disau vairī ca satva’ri|

amittau ca sapattau ca, aivaṃ paṇṇattikārisū’’ti||

tattha mhayataiti sitaṃ karauti| umhayataiti pahaṭṭhākāraṃ dassaiti| vimhayataiti vimhayanaṃ karauti| tatrāyaṃ pāḷi ‘‘na naṃ umhayatai disvā| paikkhitaina mhitaina ca| mhitapubbaṃva  bhāsati| yadā umhayamānā maṃ, rājaputtī udikkhati| umhāpaiyya pabhāvatī| pamhāpaiyya pabhāvatī’’ti|
