athāpi vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu ca bhau yathā ‘āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsū’ti aittha ‘‘āsavatau cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsū’ti ca ‘āsavaihi kattubhūtaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsū’ti ca aivaṃ divādigaṇikassa dhātussa ‘vimuc‍ciṃsū’ti rūpapadassa akammakattañ‍ca sakammakattañ‍ca bhavati, tathā ‘nirayai pac‍cati, kammāni vipac‍cantī’ti ca akammakattainapi bhavitabbaṃ| ‘pac‍cati pākānaṃ pavattai aṭṭhakai duvai, asaṅkhāraṃ sasaṅkhāravipākāni na pac‍catī’ti sakammakattainapi bhavitabba’’nti| akammakattainaiva bhavitabbaṃ, na sakammakattaina, ‘‘pac‍cati pākāna’’ntiādinā vuttappayaugānaṃ ‘‘āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsū’’ti payaugaina asamānattā| tathā haittha ‘‘vimuc‍ciṃsū’’ti padaṃ kammarahitakattuvācakayapac‍cayantampi bhavati kattusahitakammavācakayapac‍cayantampi| ‘‘vimuc‍ciṃsū’’ti imassa hi padassa kammarahitayapac‍cayavantattā ‘‘āsavaihī’’ti karaṇavacanaṃ apādānakārakavācakaṃ bhavati| ‘cittānī’’ti pac‍cattavacanaṃ pana kattukārakavācakaṃ bhavati| tathā ‘‘vimuc‍ciṃsū’’ti padassa kattusahitakammavācakattā ‘‘āsavaihī’’ti  karaṇavacanaṃ kattukārakavācakaṃ bhavati| ‘‘cittānī’’ti pac‍cattavacanaṃ pana kammakārakavācakaṃ bhavati| ayaṃ nayau ‘‘pac‍cati pākāna’’ntiādinā vuttappayaugaisu na labbhati| tathā hi tattha pac‍cattavacanaṃ kattāraṃ vadati, upayaugavacanaṃ kammaṃ vadatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| kāritai ‘‘purisau purisaina purisaṃ vā bhattaṃ pācaiti pācayati pācāpaiti pācāpayatī’’ti ca, ‘‘anantai baudhisambhārai, paripācaisi nāyakau’’ti dassanatau pana ‘‘paripācaiti, paripācayatī’’ti ca rūpāni bhavanti| imāni haitukattupadāni| kammai – yañ‍ñadattaina audanau pac‍catai, bhāvapadaṃ appasiddhaṃ| imāni bhūvādidivādigaṇikarūpāni| iminā nayaina añ‍ñānipi dvigaṇikarūpāni yaujaitabbāni|

taigaṇikattai su pasavai| haituphalaṃ savati, pasavati| su savanai| saddhau dhammaṃ suṇauti, suṇāti| su hiṃsāyaṃ| yaudhau pac‍cāmittaṃ sunāti| imāni yathāk‍kamaṃ bhūvādisvādikiyādigaṇikāni kattupadāni| tathā haitunā phalaṃ saviyyati, un‍nādasaddau pathavīundriyasaddau viya suyyati| yaudhaina pac‍camittau suniyyati| imāni kammapadāni| bhāvapadaṃ na labbhati sakammakattā imaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ| iminā nayaina añ‍ñānipi taigaṇikarūpāni upaparikkhitvā yaujaitabbāni| atra panāyaṃ nayavibhāvanā –

bhvādirudhādikā dhātū, svādidivādikā tathā|

rudhādikadivādiṭṭhā, bhūvādikacurādikā||

bhūvādikagahādiṭṭhā, bhvādisvādikiyādikā|

aivamādippabhaidaihi, vitthāraintu vicakkhaṇā||

ic‍caivaṃ saṅkhaipatau yathārahaṃ aikagaṇikadvigaṇikataigaṇikavasaina suddhakattuhaitukattukammabhāvapadāni ca sakāritaikakammāni  ca sakāritadvikammāni ca sakāritatikammāni ca dassitāni|

idāni aikakāritadvikāritapadānaṃ vacanaukāsau anuppattau, tasmā taṃ vadāma| sau antakammani| arahattamaggau mānaṃ siyati, kammaṃ pariyausiyati| imāni tāva suddhakattupadāni| aittha mānaṃ siyatīti mānaṃ samucchindati| kammaṃ pariyausiyatīti kammaṃ nipphaj‍jati| pari ava ic‍cupasaggavasaina hi idaṃ padaṃ akammakaṃ bhavati, atthau pana ‘‘pariyausānaṃ gacchatī’’ti sakammakavasaina gahaitabbau| attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti| idamaikaṃ kāritaṃ haitukattupadaṃ| aittha pana pariava ic‍cupasaggavasaina akammakabhūtassa saudhātussa laddhakāritapac‍cayattā aikakammamaiva sakāritapadaṃ bhavati| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāvāpaiti| idaṃ dvikāritaṃ haitukattupadaṃ| aittha ca pana pari ava ic‍cupasaggavasaina akammakabhūtassa saudhātussa laddhakāritapac‍cayadvayattā dvikammakaṃ sakāritapadaṃ bhavati| ‘‘pariyausāvāpaitī’’ti idampi pari ava pubbasmā saudhātumhā ṇāpai ṇāpai iti pac‍cayadvayaṃ katvā avasaddassaukārañ‍ca katvā tatau yakārāgamañ‍ca anubandhaṇakāralaupañ‍ca paṭhamapac‍cayai pakārassa vakārañ‍ca dvīsu ca ṭhānaisu pubbasaralaupaṃ katvā nipphaj‍jatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idāni tā pāḷiyau atthantaraviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ āhac‍cadaisitākāraina aikatau kathayāma – ‘‘attanā vippakataṃ attanā pariyausāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| attanā vippakataṃ paraihi pariyausāvāpaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassā’’ti aittha ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti haitukattupadaṃ ānaitabbaṃ| attanā vippakatanti aittha ca attanāti vippakaraṇakriyāya kattukārakavācakaṃ karaṇavacanaṃ| vippakatanti kammakārakavācakaṃ  upayaugavacanaṃ| attanā pariyausāpaitīti aittha pana attanāti abyayapadabhūtaina sayaṃsaddaina samānatthaṃ vibhatyantapatirūpakaṃ abyayapadaṃ, sayaṃsaddasadisaṃ vā tatiyāvibhatyantaṃ abyayapadaṃ| tathā hi ‘‘attanā pariyausāpaitī’’ti vuttavacanassa ‘‘sayaṃ pariyausāpaitī’’ti atthau bhavati ‘‘attanā ca pāṇātipātī’’tiādīsu viya| paraihi pariyausāvāpaitīti aittha pana paraihīti kammakārakavācakaṃ karaṇavacananti gahaitabbaṃ, ‘‘sunakhaihipi khādāpaintī’’ti aittha ‘‘sunakhaihī’’ti padaṃ viya| aittha hi yathā ‘‘rājānau cauraṃ sunakhai khādāpaintī’’ti upayaugavasaina atthau bhavati, tathā ‘‘bhikkhu attanā vippakataṃ parai janai pariyausāvāpaitī’’ti upayaugavasaina atthau bhavati| aivaṃ imasmiṃ acchariyabbhutanayavicittai bhagavatau pāvacanai dvikāritapac‍cayavantampi padamatthīti sāratau pac‍caitabbaṃ| ayaṃ nayau sukhumau sāsanai ādaraṃ katvā āyasmantaihi sādhukaṃ manasi kātabbau| yassa hi atthāya idaṃ pakaraṇaṃ karimha, na ayaṃ attanau mati, atha khau pubbācariyānaṃ santikā laddhattā taisañ‍ñaiva matīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

idāni akāritadvikammikapadānaṃ vacanaukāsau anuppattau, tasmā tāni kathayāma, tāni ca khau dhātuvasaina aivaṃ vaiditabbāni savinicchayāni| saiyyathidaṃ?

duhikaravahipucchi, yāci bhikkhi ca nibrūti|

bhaṇivadivacibhāsi, sāsidahināthadhātu||

rudhi ji cipabhutīti, yai tai dvikammakā dhīrā|

pavadumapi viyuttā, kāritappac‍cayaihi ca||

apādānādikai pubba-vidhimhā sahimai’bravuṃ|

upayaugavacanassa, nimittanti sanantanā||

aitai  duhādayau dhātū, tikammāpi bhavanti tu|

kāritappac‍cayai laddhai, iti ācariyā’bravuṃ||

tatrimāni udāharaṇāni – gavaṃ payau duhati gaupālakau| gāviṃ khīraṃ duhati gaupāladārakau| tattha payauti upayaugavacanaṃ, ‘‘yasau laddhā na maj‍jaiyyā’’ti aittha ‘‘yasau’’ti padamiva| manaugaṇikassa hi īdisampi upayaugavacanaṃ hauti añ‍ñādisampi| issarau gaupālaṃ gavaṃ payau duhāpaiti| gaupālaina gāvau khīraṃ duhitā| gauhi payau duhatīti aittha apādānavisayattā dvikammakabhāvau natthi| ‘‘visāṇatau gavaṃ duhaṃ, yattha khīraṃ na vindatī’’ti aittha pana apādānavisayattaipi gavāvayavabhūtassa visāṇassa visuṃ gahitattā ‘‘gavaṃ khīraṃ duhantau’’ti dvikammikabhāvau labbhatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| duhinau payaugauyaṃ|

karautissa payaugai kaṭṭhamaṅgāraṃ karauti, suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ karauti, sacai jai sac‍caṃ bhaṇasi, adāsiṃ taṃ karaumi| aittha ca aṅgāraṃ karautīti paric‍cattakāraṇavasaina vuttaṃ| kaṭṭhañhi aṅgārabhāvassa kāraṇaṃ, aṅgārai katai kāraṇabhūtassa kaṭṭhassa kaṭṭhabhāvau vigacchati| kaṭakaṃ karautīti idaṃ aparic‍cattakāraṇavasaina vuttaṃ| suvaṇṇañhi kaṭakabhāvassa kāraṇaṃ, kaṭakai kataipi kārakabhūtassa suvaṇṇassa suvaṇṇabhāvau na vigacchati, atha khau visaisantaruppattibhāvaina sampaj‍jati| adāsiṃ taṃ karaumīti idaṃ pana ṭhānantaradānavasaina vuttaṃ ‘‘uparājaṃ mahārājaṃ karaumī’’ti aittha viya| tattha ‘‘issarau purisaina purisaṃ vā kaṭṭhamaṅgāraṃ kāraiti| tathā suvaṇṇaṃ kaṭakaṃ kāraitī’’ti tikammikappayaugaupi daṭṭhabbau| tathā ‘‘brahmadattau raj‍jaṃ kāraitī’’ti, ‘‘brahmadattai raj‍jaṃ kāraintai’’ti dvikammakappayaugau|

aitthaikai  vadaiyyuṃ ‘‘nanu ca bhau aittha aikamaiva kammaṃ dissati, kaināyaṃ payaugau dvikammikappayaugau hautī’’ti| kiñ‍cāpi aikamaiva dissati, tathāpi atthatau dvaiyaiva kammāni dissantīti gahaitabbaṃ| tathā hi brahmadattau raj‍jaṃ kāraitīti aittha brahmadattau attanau rājabhāvaṃ mahājanaina kārayatīti atthau| aivaṃ pana atthai gahitai ‘‘raj‍jaṃ kāraihi bhaddantai, kiṃ arañ‍ñai karissasī’’tiādīsupi tvaṃ attanau rājabhāvaṃ amhaihi kārāpaihi, attānaṃ raj‍jai abhisiñ‍cāpaihi, mayaṃ taṃ raj‍jai abhisiñ‍citukāmāti atthau samatthitau bhavati|

brahmadattai raj‍jaṃ kāraintaiti aitthāpi brahmadattai attanau rājabhāvaṃ mahājanaina kārayantaiti atthau bhavati| sāsanasmiñhi kāritavisayai karaṇavacanaṃ upayaugatthañ‍ñaiva dīpaiti, tasmā atthatau dvaiyaiva kammani dissantīti vadāma| ayamatthau abhidhammaṭīkāyaṃ cakkhundriyādinibbacanatthavibhāvanāya dīpaitabbau| tathā hi abhidhammaṭīkāyaṃ idaṃ vuttaṃ ‘‘cakkhudvārai indattaṃ kāraitīti cakkhudvārabhāvai taṃdvārikaihi attanau indabhāvaṃ paramissarabhāvaṃ kārayatīti atthau| tañhi tai rūpaggahaṇai attānaṃ anuvattaiti, tai ca taṃ anuvattantī’’ti| yadi pana karadhātu dvikammakau, aivaṃ santai ‘‘brahmadattau raj‍jaṃ kāraitī’’tiādīsu laddhakāritapac‍cayattā ‘‘kāraitī’’tiādīhi padaihi tikammakaihiyaiva bhavitabbanti? na, niyamābhāvatau, tādisassa ca payaugassa vauhārapathai anāgatattā|

kaṭṭhaṃ purisaina aṅgāraṃ kataṃ| suvaṇṇaṃ kammāraina kaṭakaṃ kataṃ, dāsī sāmikaina adāsiṃ katā, aivampaittha dvikammakappayaugā vaiditabbā| suvaṇṇaina kaṭakaṃ karautīti aittha  hi visaisanatthai pavattakaraṇavisayattā dvikammikabhāvau na labbhatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| ayaṃ nayau añ‍ñatrāpi upaparikkhitvā yathāsambhavaṃ naitabbau| karautissa payaugauyaṃ|

vahiādīnaṃ payaugai rājapurisā rathaṃ gāmaṃ vahanti| ayaṃ rājā maṃ nāmaṃ pucchati| parābhavantaṃ purisaṃ, mayaṃ pucchāma gautamaṃ| āyasmā upāli āyasmatā mahākassapaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau| daivadattau rājānaṃ kambalaṃ yācati| tai maṃ assai ayācisuṃ| dhanaṃ taṃ tāta yācati| brāhmaṇau nāgaṃ maṇiṃ yācati| nāgau maṇiṃ yācitau brāhmaṇaina brahmunā āyācitau dhammadaisanaṃ bhagavā| tāpasau kulaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhikkhati| ajaṃ gāmaṃ naiti| ajau gāmaṃ nītau| muttau campaiyyakau nāgau, rājānaṃ aitadabravi|

aittha rājānanti mukhyatau kammaṃ vuttaṃ| aitanti guṇatau| tathā rājānanti akathitakammaṃ vuttaṃ| aitanti kathitakammaṃ| aisa nayau añ‍ñatrāpi upaparikkhitvā yathārahaṃ yaujaitabbau| aivamaiva ‘‘brūhi bhagavā’’tiādīsu sampadānavisayattā dvikammakabhāvau na labbhati| bhikkhu mahārājānaṃ dhammaṃ bhaṇati| yaṃ maṃ bhaṇasi sārathi| yaṃ maṃ vadati| bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca| pitā puttaṃ bhāsati| yaṃ maṃ tvaṃ anusāsasi| sakyā khau pana ambaṭṭha rājānaṃ auk‍kākaṃ pitāmahaṃ dahanti| bhagavā bhikkhū taṃ taṃ hitapaṭipattiṃ nāthati| gāvau vajaṃ rundhati gaupālakau| dhuttau dhuttajanaṃ dhanaṃ jināti| aittha ca ‘‘kamanuttaraṃ ratnavaraṃ jināmā’’ti puṇṇakajātakapāḷi nidassanaṃ| tatthāyamatthau ‘‘mayaṃ janindā kataraṃ rājānaṃ anuttaraṃ ratnavaraṃ jināmā’’ti| iṭṭhakāyau pākāraṃ cinauti vaḍḍhakī| añ‍ñānipi yaujaitabbāni|

aittha  kaici pucchaiyyuṃ ‘‘gandhakuṭiṃ padakkhiṇaṃ karauti, buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmi| upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitū’ti payaugaisu kiṃ dvikammakabhāvau labbhatī’’ti? aittha vuc‍catai – ‘‘gandhakuṭiṃ padakkhiṇaṃ karautī’’ti aittha na labbhati guṇaguṇīnaṃ vasaina gahitattā| ‘‘buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gacchāmī’’ti aitthāpi na labbhati ‘‘saraṇaṃ iti gacchāmī’’ti itisaddalaupavasaina vuttattā| tathā hi buddhanti upayaugavacanaṃ| saraṇanti pac‍cattavacanaṃ| ‘‘buddhaṃ mama saraṇaṃ parāyaṇaṃ, aghassa tātā hitassa ca vidhātā’’ti iminā adhippāyaina ‘‘bhajāmi saivāmi bujjhāmī’’ti atthau| ‘‘upāsakaṃ maṃ bhavaṃ gautamau dhāraitū’’ti aittha pana dvikammakabhāvau labbhatīti vattabbau ‘‘maṃ itau paṭṭhāya upāsakaṃ dhāraitū’’ti atthasambhavatau ‘‘sakyā khau pana ambaṭṭha rājānaṃ auk‍kākaṃ pitāmahaṃ dahantī’’ti dahadhātuppayaugaina samānattā ca, adhippāyatthatau pana ‘‘maṃ ‘upāsakau mai aya’nti dhāraitū’’ti atthau sambhavatīti daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ akāritāni dvikammikadhāturūpāni vibhāvitāni|

ic‍caivamamhaihi āditau paṭṭhāya bhagavatau sāsanatthaṃ yathāsatti yathābalaṃ dhātuyau ca taṃrūpāni ca tadanurūpaihi nānāpadaihi nānāatthaihi nānānayaihi ca yaujaitvā vibhāvitāni, aivaṃ vibhāvaintaihipi amhaihi tāsaṃ sarūpaparicchaidau atthaparicchaidau vā na sak‍kā sabbasau vattuṃ| tadubhayañhi kau sabbasau vattuṃ sakkhissati añ‍ñatra āgamādhigamasampan‍naihi pabhin‍napaṭisambhidaihi mahākhīṇāsavaihi|

atthātisayayuttāpi, dhātū haunti yatau tatau| payaugataunugantabbā, anaikatthā hi dhātavau|

yainaikatthadharā  caranti vividhā nāthassa pāṭhai varai,

tainaikatthadharāva haunti sahitā nānūpasaggaihivai|

dhātūnaṃ pana taisamatthaparamaṃ khīṇāsavai paṇḍitai,

vaj‍jaitvā paṭisambhidāmatiyutai kau sabbasau bhaṇatīti||

iti navaṅgai sāṭṭhakathai piṭakattayai byappathagatīsu viñ‍ñūnaṃ

kausal‍latthāya katai saddanītippakaraṇai

sabbagaṇavinicchayau nāma aikūnavīsatimau paricchaidau||

saha rūpavibhāvanāya dhātuvibhāvanā niṭṭhitā|

dhātumālā niṭṭhitā|
